### `psalm_139_part_2`

- **EN** I will praise thee; for I am fearfully and wonderfully made:
  **CN** 我要称谢祢，因我受造，奇妙可畏；
- **EN** marvellous are thy works; and that my soul knoweth right well.
  **CN** 祢的作为何等奇妙，我内心深知这一点。
- **EN** My substance was not hid from thee, when I was made in secret,
  **CN** 我在隐秘之处受造时，我的形体并不向祢隐藏，
- **EN** and curiously wrought in the lowest parts of the earth.
  **CN** 我在地底深处，被精巧地编织成形。
- **EN** Thine eyes did see my substance, yet being unperfect;
  **CN** 我尚未成形，祢的眼便已看见；
- **EN** and in thy book all my members were written,
  **CN** 我那后来逐渐成形的肢体，
- **EN** which in continuance were fashioned, when as yet there was none of them.
  **CN** 在尚无一处成形时，就都已记在祢的册上。
- **EN** How precious also are thy thoughts unto me, O God! how great is the sum of them!
  **CN** 神灵啊，祢的意念于我何等宝贵！其数何等众多！
  *导演注释：Todo: The current recording says "were none of them" instead of "was none of them".*
- **EN** If I should count them, they are more in number than the sand:
  **CN** 我若数，则比海沙更多；
- **EN** when I awake, I am still with thee.
  **CN** 我睡醒的时候，仍和祢同在。


<!-- ================= 04_sokoban_leamington ================= -->

# Leamington（勘测员 / 后来的 Sokoban）

> 玩家的前任。audiolog_g_* 录音日志（致 Quigley）与全部主线对话的主人翁；通关后获仓房管辖权，职级 Sokoban、头衔 Magistrate。证据：intro_1 "Mr. Quigley,"；crisis 日志第三人称自指 "Leamington was rash, you'd say"。

**统计**：台词块 446 · 英文词数 4128 · 涉及段落 51 · 官方中译覆盖 321/446

---


### `intro_1`

- **EN** Mr. Quigley,
  **CN** 奎格利先生，
- **EN** Moments after I came through, the doorstead abruptly closed and disappeared. I am on my own for the time being.
  **CN** 我过来没多久，界门就突然关闭、消失了。 暂时只能靠我自己了。
- **EN** I trust you'll reopen it when you can manage. Meanwhile, I shall look about.
  **CN** 我相信等你有能力时，就会重新打开它。 在那之前，我先四处看看。
- **EN** I'll leave a trail of beacons, should you send someone after,
  **CN** 我会沿途留下信标，以防你派人来找，
- **EN** and set down what information I can, in case of my disappearance or expiry.
  **CN** 我也会尽量把所见所知记录下来，以防自己失踪或丧命。
- **EN** I'm standing in ... an anteroom. The furnishings are very strange, unlike the handiwork of any people known to me, historic or modern.
  **CN** 我正站在……一间前厅里。 这里的陈设十分古怪，不像任何我所熟知的古今文明的作品。
- **EN** It does appear to be a completely other world.
  **CN** 这里看起来，确实是一个截然不同的世界。
- **EN** These chambers were made for a large gathering, but they're empty.
  **CN** 这些厅房是为大型聚会建造的，现在却空无一人。
  *导演注释：The other possibility is, maybe they opened a portal to somewhere far away in their own world. He is discounting that.*
- **EN** The surfaces look newly made; no trace of wear, and no dust.
  **CN** 表面看起来像是刚建成的；没有磨损的痕迹，也没有灰尘。
  *导演注释：shifting gears, looking around*
- **EN** Someone tends this place, so I should see him before long.
  **CN** 有人打理着这个地方，所以用不了多久我就能见到他。
  *导演注释：Making the observations, and connecting the implication of active use, as he is speaking. "should not remain alone" does not necessarily mean that whoever's here is friendly.*
- **EN** I'll press forward.
  **CN** 我继续前进。

### `intro_before_teleporter`

- **EN** Quigley, It's very strange.
  **CN** 奎格利，太奇怪了。
- **EN** There are plaques inscribed in an unknown language, glyphs I've never seen before, but I just ... know what they mean.
  **CN** 牌匾上刻着一种未知语言，那些字形我从未见过， 可我就是……知道它们的意思。
- **EN** This ... facility ... was constructed to receive strangers,
  **CN** 这座……设施……是为了接待外来者而建造的，
- **EN** which explains the pronounced peak in the sounding-outs: they are advertising this place.
  **CN** 这也解释了探测时为什么会出现那么明显的峰值： 他们是在向外界宣告这里的存在。
- **EN** There's something ahead ... I don't know where it leads.
  **CN** 前面还有东西……不知道它通往哪里。
  *导演注释："sounding-outs" are like an interdimensional sonar that they used to find this destination.*
- **EN** But of course I'm going through. Ever forward.
  **CN** 不过我当然会进去。永远向前。

### `sokoban_overworld`

- **EN** I've explored a good deal of the surrounding territory.
  **CN** 我已经探索了周边相当大一片区域。
- **EN** There is more here than I presently understand; you'll have to see for yourself.
  **CN** 这里还有许多我暂时无法理解的东西；你得亲眼看看才会明白。
- **EN** From here, North is the clearest way to make progress. Avoid the West until you are ready for sterner work.
  **CN** 从这里出发，北边是最容易前进的方向。 在你准备好迎接更严峻的挑战之前，先别去西边。
- **EN** Quigley, we have indeed done it. Rest assured that I count my life's work a success, regardless of what may follow.
  **CN** 奎格利，我们确实做到了。请放心，无论接下来发生什么， 我都认为自己毕生的事业已经成功了。

### `audiolog_g_possession`

- **EN** Repetition has accustomed me somewhat, but it's still very strange.
  **CN** 这种事经历多了，我多少已经习惯，但还是觉得很古怪。
- **EN** I feel as though I'm making the decisions ... but I'm sure they believe the decisions are theirs.
  **CN** 我觉得是自己在做决定…… 可他们恐怕也觉得，真正做决定的是他们。
  *导演注释：He is referring to the way in which you take control of other characters, in other worlds, to solve puzzles.*
- **EN** We can't both be right.
  **CN** 我们不可能都对。

### `audiolog_g_gold_rooms`

- **EN** Extravagant. But it has me thinking: much in this place is not what it appears.
  **CN** 真够奢华的。不过这也让我想到： 这里的很多东西并非表面看起来的那样。
- **EN** For whoever could construct so vast a place as this --
  **CN** 毕竟，能建造出如此广阔的场所，
- **EN** who could reproduce these buildings, objects, natural environs in such number and variety --
  **CN** 能以如此惊人的规模和多样性再现这些建筑、物件与自然环境的人——
- **EN** surely conjuring a little gold would be of no great difficulty.
  **CN** 要变出一点黄金，想必易如反掌。
- **EN** Gold, then, could scarcely be precious, yet here it's displayed as though it were.
  **CN** 照理说，黄金根本算不上珍贵，这里却把它当成珍宝陈列。
- **EN** Still, I am impressed, and maybe that's it: to dazzle the uninitiated.
  **CN** 可我依然觉得震撼。也许这正是目的：让不明就里的人大开眼界。
- **EN** I've caught glimpses of several such platforms. Perhaps there is some order to their distribution.
  **CN** 我曾见过好几座这样的平台。它们的分布或许有某种规律。
- **EN** But this is the first I have managed to stand upon, and I have yet to engage with its central star.
  **CN** 不过这是我第一次真正站上这种平台，还没有接触它中央的星形标志。
  *导演注释：(short pause)*
- **EN** So I've no idea yet of the function of these golden platforms, but it's time I found out.
  **CN** 所以我还不知道这些黄金平台有什么用，但现在该去寻找答案了。

### `audiolog_g_how_many_gold_rooms`

- **EN** This is the third gilded deck that I have found; I am starting to see the pattern,
  **CN** 这是我找到的第三处镀金平台。我开始看出规律了，
- **EN** and I have some notion of the shape of the whole.
  **CN** 而我对整体的模样也有了几分概念。
- **EN** It's striking -- Quigley, I see the layout as mathematical,
  **CN** 真令人惊叹……奎格利，我是从数学角度理解这个布局，
- **EN** but you might choose to see it as ceremonial,
  **CN** 但你也可以把它当成一种仪式：
- **EN** each thing in its accorded place.
  **CN** 万物各安其位。

### `audiolog_g_world_selection_1`

- **EN** This place brings together elements from different worlds.
  **CN** 这个地方汇聚了不同世界的元素。
- **EN** Those who built it would not build thousands more, I should think,
  **CN** 我想，建造者想必就不会再建数千座这样的设施。
- **EN** if they employed the same worlds each time. They'd merely be reproducing the same result.
  **CN** 要是每次都用同样的世界，那不过是重复同样的结果罢了。
- **EN** This implies that there are many more worlds from which to draw than the four enclosed here.
  **CN** 这说明，可供选用的世界远不止这里所包含的四个。
- **EN** Quigley, I expect we could find another facility, perhaps many, now that we know what to look for.
  **CN** 奎格利，既然知道了该找什么， 我想我们还能找到其他这样的设施，也许还不止一处。
- **EN** I wonder what worlds they hold -- what strange behaviors we might see, as with the water --
  **CN** 不知道其中装着怎样的世界，又会出现哪些像水那样的奇异现象……
  *导演注释：just realizing*
- **EN** I haven't told you this yet. There is so much to set down.
  **CN** 这件事我还没告诉你。要记下的东西实在太多了。
- **EN** Two of the worlds have water so dense it will drown you at the mere depth of a man;
  **CN** 其中有两个世界，水的密度高得惊人，才一人深就足以把人淹死；
- **EN** In the others, water repels itself when agglomerated beyond a certain size, so you just get a profusion of large drops;
  **CN** 而在另外几个世界里，水一旦聚集到一定大小， 就会互相排斥，最终形成大量的巨型水滴；
- **EN** in those worlds there's no such thing as an expanse of water.
  **CN** 那些世界里根本不存在大片水域。
- **EN** When we broaden our explorations, what else might we see?
  **CN** 如果扩大探索范围，我们还会看到什么？

### `audiolog_g_world_selection_2`

- **EN** Before they can employ a world to their mysterious ends, they must locate it somehow, and connect.
  **CN** 那些建造者若想利用某个世界实现不为人知的目的， 首先得想办法找到它，并与之建立连接。
- **EN** By what criteria do they locate? "I want a place wherein machinery casts beams of light, powered by giant gems --"
  **CN** 他们按什么标准来寻找？难道只要想着： “我要一个机器能借巨型宝石之力射出光束的地方……”
- **EN** How would that even work? Or are the criteria specified in some other way that helps this make sense?
  **CN** 可这要怎么实现？还是说，筛选标准是以其他方式设定的， 好让这一切说得通？
- **EN** Quigley, unless you've made considerable discoveries in my absence,
  **CN** 奎格利，除非我不在时你有了什么重大发现，
- **EN** by our way of doorsteading, it would be a random search --
  **CN** 否则按我们的界门探索方式，就只能靠碰运气瞎找……
- **EN** we'd have to test our guesses exhaustively, exposing the traveler to unknown hazards each time.
  **CN** 我们只能逐一验证所有猜测， 每次验证还会让旅行者置身于未知的危险之中。
- **EN** Surely the tossing of the dice would bring misfortune sooner than later.
  **CN** 这样碰运气，迟早会有不幸。
- **EN** So their techniques must be of a different nature -- and vastly more effective.
  **CN** 所以，他们的方法必然在本质上有所不同，且有效得多。
- **EN** If we could learn even a small bit about this, our progress would accelerate tremendously.
  **CN** 哪怕只弄明白一点，我们的研究也能突飞猛进。

### `audiolog_g_world_selection_3`

- **EN** A curious thought has come to me, and I wonder if you would see it the same way.
  **CN** 我有一个有趣的想法，不知你是否也会有同感。
- **EN** These worlds are chosen according to the kinds of events that happen inside them. Keep that in mind for a moment.
  **CN** 这些世界是根据其中发生的各类事件筛选出来的。先记住这一点。
- **EN** Our doorstead, by which we sent me here, is of course very special and unique,
  **CN** 我们的那扇界门——也就是把我送到这里的那扇——当然独一无二，
- **EN** but we based it on the common technology already in use for a hundred years.
  **CN** 但我们是基于已经沿用上百年的常见技术来建造的。
- **EN** My observation is: none of the worlds into which I've ventured have even these mundane doorsteads.
  **CN** 据我观察，我去过的世界里，连这种普通的界门都没有。
- **EN** Three of them have various kinds of remote transport abilities,
  **CN** 其中三个世界具备各式各样的远程传送能力，
- **EN** but they permit only the translocation of single, discrete objects.
  **CN** 但它们一次只能转移一个独立物体。
- **EN** None of them connect space itself, or provide the convenience and capacity of a doorstead.
  **CN** 它们都无法直接连通空间，便利程度和运载能力也比不上界门。
- **EN** To a visitor from one of these other places, the behavior of *our* world would seem peculiar,
  **CN** 在来自那些世界的访客眼里，我们世界的规律也会显得独特，
- **EN** the way we would consider those elephantine gems peculiar.
  **CN** 正像我们也会觉得那些巨型宝石很古怪。
- **EN** Earlier I asked how these Builders find their component worlds. I put it to you, Quigley: is it possible that my arrival here
  **CN** 我先前问过，这些建造者是怎么找到各个组件世界的。 我倒要问你，奎格利：有没有可能，
- **EN** was encouraged, as a means of their finding *us*? Will one of these stars find me back in our own world, faced with
  **CN** 其实是他们有意引导我来到这里，好借此找到我们？ 会不会是某个星形入口把我带回自己的世界，
- **EN** some carefully constructed enigma about maneuvering a doorstead through a doorstead?
  **CN** 然后迎面看到一道精心设计的谜题， 要求我让一扇界门穿过另一扇界门？

### `audiolog_g_musing_on_fast_travel`

- **EN** Quigley, this way of getting around is ... charming. I don't mind it.
  **CN** 奎格利，这种移动方式……还挺迷人的。我并不讨厌。
- **EN** Of course I have always enjoyed the binary partitioning
  **CN** 当然，我一直很喜欢那种层层二分的区划方式，
- **EN** we use for National Transport (confusing though it may be to newcomers);
  **CN** 全国交通系统采用的正是这一套（尽管新人往往很难弄懂）；
- **EN** but the weakness of our scheme is that it manifests as a sequence of skips, which create a linear path, like a road.
  **CN** 但我们这套方案的缺点在于： 它是一连串的跳跃，连起来只是一条线，就像一条路。
- **EN** Of course, we make a web of roads. But the underlying limitation into one dimension is still there.
  **CN** 当然，我们也会把道路织成一张网。 但局限于单一维度的限制依然存在。
- **EN** Here, travel is free across two dimensions.
  **CN** 而在这里，可以在两个维度上自由移动。
- **EN** So. How do these transportation platforms work? Could we build these in our world too,
  **CN** 那么，这些传送平台是如何运作的？ 我们也能在自己的世界里造出这种东西吗，
- **EN** or do they function due to some enabling property in the basic nature of this place, absent in ours?
  **CN** 还是说，这里的空间本身具有某种特性， 才能实现这种传送，而我们的世界没有？
- **EN** Speaking of which...
  **CN** 说到这个……

### `audiolog_g_substrate`

- **EN** Speaking of which, another question is naturally raised: Where does this place exist, wherein I now stand?
  **CN** 说到这个，自然又引出了一个问题： 我脚下这片地方究竟存在于何处？
- **EN** I don't mean just this miniaturized version of the facility, but, really, the whole place.
  **CN** 我不是只指这座设施的缩小版，而是指这整片地方本身。
- **EN** Let's say we have a world A that has its particular laws of Nature, and another world B, which has its own, different, laws of Nature.
  **CN** 假设有个世界A，有它自己的一套自然法则； 再有个世界B，有另一套不同的法则。
- **EN** To create a place where people and objects from both A and B can interact,
  **CN** 要造出一个地方，让来自A和B的人与物都能在其中打交道，
- **EN** well, it's clear the laws of Nature of A and B cannot be in direct conflict each withother.
  **CN** 嗯，显然A和B的自然法则不能彼此直接冲突。
- **EN** But the space itself that contains this fusion -- what were its laws of Nature prior to the
  **CN** 但承载这场融合的空间本身，在A和B来到这里之前，
- **EN** introduction of A or B, when it was quiet and empty awaiting some contents to arrive?
  **CN** 空无一物、静候填充之时，又遵循着怎样的自然法则？
- **EN** The laws of Nature of such a place must have been compatible with both, perhaps a subset of A and B,
  **CN** 这种地方的自然法则必定与两者都相容， 也许是A与B共有的一个子集，
- **EN** that can yet be built outward to meet A or B where necessary. Or else a superset of A and B,
  **CN** 但又能在必要时扩充，以与A或B的法则相适应。 又或者，它原本是A与B的超集，
- **EN** that is then trimmed back?
  **CN** 随后再删减其中的法则？
- **EN** How do you make such a substrate? Is there one substrate for all worlds,
  **CN** 要怎样才能造出这种基底？是所有的世界共用一个基底，
- **EN** or are there different substrates that manifest as needed?
  **CN** 还是有不同的基底，按需显现？
  *导演注释：beat*
- **EN** I shall try to apprehend more.
  **CN** 我会试着去了解更多。

### `audiolog_g_work_and_care`

- **EN** It obviously requires a great deal of work and care to arrange all this.
  **CN** 显然，要把这一切安排妥当，需要投入大量心血。
- **EN** To what end?
  **CN** 到底图什么？
- **EN** I face the challenges they've put before me,
  **CN** 他们在我面前摆下一道道难关，
- **EN** and I don't do too badly, if I may say.
  **CN** 而且说句不谦虚的话，我处理得还算不错。
- **EN** Is this a test? Do I earn distinction by passing?
  **CN** 这是一场考验吗？通过之后，我能得到奖赏吗？
- **EN** Is it a kind of training, to make me more capable of solving problems?
  **CN** 这是为了训练我，让我更善于解决问题吗？
- **EN** Is it merely a show of grand workmanship, like the monuments of forgotten civilizations?
  **CN** 这难道只是在炫耀高超的建造技艺， 就像那些失落文明留下的纪念碑一样？
- **EN** They left no direction as to my purpose here. Why not?
  **CN** 他们丝毫没有说明我为何来到这里。为什么？
- **EN** And lastly -- perhaps most importantly: Why is it unpopulated?
  **CN** 最后一个问题——也许也是最重要的： 为什么这里空无一人？
- **EN** I'd have expected to see someone... ... a real person *here*, not in the worlds... well before now.
  **CN** 我原以为早该遇见什么人了…… 一个真正属于这里的人，不是其他世界的居民……
- **EN** Why build all this, then abandon it?
  **CN** 既然要抛弃，又为什么建造这一切？
- **EN** I'll continue. Ever forward. What other course is given me?
  **CN** 我会继续走下去。永远向前。 除此之外，我还能有别的路可走吗？
- **EN** New facts may make sense of this yet.
  **CN** 新的线索或许终能让这一切说得通。

### `audiolog_g_no_eating`

- **EN** I'm down to five beacons, not counting this one.
  **CN** 除去这一枚，我只剩五枚信标了。
- **EN** As I have ventured farther from the center in a few directions now,
  **CN** 如今，我已经从中心朝好几个方向走出了很远，
- **EN** I've seen that the stars -- the entries to other worlds -- they decrease in density.
  **CN** 我发现，那些星形标志—— 也就是通往其他世界的入口——分布得越来越稀疏。
- **EN** I still don't know the full extent of their spread, but the thinning-out gives me hope of a reasonable, finite size.
  **CN** 我仍不清楚它们分布得多广，但既然越来越稀疏， 我便盼着这里终有尽头，不会太过辽阔。
- **EN** Quigley, you're still not here. I hope all is well on your end.
  **CN** 奎格利，你还是没来。希望你那边一切都好。
- **EN** I should expect to be in some alarm by now, for want of food and fresh water.
  **CN** 照理说，缺少食物和淡水这么久，我早就该慌了。
  *导演注释：shifting from external observations to personal matters*
- **EN** But here I don't seem to need nourishment. You know I love a good brisket,
  **CN** 但在这里，我似乎不需要进食。你知道我最爱吃烤牛胸肉，
- **EN** but here the physical hunger just never arises. That's one more item to investigate.
  **CN** 可在这里，身体根本不会感到饥饿。又多了一件需要调查的事。
- **EN** Parsons always complains of the nuisance of eating.
  **CN** 帕森斯总抱怨吃饭麻烦。
- **EN** Send him through if you get a chance; he'll love it here.
  **CN** 有机会就把他送过来吧，他肯定会爱上这里。
  *导演注释：beat*

### `audiolog_g_musing_on_freezers_1`

- **EN** By now I've been through many of these locked gates and cage-like fixtures...
  **CN** 一路走来，我已经通过了许多类似这样的上锁闸门和笼状装置……
- **EN** and I see that these fixtures are not here to block me full stop.
  **CN** 而且我也看出来了，这些装置并不是要永远阻挡我。
- **EN** They are here to block me *until*.
  **CN** 这些阻碍会一直拦着我，直到……
- **EN** Until I know what they want me to know, and am thereby prepared for the next place, to confront the next problem.
  **CN** 我领悟这些关卡想让我明白的道理， 做好前往下一处、面对下一个难题的准备为止。
- **EN** Thus they shape my journey.
  **CN** 就这样，它们塑造了我的旅程。
- **EN** Just as this whole territory is organized with each star occupying its proper place,
  **CN** 正如整片区域井然有序，每个星形入口都各居其位，
- **EN** these silvery barriers are arranged quite intentionally, each invisibly joined to its star, each star holding an idea.
  **CN** 显然这些银色屏障经过精心布置， 每一道都与相应的星形入口无形相连， 而每个入口都承载着一个概念。
- **EN** If I were to trace the constellations on a map, the connections from star to star, and star to cage,
  **CN** 假如我在地图上描出这些“星座”， 连起各个星形入口，以及入口与笼子之间的连线，
- **EN** they would make a delicate webwork.
  **CN** 它们会织成一张精巧的网。
- **EN** An underlying geometry, compressed and occulted,
  **CN** 一套经过压缩、隐而不见的底层几何结构，
- **EN** a chart of relations in that realm of Ideas that we humans can only dream of touching directly.
  **CN** 一幅描绘理念世界中各种关系的图谱； 而我们人类，也只能梦想直接触及那个世界。

### `audiolog_g_far_from_home`

- **EN** Quigley, It's been a long time.
  **CN** 奎格利，已经过去很久了。
- **EN** I am not sure how long; I've no cycle of the sun by which to count,
  **CN** 具体有多久，我也说不准；这里没有规律的日升日落供我计时，
- **EN** and I've been gradually unburdening myself of devices.
  **CN** 一路走来，我也在逐渐丢下身上的各种装置，减轻负担。
- **EN** I confess that home is beginning to feel distant, more like a half-remembered dream than a real place I might hope ever to inhabit once again.
  **CN** 老实说，家乡于我已渐渐变得遥远，更像一场记忆模糊的梦， 而不是一个我仍盼着有朝一日能回去生活的真实地方。
- **EN** Unceasingly alone, every day I feel closer to these worlds and their inhabitants. Even though I cannot speak to them.
  **CN** 我始终孤身一人， 反倒觉得自己与这些世界及其中的居民一天比一天亲近， 尽管我无法与他们交谈。
- **EN** And also, between the worlds... I'm starting to see, faintly,
  **CN** 还有各个世界之间……我开始隐约看到一点东西，
- **EN** certain kinds of connections. Silken strands.
  **CN** 某种连接。丝一般的细线。
- **EN** Perhaps... Perhaps I don't *want* to return, except to see for a moment how you're all getting on.
  **CN** 也许……也许我根本不想回去。 最多只是短暂回去，看看你们大家过得怎么样。
- **EN** Then I would be on my way again.
  **CN** 然后我就会再次启程。
  *导演注释：Having a sudden change of mind; but there is some denial here... "I don't want to come back, except, yeah I guess I would like to see people, but no I don't want to come back."*
- **EN** I must keep driving forward. Ever onward. Ever forward.
  **CN** 我必须继续向前。永远前进，永远向前。

### `audiolog_g_being_watched`

- **EN** Quigley, I'll continue to address you when I leave these recordings,
  **CN** 奎格利，往后我每次留下录音时，还是会把你当做我的听众，
- **EN** but I confess that it now feels a bit false.
  **CN** 但我得承认，如今再把这些话说给你听，总有些自欺欺人。
- **EN** I've realized that I'm not merely speaking to *you* any more.
  **CN** 我发现，自己已经不只是在对你一个人说话了。
- **EN** As I navigate these trials, I feel I'm being judged by a watchful eye whose nature I know not.
  **CN** 闯过这些试炼时，我总觉得有什么在暗中审视我， 却不知道那究竟是什么。
- **EN** I now realize this feeling has been pervasive from the beginning. Low and subtle.
  **CN** 我现在才意识到，这种感觉从一开始就无处不在。 隐隐约约，十分微妙。
  *导演注释：As he says the following stuff, he is not just recounting to Quigley that he is being watched, but telling the watcher that he knows he's being watched, and trying to say something like, "if I know you're there, maybe the game is up? Maybe you realize I am smart and we can move on to the next phase?"*
- **EN** I find myself courting that Watcher's approval; I wish to be judged clever and perceptive,
  **CN** 我发现自己开始寻求那位“观察者”的认可， 希望对方觉得我聪明又敏锐，
- **EN** and this shows itself in the topics I choose to discuss, and the precise words I employ.
  **CN** 这种心态会流露在我选择的话题和所用的字句里。
  *导演注释：shifting directions*
- **EN** Yet I see no evidence of a Watcher physically present. I wander alone.
  **CN** 然而我看不到任何“观察者”实际在场的迹象。我只是独自徘徊。
- **EN** Is He some spirit peering in from Beyond,
  **CN** 他会不会是某个灵体，在彼岸窥视着我，
- **EN** the way I observe the denizens of these... challenge worlds... from this higher place?
  **CN** 就像我从这处更高的地方观察这些……挑战世界……里的居民一样？
- **EN** Regardless. Prickles at the back of my neck, I feel that watchful eye.
  **CN** 不管怎样，我的后颈忽然一阵发麻：我又感觉到了那道注视的目光。

### `audiolog_g_crisis`

- **EN** Quigley,
  **CN** 奎格利，
- **EN** I am starting to think --
  **CN** 我开始觉得——
- **EN** I was assuming you were doing your best to follow me with a rescue team.
  **CN** 我原以为你会尽全力带着救援队赶来营救我。
- **EN** We made *careful* provision for this contingency and many others. Don't you remember?
  **CN** 这种情况，还有其他许多意外，我们都做了周全准备。 你不记得了吗？
  *导演注释：(Discontinuous from the previous, cutting short that thought and rephrasing)*
- **EN** But now I wonder. It has been a long time. With me gone, you are the head of the group,
  **CN** 可如今我又不确定了。我已经离开太久，现在你接手了我们团队，
- **EN** the elite laboratory built around a world-altering invention.
  **CN** 也就是那个围绕一项改变世界的发明而建造的精英实验室。
- **EN** Leamington was rash, you'd say. He barreled through before we could properly ensure his safety.
  **CN** 你会说利明顿太鲁莽，还没等我们妥善确认他的安全， 他就一头冲了进去。
- **EN** He is gone but will live nobly in our memories, while the fame and fortune go to us....
  **CN** 他虽已离去，却会带着荣光活在我们的记忆中， 名利则归我们所有……
  *导演注释：Perhaps we keep this contraction to show him thinking in a less-ordered fashion. But we could also uncontract it.*
- **EN** Perhaps there was no accident at all. Perhaps you shut the door with cold hands.
  **CN** 也许根本没有什么意外。也许是你冷血无情，亲手关上了门。
  *导演注释：With "fame and fortune go to us...." he is letting the thought tail off, and not verbalizing a complex set of further imaginings he has there.*
- **EN** Or perhaps you just cannot get back. For reasons I cannot presently conceive.
  **CN** 又或许你只是回不去了。 因为一些我此刻还无法想象的原因。
  *导演注释："with cold hands" is a colloquialism where he comes from, like "in cold blood".*
- **EN** I'm sure there are many difficulties. There are always many difficulties.
  **CN** 我敢肯定困难重重。 困难总是很多。
  *导演注释：(A pause to shake out the previous thoughts; he rubs his eyes hard)*
- **EN** I will try to prevent myself from assuming the worst, but I am done assuming the best either;
  **CN** 我会尽量不往最坏处想，不过也不会再一厢情愿地往好处想了；
- **EN** clinging to hopes that never materialize -- it is not helping me move forward.
  **CN** 执着于从未兑现的希望，根本无法帮我继续前进。
  *导演注释：He is saying "I won't assume the best either", but portmanteaus this into "done assuming the best either".*
- **EN** Goodbye, Quigley. From this point I assume that I shall never see you again.
  **CN** 再见了，奎格利。从现在起，我就当此生再也见不到你了。
- **EN** Whatever is to be gained here, it is for me to discover on my own.
  **CN** 这里无论有什么，都得靠我自己去发现。
  *导演注释：a pause*
- **EN** The team have all been great friends to me, even you, seemingly.
  **CN** 团队里的每个人都曾真心待我，连你似乎也一样。
- **EN** But I built my life from nothing once already, and I may yet carve out another life here.
  **CN** 但我曾白手起家建立自己的生活，也许还能在这里开辟一段新的生活。
- **EN** For I've now seen signs of the caretakers of this Overworld.
  **CN** 因为我现在已经看到一些迹象，证明这个主世界仍有人照料。
  *导演注释：Becoming more solumn and hopeful.*
- **EN** A shimmer through the haze -- a shadowy figure in the distance, cloaked -- gone before I could reach that spot.
  **CN** 迷雾中掠过一抹微光——远处有个披着斗篷的模糊身影—— 等我赶到那里时，已经消失了。
- **EN** It has happened more than once. They are watching.
  **CN** 这种事已经不止一次发生。他们在监视我。
- **EN** The people who dreamt up all this. People who seem interested in *me*.
  **CN** 就是那些构想出这一切的人。他们似乎对我感兴趣。
- **EN** They are just waiting for me to advance a few more steps.
  **CN** 他们只是在等我再向前走上几步。
- **EN** And I'm about to do so.
  **CN** 而我马上就会这么做。
  *导演注释：As in the previous audiolog, he is aware that the Watchers can hear him saying this, and he is appealing to them to some degree. "Advance a few more steps" is not literal; he means he is getting close to the first endgame and knows that this is a turning point in how he may be treated.*

### `queen_og_ask`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 请原谅。我已经……很久没见过其他人了。
  *导演注释：She has decided to drop in on Leamington who has been exploring the facility on his own and has not seen another human being in a long time. He looks at her like she's an oasis in the desert. She teases him to try to break the ice and show it's not serious. We drop into the conversation after she's already into a sentence or two... as it took him this long to press the button on his recorder.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我是说，第一个真人，而不是……幻影？ 你们管那叫什么来着。
  *导演注释：serious, bewildered*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那就好。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那么，你为什么和其他纨绔子弟不一样？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那么，你为什么和其他纨绔子弟不一样？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 所以，你们是个宗教组织？
- **EN** 
  **CN** ……而如果你追求的是清晰明了， 那这个地方为什么要建得这么大？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 说白了，刚抵达时这里让我晕头转向。我倒希望它小一点！
  *导演注释：This is continuing a conversation (but we don't hear what came before this).*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我近来一直省着用它们。 我觉得应该把最后几个信标 留作应急之用。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我留下了自己的录音，好让后来者能追随我的足迹。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可这里属于你们；既然如此，又为什么要留下信标？
  *导演注释：Not understanding; starting to ask for clarification.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可你们后来不录了？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你最后说的那种传统，我倒很想听听。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 比如种群数量的变化、疾病的传播。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我会尽量去了解这些想法。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这我做得到。毕竟，不带成见地观察，是自然哲学的根基。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 什……什么？
  *导演注释：Warmly. She knows she's hit him with a lot.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们有个想法，认为自己的宇宙始于一场极其剧烈的爆炸。
  *导演注释：(wryly)*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 虽然这么直白地说有些不体面，但我得承认： 能让自己多活上许多年……确实很难不心动。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 圣典在上，你就是……！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你在这里多久——
  *导演注释："pleased to meet me" is him being wry, saying like, "I'm such a big deal, you're pleased to meet me" (as opposed to saying "pleased to meet you" of course).*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我……你……
  *导演注释：making a connection in his mind*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我会和她商量。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 换作是我，一定会非常不安。 我想，还是离那里远些为妙。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 要是你们当时已经掌握了创造新空间的技术， 想必就能帮上大忙。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那其他人都住在哪里？
  *导演注释："we" means humanity of Earth but does not literally include her, as she was born later.*

### `audiolog_sky_take_a_tour` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Forgive me. You are the first person I have seen in ... quite some time.
  *导演注释：She has decided to drop in on Leamington who has been exploring the facility on his own and has not seen another human being in a long time. He looks at her like she's an oasis in the desert. She teases him to try to break the ice and show it's not serious. We drop into the conversation after she's already into a sentence or two... as it took him this long to press the button on his recorder.*
- **EN** The first person *in* person, I mean, not as a ... figment? What do you call it.
  *导演注释：serious, bewildered*
- **EN** Good to hear.

### `audiolog_sky_lifeboat_societies_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** So, what made you different from the other rich children?

### `audiolog_sky_lifeboat_societies_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** So, what made you different from the other rich children?

### `audiolog_sky_lifeboat_societies_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** So, you're a religious organization?

### `audiolog_sky_why_so_big` （无官方中译）

- **EN** ... and if you're trying to achieve clarity, then, why's this place so big?
- **EN** To be blunt, it was a disorienting place to land. Would have preferred something smaller!
  *导演注释：This is continuing a conversation (but we don't hear what came before this).*

### `audiolog_s_tradition_of_our_people_part_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I'd been spare with them of late. I felt I should save the last few beacons for emergency purposes.
- **EN** I left my recordings that I might be followed.
- **EN** But you own this place; for what purpose would you leave beacons?
  *导演注释：Not understanding; starting to ask for clarification.*
- **EN** But you stopped making them?

### `audiolog_sky_play_of_form_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I'll enjoy hearing about that last one.

### `audiolog_sky_play_of_form_1a` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Population dynamics, the spread of disease.
- **EN** I'll do what I can to take note of these ideas.
- **EN** I can do that. Seeing objectively is the foundation of Natural Philosophy, after all.
- **EN** What ... what?
  *导演注释：Warmly. She knows she's hit him with a lot.*

### `audiolog_sky_10000_things` （无官方中译）

- **EN** We have an idea that our universe began with an immense explosion.
  *导演注释：(wryly)*

### `audiolog_s_magistrate` （无官方中译）

- **EN** ... and though it feels uncouth to say outright, I must admit: the prospect of greatly prolonging my own life is ... quite a temptation.
- **EN** By the Book, you're the -- !
- **EN** How long --
  *导演注释："pleased to meet me" is him being wry, saying like, "I'm such a big deal, you're pleased to meet me" (as opposed to saying "pleased to meet you" of course).*
- **EN** I -- you --
  *导演注释：making a connection in his mind*
- **EN** I'll discuss.

### `audiolog_sky_wool` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I would regard that with the gravest apprehension. I should think it wise to remove myself some distance away.
- **EN** If you'd already developed the means of creating new volumes of space, that would have been a great help, I imagine.
- **EN** So everyone lives ... where?
  *导演注释："we" means humanity of Earth but does not literally include her, as she was born later.*

### `end1_dimension_0`

- **EN** Welcome back, Acting Director.
  **CN** 欢迎回来，代理主管。
- **EN** Did my best.
  **CN** 我尽力了。
- **EN** 16 months' proper time, yesterday.
  **CN** 到昨天为止，按这里的时间算，已经过去十六个月了。
- **EN** There's a lot to learn yet. And I'm spending a decent amount of time back in this Warehouse.
  **CN** 要学的东西还多着呢。而且， 我也经常回这座仓库，在这儿待的时间可不少。
- **EN** Doing what I can to keep it standing. This place needs a lot of help.
  **CN** 我在尽力撑住这里。这地方需要修补的实在太多了。
- **EN** Is that all on my account?
  **CN** 这条规定是因为我才定的？
  *导演注释：Audible sigh*
- **EN** Apologies.
  **CN** 抱歉。
- **EN** Heroes, Mirrors, Stones, Beams.
  **CN** 英雄、镜子、飞石、光束。
  *导演注释：From here, they are reciting a formal script, with a hint of ritual.*
- **EN** One by one, visit and observe.
  **CN** 逐一造访，细心观察。

### `end1_dimension_1`

- **EN** As you say, Acting Director.
  **CN** 您说得是，代理主管。
- **EN** Not remotely.
  **CN** 差得十万八千里。
- **EN** *Four points* admit *six* such lines.
  **CN** 四点两两相连，可成六线。
  *导演注释：After a pause, return to more formal and ritualistic speech.*
- **EN** Now ascend, from dimension zero, to dimension one.
  **CN** 现在，从零维升至一维。

### `end1_dimension_2`

- **EN** Well ... exhilarating, really. *Quite* different from playing it down there.
  **CN** 嗯……说实话挺刺激的。和在下面亲自解题大不相同。
  *导演注释：Back to more-casual speech.*
- **EN** But I have to say, I don't understand all the ancillary data I'm seeing.
  **CN** 不过我得说，眼前这些辅助数据，我还没能全看懂。
- **EN** Wandering each area, visit any mix of its worlds that you choose to explore.
  **CN** 漫游各片区域，随心探索不同世界的组合。

### `end1_dimension_3`

- **EN** Though I've been to quite a few Warehouses since, I make it a rule to replay this one after every third.
  **CN** 后来我虽然去过不少别的仓库，但还是坚持每去三座， 就回这里重玩一遍。
  *导演注释：Informal conversation.*
- **EN** You entrusted this Warehouse to me. Best I know it intimately.
  **CN** 您将这座仓库托付给我，我自然应该对它了如指掌。
  *导演注释：After "Everything gets old at some point" she almost, but doesn't, say "Even me." But she's thinking about being very old.*
- **EN** Six lines for its edges, four trigons, its sides.
  **CN** 六线为棱，四个三角形为面。
  *导演注释：After a pause, resuming formal / ritual reading.*
- **EN** + unfreeze A simplex, thick with worlds in play; roam within their rich display.
  **CN** + unfreeze 一个单纯形，其间世界纷呈流转； 漫步其间，尽览繁盛万象。

### `end1_dimension_4`

- **EN** The simplex now is whole.
  **CN** 这个单纯形如今完整了。
- **EN** Through the years, as you continue,
  **CN** 在往后的岁月里，当你继续前行，
- **EN** So mote it be.
  **CN** 诚愿如此。
- **EN** It is my pleasure.
  **CN** 这是我的荣幸。
  *导演注释：Back to informal conversation.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你说的是“划界”，不是“创造”。 这么说，这些东西原本就已经存在了？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这方面已经形成了一门相当庞大的分支学科。 我工作中用不上，但基本原理还是懂的。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 说它源于生物本能，完全讲得通。感到意外，其实就是发现
- **EN** 
  **CN** 自己对世界的理解中，有哪里出了错，或不够完备；
- **EN** 
  **CN** 修补这些错误，对我们大有裨益。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 积极的情绪，有助于把意外的那一刻牢牢固定在记忆里——
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这样我们才能时常回味、细细琢磨，从而增进理解。 倘若不这么做，未来的意外可能就会要了我们的命。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 因此，对意外怀有积极情感的生灵，死得更少，繁衍得更多。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 好吧，我洗耳恭听。
  *导演注释：Concluding remark.*

### `audiolog_sky_laws_of_form` （无官方中译）

### `audiolog_sky_play_of_form_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Its being biological makes perfect sense. To be surprised is to discover
- **EN** something wrong or incomplete in one's understanding of the world;
- **EN** it's beneficial to repair those mistakes.
- **EN** Positive emotion helps anchor the moment of surprise in memory --
- **EN** that we may revisit it frequently, dwell upon it, and improve our understanding. If we do *not* do so, future surprises could kill us.
- **EN** Thus creatures with positive feelings about surprise die less, and reproduce more.
- **EN** Well. I am here to listen.
  *导演注释：Concluding remark.*
- **EN** You should know that I've been watching your progress throughout the Overworld.
- **EN** I apologize for what may seem an invasion of privacy. It's the way they do things here.
  *导演注释：This happens a couple of years after he has entered the facility. He has been a member of OSS for these years and his diction might by lightened up.*
- **EN** For me, it's been tremendously instructive, watching you grapple with these same problems that I myself faced not long ago.
- **EN** I am somewhat new here. I have a direct overseer watching me, as I am watching you.
- **EN** You heard her earlier, during the first Endgame. She does not want me to communicate with you directly.
- **EN** Non-communication is a strict rule usually, which she broke for my sake, but we're enforcing it again. We are not supposed to pollute you, you see.
- **EN** I enjoy dictating my thoughts, as may be obvious by now;
- **EN** but when you enter this next Endgame, I'll be using written Signs to indicate the course of action.
  *导演注释：After a pause*
- **EN** Eventually we may discuss why. For now you'll have to take it as given.
  *导演注释：The capitalization on Signs is part of an Order habit to capitalize the names of gameplay objects.*
- **EN** Good luck.
- **EN** As I have previously mentioned, I had a choice whether to dictate or to write Signs, and I chose to write.
- **EN** There was then, naturally, a further choice: how *much* to write.
- **EN** If I wrote at great length, I could be very specific in pointing out every detail that I wish you to observe.
- **EN** But I prefer to use the nonverbal channels, when I am able to employ them successfully.
- **EN** So I chose brevity, to give space for the Puzzles themselves to speak.
- **EN** This representation of me, they call a 'hologram', though where I come from, the corresponding word indicates a mirage made only of light.
- **EN** But as you can feel, this 'hologram' is solid. Once I've reached a certain rank I'll be taught the sciences that make it possible.
- **EN** In this area, inert Signs are no longer sufficient for the level of communication we wish to provide.
- **EN** The hologram will draw upon a wider variety of pre-written responses.
  *导演注释：There is a paragraph break before "In this area," since it's a different topic. But the spreadsheet formatting might put them together.*
- **EN** It is also a somewhat more-personal substitute for my presence.
- **EN** Godspeed.
- **EN** As with the previous Endgame, she gave me a choice of medium:
- **EN** She said I could communicate via writing, or imbue the hologram with voice.
- **EN** Voice would make the hologram more lifelike, as though I were speaking to you directly;
- **EN** and it would add nuance to the communications, via inflection, pacing and all that.
- **EN** But I felt voice to be the wrong mode here.
- **EN** In these Puzzles, when you are not quite sure of the answer,
- **EN** you may try many different possibilities and see what I have to say of each.
- **EN** If my hologram's responses were vocal, I think it would become tiring to receive the same response five times in a row;
- **EN** And, you must stand there listening awhile, merely to discover whether the response has changed!
- **EN** You hear the first few words, and wonder: "is this the same response I heard last time,
- **EN** or one that begins with the same words, but changes towards the end?"
- **EN** You listen for some duration, just to realize there is no new information.
- **EN** Thus your attention is spent to no purpose, and you have a little less energy to exert on the Puzzles.
- **EN** I do not like that, and I don't think you would either; the repetition would become grating.
- **EN** When the response is visual, as when written on a Sign,
- **EN** it can be inspected in an instant; and, it's no surprise when a Sign *doesn't* change.
- **EN** I think she approved of my reasoning, though it would be outside the rules
- **EN** for her to discuss it until this process is complete.
  *导演注释：short pause to switch gears*
- **EN** You are seeing this place as a battery of challenges prepared for you,
- **EN** and it is; but it's also a test for me.
- **EN** How well did I communicate via my hologram's responses?
- **EN** Did I anticipate all that you may try? Did I miss any situation that should have been met with a customized response?
  *导演注释："How well did I communicate" is the heading question; the questions after are more like bullet points explicating this top question.*
- **EN** Am I good enough to guide you through, or will I step on your toes mid-dance?
- **EN** In the course of authoring the hologram's responses, I needed to learn the system of reasoning they developed for these purposes --
- **EN** it's quite a thing, creating a mechanism that can sense the positions of objects,
  *导演注释：He's talking about a kind of simple computer programming.*
- **EN** shunt this information through some complex permutations of ... connectedness, and in the end decide which feedback to give.
- **EN** Where I came from, we had perhaps the beginnings of this kind of reasoning;
- **EN** but here, they've laid out entire fields of research around each seemingly simple idea,
- **EN** the existence of which a layman would never foresee.
- **EN** These techniques amount to setting down a sequence of instructions to be performed by an automatic device.
- **EN** It sounds simple enough. And it is, while the sequence remains short.
- **EN** But as the sequence becomes longer, the system begins to exhibit behaviors
- **EN** you did not foresee, and certainly do not wish to occur.
- **EN** No. It's really not simple at all. But I am learning rapidly.
- **EN** Okay then.
- **EN** Seeing as you've made it here, this means that, beginner though I am,
- **EN** the quality of my work has proved sufficient.
- **EN** This is a success for me. I shall move forward. Thank you for that.
- **EN** Perhaps someday I will be given the chance to design my own Warehouse, and you may try it;
- **EN** but before that can happen, there's a problem they must solve that lies beyond my present learning.
- **EN** The Director -- the still-titular Director -- said that erosion comes slowly at first,
- **EN** barely noticeable; then it accelerates, and before you can blink, it's a landslide.
- **EN** But if decay can come quickly, perhaps it can go quickly.
- **EN** I'll endeavor to advance, that soon I may actually help.
- **EN** Thank you for walking with me. As my mentor is fond of saying: See you later, Alligator.

<!-- ================= 05_magistrate_reynard ================= -->

# 治安官 Reynard

> Heroes 线大反派，后为仓房前任守门人（"按我的固有时间算，整整十四年了"）。证据：warrior 台词 "Magistrate Reynard felt slimy from the beginning"。

**统计**：台词块 118 · 英文词数 937 · 涉及段落 14 · 官方中译覆盖 94/118

---


### `heroes_warrior_mistrust`

- **EN** I heard that!
  **CN** 我可听见了！
  *导演注释：To self... but overheard*
- **EN** I'm not slimy, good sir Valens; I'm merely ... ulterior.
  **CN** 我可不是油滑小人，瓦伦斯先生；我只是……另有所图罢了。
- **EN** Come, Ratleigh. If you're to assume my duties here, I have much more to show you. Behind the scenes.
  **CN** 来吧，拉特利。既然你要接替我在这里的职责， 我还有很多东西要带你见识。台面之下的东西。
- **EN** + magistrate_disappear 2 Wouldn't dream of it!
  **CN** + magistrate_disappear 2 别做梦了！

### `heroes1_warrior_wizard`

- **EN** Now, Ratleigh, observe the way they work together. There is a lesson in this.
  **CN** 拉特利，好好看看他们是怎么配合的。这里头有学问。
- **EN** + magistrate_disappear 0.5 Shush!!
  **CN** + magistrate_disappear 0.5 嘘！！
  *导演注释：whispering*

### `heroes_mouse`

- **EN** Watch, Ratleigh, as we free the mouse, and he skitters through our maze.
  **CN** 看好了，拉特利，我们放掉这只老鼠， 看它在我们布下的迷宫里乱窜。
- **EN** + magistrate_disappear Shhhhh!
  **CN** + magistrate_disappear 嘘——！

### `heroes_jail2`

- **EN** Indeed, no more bars for you, Gravy Sorcerer!
  **CN** 是啊，这次可没有栅栏了，你这贪吃的大法师！
- **EN** Let us be off, Ratleigh, and leave them to ponder their mistakes... of which, there are so, SO many.
  **CN** 走吧，拉特利，让他们好好反省自己的过错…… 毕竟多得数都数不清。

### `heroes2_thief_goblins`

- **EN** Witness, Ratleigh, the precarity in which our Heroine is now placed! Watch how she weaves.
  **CN** 见证吧，拉特利，我们的女英雄现在处境有多岌岌可危！ 看她如何左躲右闪。
- **EN** + magistrate_disappear 1 Not now. Watch!
  **CN** + magistrate_disappear 1 现在别问。看着！
  *导演注释：Whispering. He is asking "what does precarity mean?"*

### `heroes2_thief_wizard_priestess_compartments`

- **EN** Observe, Ratleigh, how the complexities of this mechanism slow them,
  **CN** 看好了，拉特利，这套机关的复杂程度足以拖住他们，
- **EN** giving us time to bait the final trap. Let us be off.
  **CN** 够时间让我们布置最后的陷阱了。我们走吧。
  *导演注释：Calling out from the shadows, audible by all...*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你们想知道吧！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 回头见！

### `heroes2_thief_wizard_priestess_compartments_mid` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Wouldn't you like to know!
- **EN** Be seeing you!

### `heroes2_four`

- **EN** Walls did not contain them, Ratleigh, so behold: we now put Dragons to this use.
  **CN** 拉特利，既然高墙拦不住他们，那就看好了： 这回我们用龙。
- **EN** Fire will hold them, as we move onward to raise the final pillars of my design.
  **CN** 烈火自会拦住他们，我们则继续前进， 逐一立起我蓝图的最后几根支柱。
  *导演注释：Calling out from the shadows...*

### `heroes3_four_with_dragon`

- **EN** I learned my lesson with you, pesky wizard.
  **CN** 吃一堑长一智，讨厌的法师。
- **EN** I've had my second-favorite son Ratleigh clear this area of anything you could possibly employ
  **CN** 我已经叫我第二喜欢的儿子拉特利， 把这片区域里你们可能用上的东西全搬走了，
- **EN** to weasel past this faithful Guard Dragon.
  **CN** 免得你们钻空子，绕过这条忠诚的守卫龙。
- **EN** Good resource management, Ratleigh.
  **CN** 资源管理得不错，拉特利。
- **EN** No time to discuss! Soon I must away, via my ... special transport.
  **CN** 没时间讨论了！我很快要用……特别的法子离开。
- **EN** We've a terrible paucity of time in which to finish impressing
  **CN** 我们剩下共处的时间不多了，我得赶紧把该教的都教给你，
  *导演注释：The 'special transport' is interdimensional travel out of their world, as enabled by Acting Director.*
- **EN** all the great multitude of your new duties into your tiny little head.
  **CN** 把那一大堆新职责，全都塞进你那小小的脑袋里。
- **EN** Come along!
  **CN** 跟上！

### `heroes3_victory_lap`

- **EN** In that case, let me tell you all a story.
  **CN** 既然如此，我给各位讲个故事。
  *导演注释："my charge to apprehend" is a callback to his question in heroes3_warrior_and_druid_1*
- **EN** Once-a-time, just some months ago actually, the sickly moon casting down
  **CN** 从前……其实也就是几个月前。惨白的月光倾洒而下，
- **EN** through my window, I was knelt by my bed, praying.
  **CN** 透过窗户照了进来，我正跪在床边祈祷。
- **EN** Utterly despondent from all my problems. What do I do with my son Ratleigh,
  **CN** 种种烦恼让我万念俱灰。我该拿儿子拉特利怎么办，
- **EN** to help him become -- aught but such a useless lump?
  **CN** 才能让他……至少不再是这么个没用的废物？
- **EN** And as for me -- is my time of youthful creativity expended?
  **CN** 至于我——难道我年少时的创造力已经耗尽？
- **EN** Am I to be set aside, my role from now til the End being merely to dodder and wither away?
  **CN** 难道往后余生，我都只能被晾在一旁，颤颤巍巍地衰老下去？
- **EN** I am an accomplished man. I have done some quite good things -- greatly increased the efficiency of the prisons,
  **CN** 我也是有所成就的人，做过不少好事， 比如大幅提升了监狱的运作效率，
- **EN** and ensured that many criminals could no longer feed upon the upright citizenry,
  **CN** 让许多罪犯再也无法欺压正直的百姓，
  *导演注释：(a bit of a gear shift as he goes back to explain how he arrived at this point)*
- **EN** and thus kept our land a healthy place in which the good may thrive.
  **CN** 从而让我们的土地安宁有序，让良善之人得以安居乐业。
- **EN** But such accomplishments, after years, become ... taken for granted. Usual, Obvious, Boring!
  **CN** 可几年过去，人们就把这些功绩视为…… 理所当然。稀松平常！本来就该如此！无聊透顶！
- **EN** Life had become paperwork, maintenance, a slate of ongoing convictions that really *anyone* could prosecute in my stead.
  **CN** 每天就是处理文书、打理日常事务， 还摊上一连串没完没了的定罪判刑。 这些事，随便换个人都能替我做。
- **EN** I was weighed down each day with an ever-greater fatigue.
  **CN** 我一天比一天疲惫，愈发觉得不堪重负。
- **EN** On the night in question, I felt my usefulness had been fully exhausted. In complete honesty, I was drenched in tears.
  **CN** 就在那天夜里，我觉得自己已经毫无用处。 说实话，我当时早已泪流满面。
- **EN** I was praying out loud, as I usually do -- so that God sees I really mean it, you know --
  **CN** 我像往常一样大声祈祷—— 这样神灵才看得出我是认真的，你懂吧——
- **EN** and for once, my prayers were heard in concrete fashion.
  **CN** 这一次，我的祈祷竟然得到了实实在在的回应。
- **EN** Before me appeared a woman, bathed in multicolored light, beatific.
  **CN** 一名女子出现在我面前。她沐浴在五彩光芒中，神圣而安详。
  *导演注释："for once" -- he has been praying thus for years, to no clear effect.*
- **EN** I understand now that she was not an angel, but she might as well have been.
  **CN** 如今我明白她并非天使，却也与天使无异。
  *导演注释：As he says the lines below about the woman, he is aware that she may be watching him say these things (though his audience doesn't know this),*
- **EN** She said she needed my help.
  **CN** 她说，她需要我的帮助。
- **EN** She had pledged her life to walking in the ways of God, and said that I could walk beside her,
  **CN** 她立誓终生遵循神灵之道，还说我也可以与她同行，
- **EN** so long as I first did ... a certain thing.
  **CN** 只要我先完成……某件事。
- **EN** What she asked of me was very difficult to eventuate, and took lavish expenditure, I must say.
  **CN** 她交代我的事极难完成，而且耗资不菲。这点我必须说明。
- **EN** She needed me to bring together a particular set of elements, all into one place:
  **CN** 她要我把一组特定元素全部汇聚在同一处：
  *导演注释：TRANSLATOR note: The phrase "very difficult to eventuate and took lavish expenditure" recurs several times through the game, and should be translated the same way each time, so that the player can match them up.*
- **EN** + flash_warrior Warrior,
  **CN** + flash_warrior 战士，
- **EN** + flash_thief Thief,
  **CN** + flash_thief 盗贼，
  *导演注释：Each of the following elements is stated separately and prominently and given its own time, and we flash the elements on the screen as he indicates them. For example, as he says "Warrior", we flash the Warrior in his position on screen, to call the player's attention to him.*
- **EN** + flash_wizard Wizard,
  **CN** + flash_wizard 法师，
- **EN** + flash_priestess Priestess,
  **CN** + flash_priestess 女祭司，
- **EN** + flash_bard Bard,
  **CN** + flash_bard 吟游诗人，
- **EN** + flash_druid Druid;
  **CN** + flash_druid 德鲁伊；
- **EN** + flash_goblins Goblins,
  **CN** + flash_goblins 哥布林，
- **EN** + flash_dragons Fire-breathing Dragons,
  **CN** + flash_dragons 喷火龙，
- **EN** + flash_crystals Crystals,
  **CN** + flash_crystals 水晶，
- **EN** + flash_buttons_and_gates Buttons and Energy Gates;
  **CN** + flash_buttons_and_gates 按钮和能量门；
- **EN** and if I did all this, and did it well, she'd set me upon the course of a new life.
  **CN** 只要我把这些都办好，她就会为我开启一段新的人生。
- **EN** She said I had truly exceptional powers of Judgement;
  **CN** 她说我拥有真正非凡的判断力；
- **EN** To be seen so clearly released in me a torrent of love.
  **CN** 她竟如此了解我，我心中顿时涌出无尽爱意。
- **EN** I have worked so hard to hone my Judgement, you have no idea how hard.
  **CN** 我为磨炼自己的判断力付出多少，你们根本无法想象。
- **EN** She said she'd give me a new way to sophisticate my Judgement, in and among a wider Existence.
  **CN** 她说会给我一个新办法，让我能在更广阔的天地里磨炼判断力。
- **EN** Dear Listener, she showed me visions,
  **CN** 亲爱的听众，她让我看到了幻象：
- **EN** a vast array of distant lands, and I loved the whole of what I saw.
  **CN** 一大片辽远的土地，而我爱上了眼前的一切。
- **EN** I knelt to her, and pledged to perform.
  **CN** 我跪在她面前，发誓完成使命。
- **EN** And now, *right* now, right this *second*,
  **CN** 而现在，就在此刻，就在这一秒，
- **EN** the act is consummated.
  **CN** 使命终于完成。
- **EN** You won't catch me today. Perhaps not ever.
  **CN** 你们今天抓不到我。也许永远都抓不到。
  *导演注释：snaps his fingers*
- **EN** But you haven't failed, because now we're all a part of something bigger.
  **CN** 可你们并未失败，因为从此刻起，我们都属于更宏大的事业。
- **EN** + magistrate_disappear 4.8 I *now* ... ... ... take my leave. See you later, Alligators!
  **CN** + magistrate_disappear 4.8 我现在……就此告辞。回头见啦，小鳄鱼们！

### `queen_og_ask`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 总算找到你了！
  *导演注释：She sees Magistrate Reynard approaching.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 按你的吩咐，我一直在四乘四组合体中工作。
  *导演注释：This Magistrate scene occurs much later (about 14 years), after he has departed his world and joined up with the Order of the Sinking Star.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 试着把断掉的连接接回去，诸如此类。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 虽有进展，但十分有限。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 正是在下。肉身俱在，心神俱在，连灵魂也在！ 见到我，荣幸吧。
  *导演注释："by the Book" is an interjection, like "Great Scott" or "Oh my God".*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 按我自己经历的时间算，足足十四年了。 但我内心深处的某个角落，还是从前的我。
  *导演注释："How long have you been here?"*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你看着像新来的。
  *导演注释："proper time" as in Einsteinian relativity -- different people experience different amounts of time -- but here it's more pronounced, because when you're in a different space that is disconnected, your rate of passage of time is not necessarily correlated. Magistrate knows that Leamington is referring to "how long since the end of Heroes 3".*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你就是那位界门来客？
  *导演注释：factually obseving*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你竟然就这么不请自来，简直离谱。
  *导演注释：doesn't know what to make of this characterization; at the very least, it's oversimplified and dismissive*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我好歹还得先被判定符合标准，才能拿到邀请。不过，欢迎。
  *导演注释：(He is talking to Leamington)*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我一直在研究那位界门来客所在世界的规则， 想看看能不能把它纳入四乘四组合体， 替换掉其中一个世界，以降低整体互动密度。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这个方案有可行之处。我们该谈谈。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 还得自信地谈。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你大概已经猜到了：这说明我重新连上了你的家乡。 没错，我已经连上了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你随时都能走！从你的日志看，你也早就归心似箭了。
  *导演注释：turning to Leamington*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 当然，要私下商量。
  *导演注释："I'll discuss all this with Acting Director at a later date, but I don't want to give you very much."*

### `audiolog_s_magistrate` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Found you at last!
  *导演注释：She sees Magistrate Reynard approaching.*
- **EN** As you requested, I've been working at the four-four.
  *导演注释：This Magistrate scene occurs much later (about 14 years), after he has departed his world and joined up with the Order of the Sinking Star.*
- **EN** Attempting to mend connections that have broken, and so forth.
- **EN** Success is limited, but nonzero.
- **EN** None other. In the flesh. In the spirit. In the *soul*! Pleased to meet me.
  *导演注释："by the Book" is an interjection, like "Great Scott" or "Oh my God".*
- **EN** By my proper time, it's been a good fourteen years. But, I am still the same man I was. Deep down. Somewhere.
  *导演注释："How long have you been here?"*
- **EN** You seem new.
  *导演注释："proper time" as in Einsteinian relativity -- different people experience different amounts of time -- but here it's more pronounced, because when you're in a different space that is disconnected, your rate of passage of time is not necessarily correlated. Magistrate knows that Leamington is referring to "how long since the end of Heroes 3".*
- **EN** Are you the Doorstead Fellow?
  *导演注释：factually obseving*
- **EN** Outrageous how you just *showed up*, without an invitation.
  *导演注释：doesn't know what to make of this characterization; at the very least, it's oversimplified and dismissive*
- **EN** I at least had to be judged worthy of an invite. But, welcome.
  *导演注释：(He is talking to Leamington)*
- **EN** I've been looking into his dynamics as a possible lower-density substitution within the four-four.
- **EN** There is an option there. We should talk about it.
- **EN** Confidently.
- **EN** You'll surmise this means I have connected back to your home. Which I have.
- **EN** You can leave any time you want! From your diaries, it seems you're quite eager.
  *导演注释：turning to Leamington*
- **EN** In confidence, of course.
  *导演注释："I'll discuss all this with Acting Director at a later date, but I don't want to give you very much."*

### `audiolog_di_tiger_lily_v2`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 可他们也被包围了！
  *导演注释：(at 'Inner Guard' he is shouting to everyone within 100 feet)*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 时机已到。北边！
  *导演注释：(simultaneously w/ Jackays above; she recognizes his voice, is confused by how she could be hearing that voice in this place and time, but also, she is starting to understand, and realizes this is her salvation)*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 东边！
  *导演注释：Aiah is the Priestess' name (nobody knows this but Thief)*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 西边！
  *导演注释：There's a push and a mirror teleport sound, and the sound of Priestess protecting the Queen*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 接下来……南边！
  *导演注释：Bard has teleported via the endpoint beam. (playing her instrument)*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 看来我找到新爱好了。那么，接下来轮到谁了？……
  *导演注释：Endpoint off mirror drowns some guys. After she says "Engaging": We hear Sailor push a crystal skipping stone that skips quickly, then it gets mirrored and cloned, and two of these come to rest on land. We hear a volley of arrows thudding into one of these.*

### `end3_suit` （无官方中译）

- **EN** But *they* are also surrounded!
  *导演注释：(at 'Inner Guard' he is shouting to everyone within 100 feet)*
- **EN** It is time. North!
  *导演注释：(simultaneously w/ Jackays above; she recognizes his voice, is confused by how she could be hearing that voice in this place and time, but also, she is starting to understand, and realizes this is her salvation)*
- **EN** East!
  *导演注释：Aiah is the Priestess' name (nobody knows this but Thief)*
- **EN** West!
  *导演注释：There's a push and a mirror teleport sound, and the sound of Priestess protecting the Queen*
- **EN** Aaaaaand South!
  *导演注释：Bard has teleported via the endpoint beam. (playing her instrument)*
- **EN** I believe I've found my new hobby. Now, who's next?...
  *导演注释：Endpoint off mirror drowns some guys. After she says "Engaging": We hear Sailor push a crystal skipping stone that skips quickly, then it gets mirrored and cloned, and two of these come to rest on land. We hear a volley of arrows thudding into one of these.*


<!-- ================= 06_warrior_valens ================= -->

# 战士 Valens

> Heroes 三主角之一。证据：thief_first "Isidore! Valens!"；magistrate 称 "good sir Valens"。配音：Liam O’Brien。

**统计**：台词块 252 · 英文词数 1549 · 涉及段落 105 · 官方中译覆盖 177/252

---


### `warrior_heroes1_start_end`

- **EN** When we catch this guy, I'm going to give him a big ... big piece of my mind.
  **CN** 等我们抓到那家伙，我非得好好……好好骂他一顿。
- **EN** Wish I could punch him. Hard.
  **CN** 真想狠狠揍他一拳。
  *导演注释：To self. He pauses before 'big piece of my mind' ... the idea being he wants to just punch him, but knows he can't (due to his Swordbinding restriction).*

### `warrior_heroes1_call`

- **EN** This place is so huge. How am I going to find the others?
  **CN** 这地方也太大了。我上哪儿找他们去？
- **EN** Tif! Isidore!
  **CN** 蒂芙！伊西多尔！
  *导演注释：To self.*
- **EN** Seems I'm on my own for now. Let's go.
  **CN** 看来暂时只能靠自己了。走吧。
  *导演注释：Calling out.*

### `heroes_warrior_gate`

- **EN** Oh, one of these. Stay out of my way, you stupid ... energy.
  **CN** 又来了。给我让开，你这破……能量。

### `heroes_warrior_mistrust`

- **EN** We did really need the job, but I should have said something anyway.
  **CN** 我们当时太需要这份活儿了，可我本该说点什么的。
- **EN** Magistrate Reynard felt slimy from the beginning.
  **CN** 雷纳德执政官打从一开始就透着一股油滑劲儿。
  *导演注释：To self... but overheard*
- **EN** You!
  **CN** 你！
- **EN** Stop and explain yourself!
  **CN** 站住，给我解释清楚！

### `heroes_warrior_where_is_tif`

- **EN** When we came through during the appraisal, Tif loved this hall.
  **CN** 我们当初来估价、经过这里时，蒂芙就很喜欢这间大厅。
- **EN** I hope that girl's okay.
  **CN** 希望那丫头没事。
  *导演注释：To self.*

### `heroes1_warrior_wizard`

- **EN** Hey Izzy...
  **CN** 嘿，伊兹……
- **EN** I can't get out my way either.
  **CN** 我也出不去。
- **EN** We always do!
  **CN** 我们总能逃出生天！

### `heroes1_warrior_thief`

- **EN** Tif! Are you all right?
  **CN** 蒂芙！你没事吧？
- **EN** He went the other way looking for you.
  **CN** 他往另一边找你去了。
- **EN** Yeah... there are a lot of Other Ways in here.
  **CN** 这个嘛……这里能叫“另一边”的路还真不少。

### `heroes1_warrior_thief_mid0`

- **EN** Even when we find Izzy again, then what? We just leave, no harm done?
  **CN** 就算找到伊兹，然后呢？ 我们就这么走了，当什么都没发生？

### `heroes1_warrior_thief_mid`

- **EN** Oh. Okay. How do you know that?
  **CN** 哦。好。你怎么知道？
- **EN** You were eyeing his jewelry. Of course.
  **CN** 你果然一直盯着他的首饰。
- **EN** Oh! A key ... for a locker ... in The Crawler??
  **CN** 哦！一把钥匙……开储物柜的…… “爬行者号”上的储物柜？？
  *导演注释：Warrior and Thief used to work at the loading area for The Crawler, so they are familiar with these key pendants. She is going into the details of the physical description hoping he will catch up and "get it".*
- **EN**

### `heroes2_warrior_goblins_1`

- **EN** Sore back, crick in my neck, two days without sleep,
  **CN** 腰酸背痛，脖子都僵了，两天没合眼，
- **EN** and now... goblins.
  **CN** 现在还有……哥布林。

### `heroes2_warrior_goblins_2`

- **EN** Was hoping that'd take me out, not further in.
  **CN** 还以为那条路能带我出去，结果越走越深。

### `heroes2_warrior_goblins_2_mid`

- **EN** If I could still swing a sword, this would be a very different situation.
  **CN** 要是我还能挥剑，现在的局面可就大不一样了。

### `heroes2_warrior_goblins_2_mid_2`

- **EN** If I'd a sword, we might've repelled those raiders, and they wouldn't have scattered the caravan.
  **CN** 要是我有剑，兴许能打退劫匪， 商队也不至于七零八落。
- **EN** And I wouldn't even be here.
  **CN** 我也不至于沦落到这儿。

### `heroes2_warrior_goblins_2_end`

- **EN** Thank God to be out of there!
  **CN** 谢天谢地，总算出来了！

### `heroes2_warrior_dragon_intro`

- **EN** Town's been overrun. Caravan should have got moving sooner.
  **CN** 镇子已经沦陷了。商队早该出发的。
- **EN** I told them we needed to hurry.
  **CN** 我早就跟他们说了，得快点。

### `heroes2_warrior_dragon_intro_end`

- **EN** Here's hoping the others make it to the rally point.
  **CN** 希望其他人也能顺利赶到集合点。

### `heroes2_three_first`

- **EN** He? You think Reynard was behind the attack on the Caravan?
  **CN** 他？你觉得商队遇袭，是雷纳德策划的？
- **EN** None dare; the Caravan's defenses are legendary!...
  **CN** 没人敢这么做！商队的防卫可是出了名的！……

### `heroes2_three_first_mid`

- **EN** Not at all. It's only the higher-ups who pretend to hate me. The regular guys on the line, they respect what I did.
  **CN** 才不是。也就上头那帮人装得跟多讨厌我似的。 前线那些普通弟兄可都佩服我干的事。
- **EN** Let's just keep moving.
  **CN** 还是赶路吧。

### `heroes2_three_first_end`

- **EN** I'll search the East!
  **CN** 我搜东边！

### `heroes2_warrior_monster_gate_first`

- **EN** Another dumb ... energy ... box.
  **CN** 又一个破……能量……箱子。

### `heroes2_warrior_monster_gate_first_end`

- **EN** Dumb energy box is no match for me!
  **CN** 破能量箱才不是我的对手！

### `heroes2_warrior_monster_gate_2_optional_end`

- **EN** Thank God they didn't foresee I'd push crystals as a weapon.
  **CN** 幸好他们没料到我会把水晶当武器推。

### `heroes2_warrior_3monster`

- **EN** As long as the King keeps up his end of the deal, and orders my Swordbinding dispelled, this will all have been worth it.
  **CN** 只要国王信守他的承诺，下令解除我的剑缚， 这一切就都值了。

### `heroes2_warrior_3monster_end`

- **EN** Can't wait to deliver this guy directly to the King.
  **CN** 真想赶紧把这家伙亲手交给国王。

### `heroes2_postmeetup_warrior`

- **EN** Who'd have guessed that starting a moving company could get us into so much trouble.
  **CN** 谁能想到，开搬运公司竟会给我们惹来这么多麻烦。
- **EN** And that getting into trouble could lead to a deal with the King. A path toward absolution!
  **CN** 惹上麻烦，竟又能让我们和国王达成交易， 给我一个获赦的机会！
  *导演注释：To self.*
- **EN** But, who'd have guessed that a deal with the King would shunt us into *even worse* trouble.
  **CN** 可谁又能想到，和国王做交易， 会把我们推进更大的麻烦里。
  *导演注释：Continuing onward in a chain of cause-and-effect.*
- **EN** Sometimes, the only way out is ... forward.
  **CN** 有时候，唯一的出路就是……继续向前。
  *导演注释：Continuing onward in a chain of cause-and-effect.*

- **EN** Oh, we're about to have some fun, aren't we?
  **CN** 嚯，有好戏看了，是不是？

- **EN** Dead monsters ... satisfying.
  **CN** 怪物死了……真解气。

### `heroes2_one_fire_warrior_thief_optional`

- **EN** Small won't help. The enchantment's not that dumb, Tif.
  **CN** 小也没用。那咒术没那么蠢，蒂芙。
  *导演注释：Wishing the Warrior could just attack the goblins that are here, but he is Swordbound, an enchantment that keeps him from using swords. So she wonders what else he might use.*
- **EN** Tif, it's called "Swordbound" because we're Swordsmen.
  **CN** 蒂芙，之所以叫“剑缚”，是因为我们是剑士。
- **EN** But the binding isn't limited to blades.
  **CN** 可这道束缚并不只限于刀剑。
- **EN** I can't swing *anything* at *anyone*. Including a Goblin, sadly.
  **CN** 不管拿什么，我都没法动手打人。可惜，连哥布林也打不了。
- **EN** I could push them sternly with my fist. Not going to help.
  **CN** 我倒是可以用拳头使劲推他们。没什么用就是了。

### `heroes2_one_fire_warrior_thief_optional_end`

- **EN** Not today at least. Let's hope our luck holds.
  **CN** 至少今天不需要。希望运气一直这么好吧。

- **EN** I'm so happy to have some help. Once-a-time I would have slain these goblins easily,
  **CN** 有人帮忙真是太好了。以前我能轻松宰了这帮哥布林，
- **EN** but I can't now. Restrictions.
  **CN** 可现在不行。受了剑缚。

### `heroes2_warrior_priestess_first_end`

- **EN** Please travel with us. We're trying to stop a really bad guy.
  **CN** 请和我们同行吧。我们要阻止一个很坏的家伙。

### `heroes2_warrior_priestess_second`

- **EN** If we catch Magistrate Reynard, the King will order my Swordbinding lifted.
  **CN** 只要抓住雷纳德执政官，国王就会下令解除我的剑缚。
- **EN** And that will give me freedom to set my own path,
  **CN** 那样我就能自由地走自己的路了，
  *导演注释：Self-psychologizing himself to Priestess, who never says anything.*
- **EN** rather than just cowering, and accepting whatever scraps are left me.
  **CN** 而不是畏畏缩缩，只能接受别人施舍的那点机会。
- **EN** Even if I never make my living by the sword again... it's the indignity of it, you know?
  **CN** 就算我以后再也不靠剑谋生…… 可被这样束缚着，实在太屈辱了，你明白吗？
- **EN** The deal with the King aside, I'd capture this snakeweasel just on principle. He's left enough wreckage behind him already.
  **CN** 撇开和国王的交易不谈，光凭原则我也要抓住这条毒蛇。 他身后已经留下够多残骸了。

### `heroes2_warrior_priestess_second_end`

- **EN** My meeting spot with my friend Tif is up ahead. You'll like her.
  **CN** 我和我朋友蒂芙的碰头点就在前面。你会喜欢她的。

  **CN** 怎么讲？

### `queen_og_ask`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 真不敢相信，原来水全都……聚在一起，会是这个样子。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 要是我……掉进那东西里，会怎么样？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 等我们把水运回家，它还能这样黏在一起吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我可不想挨着一整车水……看它炸成一颗颗珠子。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 话说回来，人家干嘛要买这种水？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不知道哥布林会怎么看这些古怪的水。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这故事够我以后讲给孙辈听了。前提是我能活着离开这里……
- **EN** 
  **CN** ……再遇上一个好姑娘……再生几个孩子……
  *导演注释：To self.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** ……等孩子们也有了孩子……
  *导演注释：To self.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 伊兹说，出口有可能就在这个方向。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我会继续往前走……不过，说不定他那边更顺利。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 龙朋友？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 听他那么一说，这玩意好像挺有意思……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这么多石头！总算能好好活动一下筋骨了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 知道吗，我还真能习惯这种感觉。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我也没怎么听懂。他说，那地方的空间很小，没多少空旷的地方。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 像是世上的一切都挤在一间小屋里。他说，那里像个甜甜圈， 绕着绕着就会回到原处。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 谁不喜欢甜甜圈呢。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 嗯……这地方没多大……就跟个小房间差不多……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可要说像甜甜圈……我怎么没看出来！
  *导演注释：To self. Evaluating whether it's what Izzy is looking for.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这里真瘆人。还是干活吧，免得胡思乱想……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 熟悉得叫人发毛。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 就快到会合点了。希望那姑娘没事。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这么说，你最好的朋友是条“船”。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可也就一人大小。
- **EN** 
  **CN** “船”怎么会是活的？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 比如呢？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们没有这种船。不过……在我家乡，交通工具都是由人来驾驭的。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你不能自己驾驶那艘“船”吗？
  *导演注释：The only vehicles they have travel on land.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 所以，还没把他放进“船”里，公会就先跟他谈好条件了？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可他是个鬼魂啊。也就是说，你们公会能和鬼魂说话？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 他们怎么跟鬼魂……呃，灵体交谈？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 什么原理？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我的天。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 好吧，各位龙，我得借你们的本事一用。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你怎么进来的？
  *导演注释：This is an old acquaintance of Warrior who taught him how to use his Swordbinding to channel extra strength.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这事我们之后得好好谈谈。不过先把眼下的事办完。
  *导演注释：after a beat*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 一路上真少不了这招。太感谢你的指点了！
  *导演注释：This is the technique he taught Warrior that lets him push a huge amount of stuff. It involves redirecting your personal energy through the Swordbound restriction so that it gets squeezed (but not cut off) and the squeezing amplifies the force.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 最近我老是晕头转向，既不知道自己在哪儿，也不知道该往哪儿走。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 有时得瞎折腾半天，才能找准方向。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不过我好像还从没失败过。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 能出什么岔子？
  *导演注释：calling across the water*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 更像是座阴森的神殿。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 天啊，我觉得好……孤单。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 接着走，别停。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 景色真美。这种日子我倒是过得惯。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这个嘛……我的确更直来直去！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 也更强壮。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我倒觉得镜子男挺好的。他有时候确实不太说话。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这有什么不好？我们就有个队友，从来没说过一句话。
  *导演注释：To self.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 下次见到镜子男，我就告诉他， 我很欣赏他做的事，也很佩服他的见识。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 反正我是不介意偶尔听听这种夸奖的。
  *导演注释：To self.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 尤其还是姑娘对我这么说……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我觉得你不该对镜子男那么苛刻。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 就是……他把这些镜子带来给我们解难题用。 而且他很懂这些镜子。
  *导演注释：Not really a genuine question, more like, "I don't want to talk about this" or "I don't care."*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哦。说得也是。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 听说你需要帮忙！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我就……直接进来了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 怎么，还会有什么麻烦吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这招你已经练得很熟练了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们身受剑缚的人，必须团结！

### `hw_first` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I can't believe this is what water is like when it all just ... stays together.

### `hw_second` （无官方中译）

- **EN** What would happen if I ... fall into that stuff?

### `hw_warrior_thief` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Will the water stay stuck together when we get it back home?
- **EN** I don't want to be standing next to a cartload of ... exploding water pellets.

### `hw_warrior_thief_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Why exactly would people even buy this water?

### `hw_warrior_goblins` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I wonder how the goblins feel about all this weird water.

### `hw_warrior_surrounded` （无官方中译）

- **EN** A story to tell my grandkids. If I make it out of here ...
- **EN** ... and find a nice girl ... ... and we have kids ...
  *导演注释：To self.*
- **EN** ... who then have kids ...
  *导演注释：To self.*

### `hw_warrior_going` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Izzy said there's a chance the exit was in this direction.
- **EN** I'll keep going... But perhaps he's having better luck.

### `hw_warrior_thief_dragons` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Dragon friends?

### `sp_warrior_takes_wand` （无官方中译）

- **EN** The way he described this thing, it sounded fun to use...

### `sp_warrior_shunt` （无官方中译）

- **EN** So many rocks! Finally, a good workout.

### `sp_warrior_green_beam_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** You know, I could get used to this.

### `hp_torus` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Can't say I understood. He said, it's a place where the air, the open space, is not that big.
- **EN** Like the whole of everything is akin to a small room. He said, it turns around to itself like a doughnut.
- **EN** We all like doughnuts.

### `hp_torus_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Hmmm. This space is not that big... akin to a small room...
- **EN** Doughnut, though... I don't see it!
  *导演注释：To self. Evaluating whether it's what Izzy is looking for.*

### `hp_crystals_not_stones` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Creepy in here. I'll just get to work to keep my mind off...

### `hp_stones_not_crystals` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Eerily familiar.

### `hs_warrior_sailor_0` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Almost to the meeting point. I hope that girl's okay.

### `hs_warrior_sailor_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** So, your best friend is a 'boat'.
- **EN** But person-sized.

### `hs_warrior_sailor_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** How is a 'boat' alive?
- **EN** Such as?
- **EN** We don't have those, but ... where I come from, vehicles are steered by people.
- **EN** You couldn't steer the 'boat' yourself?
  *导演注释：The only vehicles they have travel on land.*

### `hs_warrior_sailor_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** So *before* he gets put into the 'boat', the Guild makes a deal with him?
- **EN** And he's a ghost. So your Guild can talk to ghosts?
- **EN** How do they talk to the ghosts? ... Spirits?

### `hs_warrior_sailor_4` （无官方中译）

- **EN** The what?

### `obf_many_drop_mid` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Oh my God.

### `vs_warrior_dragons` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Well. Dragons, I need what you've got.

### `spin_doctors` （无官方中译）

- **EN** How'd you get here?
  *导演注释：This is an old acquaintance of Warrior who taught him how to use his Swordbinding to channel extra strength.*
- **EN** We're going to have to talk about that. But first let's get this done.
  *导演注释：after a beat*
- **EN** Heard you needed some help!
- **EN** I just ... came in!
- **EN** Was there supposed to be a problem?

### `spin_doctors_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** It's been indispensable. Deep thanks for the lessons!
  *导演注释：This is the technique he taught Warrior that lets him push a huge amount of stuff. It involves redirecting your personal energy through the Swordbound restriction so that it gets squeezed (but not cut off) and the squeezing amplifies the force.*
- **EN** You've gotten good at the technique!
- **EN** We Swordbound must stand together!

### `rotator` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Lately I've been getting *so* confused. I don't know where I am, I don't know which direction to go.
- **EN** Sometimes it takes a lot of faffing about before I find a good direction.
- **EN** But I suppose I haven't *failed* yet.

### `spray_and_pray` （无官方中译）

- **EN** What could possibly go wrong?
  *导演注释：calling across the water*

### `spin_heroes` （无官方中译）

- **EN** It's more like a creepy temple.

### `warrior_lonely` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Oh God, I feel so ... lonely.

### `warrior_lonely_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Just keep it moving, keep it moving.

### `beautiful_view_warrior` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Beautiful view. I could get used to this.

### `more_than_mirror_guy` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Well... I certainly am more straightforward!
- **EN** And stronger.

### `vm_warrior_mirror_guy_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I don't know, I like the mirror guy fine. I mean yeah, he's quiet sometimes.
- **EN** Is that so wrong? One of *our* teammates has never said a word.
  *导演注释：To self.*

### `vm_warrior_mirror_guy_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Next time I see him, I'll just tell mirror guy I appreciate his good work. And his insight.
- **EN** I know *I* wouldn't mind hearing something like that, once in a while.
  *导演注释：To self.*

### `vm_warrior_mirror_guy_2_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Especially from one of the ladies...

### `vm_warrior_mirror_guy_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I don't think you should be so hard on mirror guy.
- **EN** Just ... he brought us these mirrors to solve stuff with. And he understands them very well.
  *导演注释：Not really a genuine question, more like, "I don't want to talk about this" or "I don't care."*

### `audiolog_di_tiger_lily_v2`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 没关系，我们都会这样。
  *导演注释：This is a surreal situation where the Heroes are scrambled relative to where they are supposed to be.*

### `end3_heroes_first` （无官方中译）

- **EN** It's okay. We all do.
