<!-- ================= 07_thief_tif ================= -->

# 盗贼 Tif

> Heroes 三主角之一。证据：warrior_heroes1_call "Tif! Isidore!"；被称 "Izzy" 的是 Isidore。配音：Sabrina。

**统计**：台词块 249 · 英文词数 1730 · 涉及段落 103 · 官方中译覆盖 176/249

---


### `thief_first`

- **EN** Isidore! Valens! Seems I'm on my own for now.
  **CN** 伊西多尔！瓦伦斯！看来暂时只剩我一个了。
- **EN** This place is a fair bit scarier without friends.
  **CN** 没有同伴，这地方可吓人多了。

### `heroes1_5`

- **EN** Sometimes I feel like I'm being watched. It's creepy, but,
  **CN** 有时总觉得有人在看着我。怪瘆人的，但
- **EN** maybe a little nice? Someone cares about me!
  **CN** 好像也不赖？说明有人在意我嘛！
- **EN** Someone creepy cares about me...
  **CN** 一个瘆人的家伙在关心我……

### `heroes_tif_so_much_space`

- **EN** So many odd-shapen courts; The architect's mind must have been a maze. My sort of fellow!
  **CN** 这么多奇形怪状的院子，设计者脑子一定跟迷宫似的。 倒是对我胃口！

### `heroes_tif_lone_crystal`

- **EN** Come along, crystal friend, we've somewhere you need to go.
  **CN** 走吧，水晶朋友，该带你去个地方了。

### `heroes_tif_return`

- **EN** We'll be back for you. Don't worry.
  **CN** 我们会回来接你的。别担心。

### `heroes1_thief_three_crystals_end`

- **EN** Whoever you are, hold tight.
  **CN** 不管你是谁，再坚持一下。
- **EN** We'll pull you out of this.
  **CN** 我们会把你救出来的。

### `heroes1_warrior_thief`

- **EN** Of course, of course, shush, we've got to catch up to the Magistrate! Where's Izzy?
  **CN** 没事没事，我们得赶紧追上执政官！伊兹呢？
- **EN** Well, let's find him. Which other way?
  **CN** 那我们去找他。另一边是哪边？

### `heroes1_warrior_thief_mid0`

- **EN** I know where Magistrate Reynard is headed. We've got to catch up.
  **CN** 我知道雷纳德执政官要去哪儿。我们得追上去。

### `heroes1_warrior_thief_mid`

- **EN** We just need to keep headed East.
  **CN** 只要一路向东就行。
- **EN** When he locked us up, he'd already donned his dustcoat. For *travel*. And, about his neck...
  **CN** 他把我们关起来时，就已经穿上了那件风衣 ，一副要出远门的打扮。而且他脖子上……
- **EN** Had your eyes been open, you'd have seen a distinctive, *recognizable* leather cord, three-strand plaited, oiled and blackened...
  **CN** 你眼睛要是睁着，就能看到那根很特别、一眼就能认出的皮绳： 三股编成，上过油，处理成了黑色……
- **EN** The Crawler.
  **CN** “爬行者号”。
  *导演注释：Seeing what she's getting at.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 走，去收拾那个……其实吧，我觉得他不蠢！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 他也有他的魅力嘛！

### `heroes1_warrior_thief_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Let's go get that ... I mean, he's not stupid though!
- **EN** He's got his charms!

### `heroes1_wizard_thief`

- **EN** I told him we need to get out of this ... echoey lonely place, then head to the retrenchment.
  **CN** 我跟他说了，我们得离开这个…… 空空荡荡的鬼地方，然后去驿堡。
- **EN** The passengers' retrenchment. For the Crawler.
  **CN** 旅客驿堡啊。坐“爬行者号”的地方。
- **EN** *If* we get there before it leaves, we can stop him.
  **CN** 要是在它离开之前赶到，我们就能拦住他。

### `heroes_three_for_the_first_time_optional`

- **EN** Well, you see, the Magistrate has been up to something.
  **CN** 这个嘛……你们也看见了，执政官一直在搞事情。
  *导演注释："Open the bag" is a colloqualism, like "spill the beans".*
- **EN** It's ... somewhat disturbing.
  **CN** 这……多少有点让人不安。

### `heroes_three_for_the_first_time_optional_end`

- **EN** It's a bit like that.
  **CN** 差不多吧。
  *导演注释：Having a realization and a leap of deduction*

### `heroes_three_for_the_first_time_mandatory`

- **EN** Sometimes things just follow me home. Without me doing *anything*. You know how it is.
  **CN** 有时候吧，东西自己就跟着我回家了。 我可什么都没干。你懂的。

### `heroes_three_for_the_first_time_mandatory_end`

- **EN** Well, yeah, in a case... how did you ... hmmm. It was a fine leather-clad case, with satisfyingly grippable handles.
  **CN** 啊对，是装在箱子里……你怎么知道…… 呃，是个精致的皮箱，提手拎起来特别顺手。
- **EN** I'd thought it might be full of shiny things, but it only held papers and notes.
  **CN** 我还以为里面装满亮闪闪的好东西， 结果只有文件和笔记。

### `heroes_three_for_the_second_time_mandatory`

- **EN** At first I was disappointed. But then I realized,
  **CN** 起初我很失望。但随后我意识到，
- **EN** little notes tell you a lot about people, you know? Reading between the lines. So I took a little peek.
  **CN** 小小几张笔记，最能看出一个人的底细。 你知道吗？字里行间，全是门道。 于是我就偷偷瞄了一眼。
  *导演注释：(picking up enthusiasm because even though notes are not gold, they have juicy gossip etc)*
- **EN** We should have never taken this job, or maybe we should have -- as we did -- because if we didn't,
  **CN** 这份活儿我们本来就不该接，可或许又该接…… 毕竟接都接了……因为要是没接的话，
- **EN** we wouldn't know what we know, which is, we have to stop him... as soon as possible.
  **CN** 我们就不会知道这些事，也不会知道我们必须阻止他…… 而且要尽快。

### `heroes_three_for_the_second_time_mandatory_end`

- **EN** These crystals. We need to talk about ... how they're made.
  **CN** 我们得谈谈……这些水晶是怎么造出来的。

### `heroes_jail`

- **EN** Traps everywhere. I really shouldn't be enjoying this.
  **CN** 到处都是陷阱。我真不该觉得有意思。

### `heroes_jail2`

- **EN** ... and tempted Providence! No bars this time!
  **CN** ……谁让你乱说话的！这次连栅栏都没有！
- **EN** You!
  **CN** 你！
  *导演注释：He is confused -- he *did* do research on how to keep gravy warm on the table, with MODERATE SUCCESS mind you ... but from the tone he knows this is supposed to be an insult. Flustered about how to reply.*

### `heroes_jail2_mid`

- **EN** The Caravan, the Royal Caravan?
  **CN** 商队，皇家商队？
- **EN** Valens, can you get us in with them?
  **CN** 瓦伦斯，你能帮我们混进商队吗？

### `heroes_jail2_mid2`

- **EN** Ooh. Which he may really be!
  **CN** 嚯！搞不好他真这么想！
- **EN** If I were the King, I'd send the Caravan immediately!
  **CN** 我要是国王，肯定马上派皇家商队出发！
- **EN** To get him!!
  **CN** 去抓他！！

### `heroes_jail2_end`

- **EN** The Caravan! I bet we'll meet all kinds of new friends.
  **CN** 皇家商队！我们肯定能认识好多新朋友。
- **EN** Save the arguing for the King! Or the ... Lord Chamberlain, or whoever we can get to!
  **CN** 等见了国王再吵！或者……宫务大臣， 反正见到谁就找谁！
  *导演注释：Upset, but flustered enough he has no idea what to say.*

### `heroes2_thief_goblins`

- **EN** Hello there, pals!
  **CN** 你们好呀，小伙伴们！
- **EN** We could all just get along nicely, couldn't we ... ?
  **CN** 我们大家完全可以和和气气地相处，对吧……？
  *导演注释：She finds herself among many dangerous goblins.*

### `heroes2_thief_goblins_mid`

- **EN** Those guards boasted that no foe could come within a day's march without being met in force.
  **CN** 那些卫兵曾夸口说，没有哪个敌人能在一天行程之内接近， 而不被大军迎头痛击。
- **EN** So how'd these Goblins get here? And why aren't they being repelled?
  **CN** 那这些哥布林怎么跑到这来了？ 怎么就没人把它们击退呢？
  *导演注释：Thinking to herself*

### `heroes2_thief_dragons_intro`

- **EN** Hey there boy-os, I don't suppose anyone ever keeps you as pets?
  **CN** 嗨，小家伙们，你们难道从来没被人当宠物养过？
- **EN** You could do all kinds of things! Warm the house, roast supper...
  **CN** 能做的事情可多着呢！给屋子取暖、烤晚饭……
  *导演注释：Here she is talking about the human-sized fire-breathing-dragons that she is encountering in person for the first time.*

### `heroes2_thief_dragons_intro_2`

- **EN** keep the bath hot *well* into the night...
  **CN** 让洗澡水一直热到深夜……

### `heroes2_thief_dragons_intro_3`

- **EN** dry the wash in half the time...
  **CN** 快速晾干衣服……

### `heroes2_thief_dragons_intro_4`

- **EN** warm my slippers...
  **CN** 暖暖我的拖鞋……

### `heroes2_thief_dragons_intro_5`

- **EN** scorch the weeds out between the paving stones...
  **CN** 把铺路石缝里的杂草烧个干净……

### `heroes2_thief_dragons_intro_6`

- **EN** And if anyone comes collecting debts... ... ...
  **CN** 要是有人上门讨债……

### `heroes2_thief_dragons_second`

- **EN** Hmm, a crystal. It's fresh.
  **CN** 嗯，一块水晶。还是新鲜的。
- **EN** Magistrate Reynard has been here, and not long ago.
  **CN** 雷纳德执政官来过这里，而且没走多久。
  *导演注释：To self.*

### `heroes2_thief_dragons_third`

- **EN** Another crystal. Following the trail...
  **CN** 又一块水晶。循着痕迹走……

### `heroes2_thief_dragons_third_end`

- **EN** Is that the crack of a teleport I hear? Izzy, is that you?
  **CN** 我是不是听见了传送的声响？伊兹，是你吗？

### `heroes2_three_first`

- **EN** He tried to stop us, but he couldn't!
  **CN** 他想拦住我们，可他拦不住！
- **EN** Such an attack has hardly ever happened in history --
  **CN** 这样的袭击在历史上几乎从未发生过——
- **EN** So *someone* had a legendarily strong motive. Perhaps that motive involves us!
  **CN** 所以，某个人一定有着极强的动机。 也许这动机跟我们有关！
  *导演注释：thinking, realizing*

### `heroes2_three_first_mid`

- **EN** Izzy, can't you see how they looked at him? They all wanted to be him, ... and they were glad not to be him.
  **CN** 伊兹，你没看见他们对他是什么态度吗？ 个个都想成为他……又庆幸自己不是他。

### `heroes2_three_first_end`

- **EN** I've got South!
  **CN** 南边交给我！

### `heroes2_postmeetup_thief`

- **EN** If I'd known that starting a moving company would lead to big adventures,
  **CN** 要是早知道开搬运公司会遇上这么多大冒险，
- **EN** I'd have talked them into it so much sooner!
  **CN** 我早就说服他们这么干了！
  *导演注释：To self.*

### `heroes2_one_fire_warrior_thief_optional`

- **EN** A dirk is small. You could sneak it through?
  **CN** 短剑那么小，不能蒙混过去吗？
- **EN** Well, use a club? Clubs aren't sharp.
  **CN** 那就用棍棒？棍棒又不锋利。
- **EN** Uhhhhh, punch 'em?
  **CN** 呃……那用拳头揍呢？

### `heroes2_one_fire_warrior_thief_optional_end`

- **EN** See, when we work together, you don't even need a club!
  **CN** 看吧，咱俩一配合，你连棍棒都不需要！

### `heroes2_thief_priestess_second`

- **EN** ... and that's when we decided to finally start the company.
  **CN** ……我们就是在那时候终于决定开公司。
- **EN** We're all good at moving things in our own ways,
  **CN** 我们在搬东西这方面都有各自的妙招，
  *导演注释：She is telling these stories to Priestess, who never says anything. Priestess has the habit of causing most people to self-psychologize and talk about their inner issues, but with Thief, the result is that she talks about her friends.*
- **EN** and we'd gained so much experience at loading docks and so forth.
  **CN** 而且我们在码头干了那么久，经验已经够丰富了。

### `heroes2_thief_priestess_crawler`

- **EN** Do you know the Crawler? Valens and I worked there for a while. At the far other end of the route, I mean...
  **CN** 你知道“爬行者号”吗？ 瓦伦斯和我在那儿干过一阵子。 我是说，在那条路线很远的另一头……
- **EN** Until I ... Well, it was a fun job while we had it.
  **CN** 直到我……嗯，那份活儿干着的时候还挺有意思的。
  *导演注释：The "I mean" is clarifying that she doesn't mean *this* end of the route, but the end that originates in the West.*

### `heroes2_thief_priestess_second_mid_warrior`

- **EN** Valens is incredibly strong. He said it's a trick, but the details are hard to talk about.
  **CN** 瓦伦斯力气大得吓人。他说这里面有诀窍，不过详情不方便说。
- **EN** Said he was taught by an older fellow, another soldier.
  **CN** 他说是一个年长的人教他的，也是个士兵。
- **EN** Another disgraced soldier.
  **CN** 另一个不光彩的士兵。

### `heroes2_thief_priestess_second_mid_disgraced`

- **EN** Don't tell him I said "disgraced", haha!
  **CN** 别告诉他我用了“不光彩”这词，哈哈！
- **EN** They have some official term for it. A neutral term.
  **CN** 他们有一套官方叫法。比较中性的那种。

### `heroes2_thief_priestess_second_mid_wizard`

- **EN** Izzy's swap powers are amazing. He got them in some kind of accident.
  **CN** 伊兹的换位能力特别厉害，好像是在某次意外中获得的。
- **EN** Great for moving heavy stuff, not so great for getting close to people.
  **CN** 搬重物很方便，但跟人亲近就不太方便了。

### `heroes2_thief_priestess_second_end`

- **EN** I'm glad we had this time to talk!
  **CN** 很高兴有机会跟你聊天！

### `heroes2_wizard_thief_priestess_goblins`

- **EN** A Guardsman told me they all saw incredible smoke coming from the mountains, last week.
  **CN** 有个卫兵告诉我，上周他们都看到山上冒出了浓得吓人的烟。
- **EN** Then how'd the dragons get here? They don't mind smoke.
  **CN** 那龙是怎么到这儿来的？它们可不怕烟。

### `heroes2_thief_wizard_priestess_compartments`

- **EN** You!
  **CN** 你！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这些哥布林根本不听命令。他是怎么让它们各就各位的？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哦！没准他手下就有这么一位！

### `heroes2_thief_wizard_priestess_compartments_mid` （无官方中译）

- **EN** These Goblins don't take orders. How's he getting them into all the right places?
- **EN** Oh! May be he has one in his service!

### `heroes2_four`

- **EN** I prefer to think of it as, we're closing in on him!
  **CN** 我更愿意说，我们离抓住他更近了！
  *导演注释：After a pause*
- **EN** Across the canyon, he'll be in a place where he has no friends,
  **CN** 过了峡谷，他可就没朋友能帮忙了，
- **EN** and his rank means nothing.
  **CN** 他的官衔也不管用了。
- **EN** We'll be diplomats! They'll spread the carpets for us. The *nice* carpets!
  **CN** 那我们就当外交官！他们还不得铺红毯迎接咱们！还得是好的红毯！

### `heroes3_bard_thief_first`

- **EN** So I found out he'd been crystalizing people,
  **CN** 所以后来我发现他一直在把人变成水晶，
- **EN** and he locked us in his dungeon. But we escaped, we told the King, the King deputized us, and now we're here.
  **CN** 他把我们关进了他的地牢，但我们逃了出去， 还把事情告诉了国王。 国王授权我们追捕他，于是我们就来了。
- **EN** Awww. Thank you. Your dress is really pretty.
  **CN** 哎呀，谢谢。你的裙子也特别漂亮。

### `heroes3_bard_thief_mid`

- **EN** Rich. Manicured... Theatrical.
  **CN** 有钱。打扮精致……爱演戏。
- **EN** Of the highest station! Great taste in clothes, really.
  **CN** 是最上流阶层穿的那种！衣服品味真是一流。

### `heroes3_bard_warrior_thief`

- **EN** Am I making you nervous, or is *she* making you nervous?
  **CN** 是我让你紧张，还是她让你紧张？
  *导演注释：Warrior is in a precarious spot where a misstep by Thief could kill him.*

### `heroes3_druid_thief_first`

- **EN** Indeed I am, friend!
  **CN** 正是，朋友！
  *导演注释：He is being a bit blunt here. To be polite he should say something like, "Forgive my presumption, but you look to be from the West," but he is just very concerned with tracking down Magistrate Reynard and suspects that seeing other Westerners is not a coincidence.*
- **EN** Pleasure or Business?
  **CN** 去游玩还是办事？

### `heroes3_druid_thief_crystal`

- **EN** Soooo, I suspect I know who you're looking for.
  **CN** 那……我猜我知道你在找谁。
- **EN** We're after him too.
  **CN** 我们也在追他。

### `heroes3_druid_thief_first_end`

- **EN** Come, I've some friends you should meet.
  **CN** 来吧，有几位朋友该介绍给你认识。

### `heroes3_bard_wizard_druid_pondering`

- **EN** He's taken two 'special transports' already! But you're right; this one seemed more ... final.
  **CN** 他都用过两种“特别的法子”了！ 不过你说得对，这回他倒像是……不打算回来了。

### `heroes3_six`

- **EN** Hey! Finally we're all together, at once!
  **CN** 嘿！大家总算都聚齐了！

### `heroes3_victory_lap`

- **EN** Could we become lost out there, and never get back?
  **CN** 我们会不会迷失在那片地方，再也回不来？
- **EN** He called us ... Alligators? What's an Alligator?
  **CN** 他叫我们……小鳄鱼？鳄鱼是什么？

### `heroes3_victory_lap_end`

- **EN** But will it be a paean or a dirge?
  **CN** 可会是颂歌，还是挽歌？

### `skipping_stones_treasures_4`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 你有没有觉得哪里不对劲？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我觉得……也许……没有？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我就说这里不对劲。我说了吧！！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你们怎么把这么多水都聚到一起的？
  *导演注释：To self and Thief, who is next to him.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你觉得我们还会见到她吗？
  *导演注释：Introducing herself.*

### `worlds_collide_HS_on_switch_to_thief` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Do you feel something strange?
- **EN** I think ... Maybe ... not?

### `worlds_collide_HS_gates_opened_proximity` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I told you this was strange. I told you!!
- **EN** How'd you put all that water in one place?
  *导演注释：To self and Thief, who is next to him.*

### `worlds_collide_HS_end_of_level` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Think we'll see her again?
  *导演注释：Introducing herself.*

### `queen_og_ask`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 我跟你说，咱们弄一辆特别特别大的车，装上一堆葫芦，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 把葫芦全灌满这种“水”，再把整车都运回家，我们就发财了。
  *导演注释：There are no big bodies of water in their world, but there are plenty here; now, surrounded by water, Tif has an idea.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 嗯……唔……要是我们有一根很长的绳子……？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那些有钱的闲人总爱买 远方的稀奇玩意儿。而这件是最稀奇的了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 异域、神秘……也许还很危险…… 它自己就能把自己卖出去！
  *导演注释："the idle rich" means a certain set of wealthy people.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 所以，这些水怎么会聚在一起不散？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 嘿——龙朋友们！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 它们让我想起家乡了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 有人把这亮闪闪的东西丢下不管了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这么多水晶！ 总算有机会来一场真正的锻炼了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 走吧，机器朋友，咱俩一起锻炼。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我寻思着你有没有……机器的肌肉……
  *导演注释：The "contraption" is a weird automated mine cart thing from another world.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这些光束让我浑身酥麻。 就好像我能撬开世上任何一把锁。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你认识了不少有趣的人吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 看来我们来得太晚，没赶上这里最美好的时候。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 就这么……没了……我差点都要同情那些哥布林了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我说了，是“差点”！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不过问题是：他们是先毁了这座城镇，再把它搬到这里来，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 还是说，这里只是照原样一木一梁仿造、专门用来烧的复制品…… 而原来的镇子毫发无损？
  *导演注释：She's thinking through this a little, as transporting an entire town does not really make sense. As brought up in the following conversation as well,*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 要是我刚才失礼了，对不起。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可是你今天……不太像平时的你。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 象征什么？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 像什么？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我没完全听懂，但我会陪着你。咱们继续走吧。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 很高兴我们能一起度过这么多时光。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 话说，伊兹说他在找什么来着？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 像山洞那么大？还是一间小屋？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我喜欢甜甜圈！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 而且在这种弯弯绕绕的小房间里，我特别得心应手……！ 要是刚吃过一个甜甜圈，那就更没得说啦！
  *导演注释：Eye-roll*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这里怎么看都不像甜甜圈。兴许瓦伦斯那边运气好些。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 谢谢你，新朋友！不过……拉东西太复杂了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 推要更直接， 而且你不会被团团围住、动弹不得。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你能让石头嗖一下冲出去，这招真是绝了！真希望我也能做到。
  *导演注释：With "surrounded and stuck" she is thinking of boxing herself into a corner when pulling to omany things.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 德鲁伊说艾娅被关在那座哥布林旧要塞里，却坚称自己什么也做不了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 他居然做不了？？他可是唯一一个能把那帮混蛋变成石头的人啊！
  *导演注释：To self. Aiah is the Priestess' name, but nobody else knows this but Thief. We don't ever see how she came to know it... but somehow because she is very good at making friends, she knows more about Priestess than anyone else.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 别担心，艾娅，我一定会找到你。 只是路上挡着这么一大片水……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 大水啊，你是我的敌人，也是我的朋友。 是我的敌人，因为显而易见，你总在挡我的路。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 是我的朋友，因为你看起来、感觉起来都那么平静。 在动荡的岁月里，我感受得到你的安宁。 它支撑着我继续走下去。
  *导演注释：Speaking to the water, personifying it.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 艾娅，老实说，你渡水渡得那么轻松，我还有点眼红。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 换成我就费劲多了。
  *导演注释：To self; thinking of Aiah as though she were speaking to her.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 对我来说可太费劲了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 但当我看到你轻飘飘地越过去…… 我就受到了鼓舞，也更加庆幸 能成为你的朋友。
  *导演注释：To self.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 快到了。已经到废墟外围了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不知道这栋建筑原来是做什么的。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 艾娅，一直以来都是你在保护我。 谁能预料到，有一天会轮到我来救你？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 真是做梦也想不到！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 好，撑住！我来救你出去！
  *导演注释：To self. Upon seeing the Priestess suspended high in the air in an unlikely manner, surrounded by a bunch of goblins. Startled.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们还没完全脱险……不过现在只要过了这座桥就行！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 别急着动手！
  *导演注释：To self. She is in a position to shut off the beams holding stuff up, which could easily kill some friends if not done exactly right.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 千里迢迢跑来，结果又进了一座阴森的城堡。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 管它城堡还是神殿，快带我出去。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 景色真美。真希望有个朋友陪我一起看。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那么，你来的地方， 没有这样的水晶吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可你们有宝石啊。宝石不算水晶吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我只是喜欢它们闪闪发亮的样子。宝石会发光，水晶也会发光。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 彼此不同，却各有各的美。

# 法师 Isidore

> Heroes 三主角之一，"肉汁法师"梗当事人。配音：Darin De Paul。

**统计**：台词块 210 · 英文词数 1635 · 涉及段落 71 · 官方中译覆盖 160/210

---


### `heroes1_thief_three_crystals_end`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 到现在还没想明白我是怎么出来的……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 像是有某种力量从远处插手了……
  *导演注释：(to himself)*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 先去找其他人。

### `heroes1_wizard_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Still don't understand how I got out of there --
- **EN** some kind of intervention from afar --
  *导演注释：(to himself)*
- **EN** First, I'll find the others.

### `heroes1_wizard_2`

- **EN** At least the Magistrate went crazy before the job --
  **CN** 好在执政官是在开工前发的疯……
- **EN** If we'd moved all these crystals out, I'd have naught to work with now.
  **CN** 要是我们真把这些水晶全搬走了， 现在可就束手无策了。
  *导演注释：(to himself)*

### `heroes1_wizard_2_end`

- **EN** Tif! Tif, where are you?
  **CN** 蒂芙！蒂芙，你在哪儿？

### `heroes1_wizard_three_crystals`

- **EN** Tif and Valens can't be far. I was imprisoned but a few hours --
  **CN** 蒂芙和瓦伦斯应该不远。我才被关了几个小时……
- **EN** I'll find the Magistrate -- he'll explain himself.
  **CN** 我先找执政官吧。他必须给个解释。
  *导演注释：(to himself)*

### `heroes1_wizard_three_crystals_end`

- **EN** That Magistrate. We were courteous and professional, and he was so incredibly rude.
  **CN** 那执政官真是的，我们既彬彬有礼又尽显专业， 他倒好，粗鲁至极。

### `heroes1_wizard_array_end`

- **EN** Strangely cut, they felt lifelike, almost ... possessed.
  **CN** 切割的方式很奇特，看着活灵活现的，可以说……灵魂附身一般。

### `heroes1_warrior_wizard`

- **EN** Valens! Finally!
  **CN** 瓦伦斯！总算找到你了！
- **EN** I am glad you came along; it seems I can't get out my way.
  **CN** 你来得正好，我怎么也找不到出路。
- **EN** We'll figure it out.
  **CN** 总会有办法的。

- **EN** The where?
  **CN** 去哪儿？

### `heroes1_warrior_wizard_confused`

  **CN** 我们只是按约定，告诉他这次搬运大概要多少钱……
- **EN** and slammo, he jails us. I still don't understand.
  **CN** 他砰的一下，就把我们关了。我还是想不通。
- **EN** Bit of an overreaction...
  **CN** 这反应有点过了……

### `heroes1_warrior_wizard_confused_mid`

- **EN** Come to think ... did she look a bit sheepish to you?
  **CN** 现在想想……她当时是不是有点心虚？
- **EN** A dog.
  **CN** 狗。
- **EN** A dog who dragged your big juicy leg of mutton off the table the moment you turned your back!
  **CN** 一只趁你一转身， 就把你那根肥美多汁的羊腿从桌上拖走的狗！
  *导演注释：He likes dogs; he is thinking of a faithful dog who's your best friend, and this is just the wrong picture.*

### `heroes_three_for_the_first_time_optional`

- **EN** That could have been a misunderstanding.
  **CN** 那可能只是一场误会。

- **EN** He locked us up ... because of you?
  **CN** 他把我们关起来……是因为你？

### `heroes_three_for_the_first_time_mandatory`

- **EN** You stole from him?
  **CN** 你偷他东西了？

- **EN** And?
  **CN** 然后呢？

  **CN** 阻止他做什么？

- **EN** Don't worry. Bars are no hard matter for me at all!
  **CN** 别担心。区区栅栏，根本难不倒我！

### `heroes_jail2`

- **EN** You -- !
  **CN** 你……！
- **EN** + magistrate_disappear Wait! You still haven't paid us for the appraisal!
  **CN** + magistrate_disappear 等等！你还没付我们的估价费呢！

### `heroes_jail2_mid`

- **EN** He's high-enough ranked, he can command the Crawler to depart.
  **CN** 他的级别够高，能命令“爬行者号”启程。
- **EN** We're not likely to catch him.
  **CN** 我们恐怕追不上他。
- **EN** Oh ... hmm?
  **CN** 哦……嗯？
- **EN** Even if ... first day of the season isn't for two weeks yet.
  **CN** 可就算能混进去……皇家商队还要两周才会开始本季行程。

### `heroes_jail2_mid2`

- **EN** If the King knew his head Magistrate's exceeding his charge,
  **CN** 可要是国王知道，他手下的首席执政官竟敢越权行事，
- **EN** crystalizing lesser nobles without rhyme nor reason...
  **CN** 平白无故把小贵族们变成水晶……
- **EN** he might consider it an emergency.
  **CN** 说不定会把这事当成紧急事件。

### `heroes_jail2_end`

- **EN** After being stuck in this place, I'll enjoy travelling in luxury...
  **CN** 在这鬼地方困了这么久，享受享受奢华的旅行也不错……
- **EN** Don't you -- !
  **CN** 你还敢……！
- **EN** Let's go.
  **CN** 走吧。

### `heroes2_wizard_goblins`

- **EN** I'd have thought the caravan's defenses would hold for longer, but,
  **CN** 我还以为商队的防线能多撑一阵呢，但
- **EN** I'm glad to still be alive, hale body, hale mind.
  **CN** 能活下来就好，身体康健，头脑也清醒。

### `heroes2_wizard_goblins_mid`

- **EN** Don't know why they didn't like the word "hale".
  **CN** 真不明白他们为什么不喜欢“康健”这个词。
- **EN** "Hale Heroes of Hauling" sounds upscale and smart.
  **CN** “康健英雄搬运社”这名字听着多上档次。

### `heroes2_three_first`

- **EN** Together again!
  **CN** 我们又会合了！

### `heroes2_three_first_mid`

- **EN** I'm surprised you weren't praying for an ambush.
  **CN** 我还以为你会巴不得有人来偷袭我们呢。
- **EN** Day after day of close-quartered travel, Guardsmen all around,
  **CN** 日复一日挤在一起赶路，四周都是卫兵，
- **EN** not much to occupy them but gossip in quiet whispers; well, *I* felt awkward for you.
  **CN** 他们除了压低嗓子嚼舌根也没什么可做的； 唉，我都替你感到不自在。

### `heroes2_three_first_end`

- **EN** I'll check... the other ways.
  **CN** 我去看看……其他方向。

### `heroes2_postmeetup_wizard`

- **EN** Had I known that starting a moving company would lead me into the East,
  **CN** 要是早知道开搬运公司会把我带到东方来，
- **EN** surrounded by foul creatures, nary a book brought with me,
  **CN** 被这些恶心的怪物包围，身边一本书都没带，
  *导演注释：Todo: Maybe this whole sequence is really not cute at all because this is how all private guards are in the USA anyway, so it's just, like, documenting reality.*
- **EN** I'd have started some company about being sedentary, instead.
  **CN** 我宁可开一家坐着不用动的公司。
- **EN** Like ... Guards. But, guards that never have any trouble.
  **CN** 比如说……保安公司。 不过是永远不会遇上事的那种保安。
- **EN** Casual guards, for buildings where nobody goes,
  **CN** 悠闲的保安，在没人去的建筑物里，
- **EN** small, no walking, we just sit there.
  **CN** 地方小，不用巡逻，就往那儿一坐。
- **EN** Warming our feet by the fire.
  **CN** 在炉火旁烤烤脚。

### `heroes2_postmeetup_wizard_mid`

- **EN** Plenty of fires round here, I suppose. Not the sort I prefer, though.
  **CN** 这附近倒是不缺火。不过不是我想要的那种就是了。

### `heroes2_wizard_per_turn_goblins`

- **EN** Let's think about this.
  **CN** 我们来梳理一下。
- **EN** Magistrate Reynard flees to the East, and about the time he gets here, it's overrun by monsters.
  **CN** 雷纳德执政官往东逃，他前脚刚到， 这里后脚就被怪物攻陷了。
  *导演注释：In this section, Wizard is thinking to himself.*
- **EN** Possibility the First: It's a coincidence. Always an option, but let's not jump straight to that.
  **CN** 第一种可能： 纯属巧合。这当然有可能，但先别急着下结论。
- **EN** Possibility the Second: He came East because this monster invasion was about to happen, and he knew.
  **CN** 第二种可能： 他事先知道这里即将遭到怪物入侵，所以才往东边来。
- **EN** And he wants to stop it? Or, the opposite: he wants to take advantage in some way? Perhaps ... to what end?
  **CN** 他是想阻止这场入侵？还是正好相反，想趁机捞到什么好处？ 也许吧……可他到底图什么？
- **EN** Possibility the Third: He caused all this. He is sowing pandemonium *intentionally*.
  **CN** 第三种可能： 这一切都是他一手造成的。他是在故意制造混乱。
- **EN** For what reason? Merely to throw us off his trail? That's a lot of chaos just for us.
  **CN** 为了什么？只是想甩掉我们？ 就冲我们几个，未免也太兴师动众了吧。

### `heroes2_wizard_per_turn_goblins_end`

- **EN** Thanks for helping me out, fella! And thank your friends for helping me think.
  **CN** 小伙子，谢谢你帮我解围！ 也替我谢谢你的朋友，让我理清了思路。

### `heroes2_wizard_priestess_first`

- **EN** Why hello there! We seem to be in a spot of trouble.
  **CN** 你好呀！我们好像遇到了点麻烦。
- **EN** Normally, goblins are no problem for me, no problem at all, but, this passageway is so narrow...
  **CN** 搁平时，哥布林根本挡不了我的路， 完全不是什么问题，可这条通道实在太窄了……

### `heroes2_wizard_priestess_mid`

- **EN** Miraculous.
  **CN** 太神奇了。

### `heroes2_wizard_priestess_first_end`

- **EN** Thank you, good ... miss?
  **CN** 谢谢你，这位好心……小姐？

### `heroes2_wizard_and_priestess_alone_first`

- **EN** Yes, we all fumble, we all trip, but have you ever noticed
  **CN** 是啊，我们都会笨手笨脚、都会失手， 可你有没有注意到，
- **EN** that sometimes, we're clumsy in ways that are downright suspicious?
  **CN** 有时候，我们笨拙得过于可疑？
- **EN** An example. You're holding a tray of carefully-arranged reagents, and, just as you've done a thousand times,
  **CN** 举个例子。你端着一个摆得整整齐齐的试剂托盘， 动作跟你做过上千次的一模一样，
- **EN** you're turning toward the workbench to tip some powders into a tincture --
  **CN** 正转身朝向工作台，想把几种粉末倒进药剂里……
- **EN** the tray clatters to the floor.
  **CN** 托盘却哐当一声掉地上了。
- **EN** Oh, that'll take hours to re-grind.
  **CN** 唉，重新研磨得花好几个小时。
- **EN** How hard could it have been just to keep the tray pinched firmly between thumb and fingers? Not hard at all, *obviously*. I do it all the time.
  **CN** 只用拇指和手指稳稳捏住托盘，能有多难？ 显然一点都不难。我天天都在做。
- **EN** Suspicious.
  **CN** 可疑。
- **EN** Did some hidden part of me *want* to make a mess?
  **CN** 是不是我内心某个隐藏的部分，就偏偏想捣乱？
  *导演注释：The "suspicious" is long and drawn out, perhaps, as if looking for clues while saying it.*
- **EN** Or, have you ever -- the candles flickering romantically, the warmth of the air just so --
  **CN** 或者，你有没有遇到过…… 烛光浪漫地摇曳，空气的温度也刚刚好……
  *导演注释：Todo: Some more explanation here; *want* to make a mess why? To avoid an appointment, to not take the medicine I know I'm supposed to take?*
- **EN** Well you didn't have to use the word "donkey", even if the sheen of her hairy, hairy legs --
  **CN** 好吧，就算她那双毛茸茸的腿泛着光芒， 你也没必要说出“驴”这个字……
- **EN** *nice* hair, I mean, soft and lustrous --
  **CN** 我是说，那毛长得挺好，柔软又有光泽……
  *导演注释：In "hairy, hairy" he is re-envisioning and thinking fondly of this girl.*
- **EN** "donkey", clumsy word, it didn't have to happen! It was like dropping the reagents, just like that.
  **CN** “驴”……用词不过脑子，本可以不说的！ 就像手一滑打翻试剂，脑一抽就说出口了。
  *导演注释：Clarifying to Priestess that he really is being positive.*

### `heroes2_wizard_and_priestess_alone_second`

- **EN** I just wonder if there's some mind behind the mind,
  **CN** 我在想：我们的意识背后，会不会还藏着另一个意识？
- **EN** that uses such fumbles to redirect our lives, by injecting drama we think we don't want;
  **CN** 它借这些失手，给生活添上我们自以为不想经历的波折， 从而改变我们的人生走向；
  *导演注释：When he tells this story, he's thinking of the accident that he outlines in the alone_third speech.*
- **EN** that hides itself away, cloaked in the guise of clumsiness.
  **CN** 它把自己藏起来，让我们以为只是自己笨手笨脚。
- **EN** A background mind that creates misadventures just to steer us where it wants us.
  **CN** 它潜藏在暗处，制造种种波折， 只为把我们引向它想让我们去的地方。

### `heroes2_wizard_and_priestess_alone_third`

- **EN** I was so careful, for so long. I was careful, I was careful, I was careful, I was careful, then ...
  **CN** 这么多年，我一直小心翼翼，行事小心再小心， 可后来……
- **EN** One day I woke up at 3 in the morning, full of energy, yearning to just do it all.
  **CN** 某天凌晨三点，我突然醒了，浑身是劲， 只想一口气把一切做完。
- **EN** *Enough* careful, years of being careful are making me old! It's time to just leap ahead.
  **CN** 我够小心了，小心了这么多年，人都要老了！ 是时候放手一搏。
- **EN** I was working feverishly, half-asleep, blearily...
  **CN** 我整个人都迷迷糊糊，却又发了疯似的工作……
- **EN** The stone shimmering with power, vibrations through my fingers, coursing up my arm.
  **CN** 那块石头闪烁着力量的光芒， 震颤顺着我的手指传上来，一路涌上手臂。
- **EN** No surprise, -- a mistake. and, I dropped it --
  **CN** 结果毫不意外地……出了意外。然后我把它摔了……
- **EN** a flash, a report, tiny fragments shooting out, piercing --
  **CN** 一道闪光，一声爆响，细小的碎片四散飞射，刺入——
- **EN** and thus you see me today.
  **CN** 于是你就看到了今天的我。
  *导演注释："a report" meaning a loud bang-like sound*
- **EN** Numerous and tiny, there's no removing them.
  **CN** 碎片又多又小，根本没法取出来。
  *导演注释："And that is why I am how I am today."*

### `heroes2_wizard_and_priestess_alone_third_end`

- **EN** So it was an accident; but many a fool would love to do what I can do,
  **CN** 所以那是一场意外，可又有多少蠢人巴不得有我这样的本事。
- **EN** so perhaps it was no accident at all. May be I wrecked my life intentionally, a mind behind my mind.
  **CN** 所以，也许那根本不是意外。 也许我是有意毁掉自己的人生， 我内心深处还藏着另一个在背后操控我的念头。

### `heroes2_wizard_thief_priestess_goblins`

- **EN** There was a fire in the goblin caves? A huge accident? Or, -- maybe someone smoked them out intentionally...
  **CN** 哥布林洞穴着火了？一场大事故？还是…… 有人故意放烟把它们熏出来……
  *导演注释："incredible amounts of smoke"*
- **EN** Maybe the dragons made the smoke...
  **CN** 也许烟就是龙弄出来的……

- **EN** + magistrate_disappear 0.3 You!
  **CN** + magistrate_disappear 0.3 你！

  **CN** 我听说过一些异域来的女子，能让怪物和男人都为她们着迷……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 也可能他自己吸收了那股力量， 就像之前获得那种…… 把人变成水晶的能力一样。

- **EN** I've heard tell of exotic women who can enthrall monsters and men...
- **EN** Or perhaps he has absorbed that power himself, as he did with that ... crystallization.

### `queen_og_ask`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 我没头绪……或者说，可能的解释太多了，不做测量的话，根本没法缩小范围！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 得做一大堆测量。 而我又没法把实验室 随身带着……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你那套装置…… 我本以为这种器械的支架 会是用轻木制成、再上漆的，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 接合处或许会用到些金属， 再以充能石作为动力；
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可木头、金属和石头都会挡住这些光束，而你那套采晶服却不会。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 它必定是用某种…… 奇异而极其先进的材料制成的。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哦？
  *导演注释：Proud of his suit, and of the contrast he's about to explain.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 因为这些光束本是为了穿透人体而造的， 所以如果采晶服在材质上更接近人体……！
  *导演注释：Showing off proudly.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你说他们“需要”站进光束里……现在已经不需要了吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 嗯。你觉得这里眼熟吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 嗯，好吧。
  *导演注释：No idea what Wizard is talking about.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你那套装置…… 我本以为这种器械的支架 会是用轻木制成、再上漆的……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可木头、金属和石头都会挡住这些光束……
  *导演注释：Reprising sp_gem_transport, but trailing off, vocally.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 呼，这些光束照得我……浑身酥麻。 但愿别留下什么永久性的副作用。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 他说这事说得那么轻描淡写。可这实在太不可思议了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 一个个小小的空间，就这么……不断循环……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这些对我的研究简直是天赐之宝！我是说，对我们大家都有价值……
  *导演注释：To self.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 他说这事说得那么轻描淡写。可这实在太不可思议了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我问他，这对他那个世界的魔法意味着什么， 但他根本不懂“魔法”这个词。
  *导演注释：To self. Reprising hp_wizard_torus_1*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 就好像对他来说，“魔法”就等于“童话故事”。
- **EN** 
  **CN** ……但魔法的力量 一直受制于 那些似乎与生俱来的矛盾：
- **EN** 
  **CN** 能量必须通过水晶之类的介质循环，才能不断叠加、达到相当可观的程度……
  *导演注释：To self, thinking about the way magic works and how these spaces might change that long-term. With the "but" we are coming in on his train of thought in the middle.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可要让能量在水晶中循环，就得让它转弯，而转弯又会降低能量的速度；
- **EN** 
  **CN** 一减速，威力便会流失。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 而在这种不必弯曲便会循环的空间里，这一矛盾便不复存在。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 能量可以又快又直地前进，又能在一次次重复叠加中不断积聚。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 或许根本没有上限！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我必须学会造出这样一处空间， 在里面搭建一套装置， 再做实验！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我知道，瓦伦斯一心想抓住执政官，国王也装得好像很上心，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 但这项发现对我们所有人的意义，或许远胜于那些政治闹剧。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这里就是我们那位吟游诗人朋友跟我说起的那座小镇。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哦，她那些歌啊……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可惜她没提醒我，这里到处都是哥布林。
  *导演注释：Remembering fondly*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 啊……你好……呃，你能不能多给我讲讲这些飞石？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我想知道它们是怎么运作的……
  *导演注释：Not necessarily remembering who he's talking to.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这些飞石为什么一推就会自己飞出去？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这种“驱动原理”究竟是什么？
  *导演注释：This is a very simple answer but the "uhh" is like, what, are you a 5-year-old?*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 如果飞石一旦飞起、永远不撞上任何东西，它还会停下来吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 如果它处在一个自身弯曲的地方呢？ 首尾相连、上下相接， 有点像……像个甜甜圈？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我不是说飞石会拐弯。我是说它会直着一直飞。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不，甜甜圈是完全笔直的， 而且无限延伸！

### `hw_thief_wizard` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I've no idea ... or, rather, so *many* possible ideas that there's no way to narrow them without taking measurements!
- **EN** A lot of measurements. And I can't carry my laboratory with me...

### `sp_gem_transport` （无官方中译）

- **EN** That contraption of yours... I'd expect the struts of such a device to be fashioned from lightweight wood, lacquered,
- **EN** perhaps some metal at the joints, charged stones for motive power;
- **EN** but wood, metal, and stone block these beams and your suit does not.
- **EN** It must be built from materials... exotic and far advanced.
- **EN** Oh?
  *导演注释：Proud of his suit, and of the contrast he's about to explain.*
- **EN** Because the beams were made to permeate people, so if the suit's more like a person, ... !
  *导演注释：Showing off proudly.*

### `sp_gem_transport_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** You said they 'needed' to stand in the beams... they don't any more?

### `sp_gem_transport_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Hmm. Does this seem familiar to you?
- **EN** Hmm, okay.
  *导演注释：No idea what Wizard is talking about.*

### `sp_gem_transport_2_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** That contraption of yours... I'd expect the struts of such a device to be fashioned from lightweight wood, lacquered...
- **EN** But wood, metal, and stone block these beams...
  *导演注释：Reprising sp_gem_transport, but trailing off, vocally.*

### `sp_wizard_open_beam` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Uff, these beams make me feel ... very tingly. I hope there are no permanent side effects.

### `hp_wizard_torus_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** He spoke of this so casually. But it's incredible!

### `hp_wizard_torus_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Little spaces that just... keep repeating...
- **EN** These would be a wondrous boon for my work! For all of us, I mean...
  *导演注释：To self.*

### `hp_wizard_torus_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** He spoke of this so casually. But it's incredible!
- **EN** I asked him about what this meant for magic in his world, but he didn't understand the term.
  *导演注释：To self. Reprising hp_wizard_torus_1*
- **EN** Like 'magic' meant 'fairy tale' to him.

### `hp_wizard_torus_4` （无官方中译）

- **EN** ... but the strength of magic has always been limited by conflicts seemingly inherent:
- **EN** The need for energy to cycle, through a medium such as a crystal, in order to compound to decent magnitude...
  *导演注释：To self, thinking about the way magic works and how these spaces might change that long-term. With the "but" we are coming in on his train of thought in the middle.*
- **EN** And the way that turning, so it can loop through the crystal, reduces the speed of energy;
- **EN** the slowdown saps potency.

### `hp_wizard_torus_5` （无官方中译）

- **EN** In a place that repeats without curving, this conflict dissolves.
- **EN** Energy can fly fast and true, and will compound as it repeats and overlaps.

### `hp_wizard_torus_6` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Perhaps there is no limit!
- **EN** I must learn to create one of these spaces, build an apparatus therein, experiment!

### `hp_wizard_torus_8` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I know Valens cares most about capturing the Magistrate, and the King pretends to care as well,
- **EN** but this could mean more for all of us than any of that political nonsense.

### `hp_wizard_be_aggro` （无官方中译）

- **EN** This is that town our friend the Bard was telling me about.
- **EN** Oh, those songs of hers...
- **EN** Pity she didn't warn me of the goblin infestation.
  *导演注释：Remembering fondly*

### `hs_island_friends_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Why ... hello ... err, perhaps you can show me
- **EN** more about these stones. I want to know how they work...
  *导演注释：Not necessarily remembering who he's talking to.*

### `hs_island_friends_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** So, what makes the stones go?
- **EN** What is the nature of this 'motive principle'?
  *导演注释：This is a very simple answer but the "uhh" is like, what, are you a 5-year-old?*

### `hs_island_friends_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** If, once going, the stone were never to hit anything, would it ever stop?
- **EN** What if it's in a place that curves on itself? Connects end to end and top to bottom, somewhat like a ... a doughnut?

### `hs_island_friends_3_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I don't mean the stone would turn. I mean it would go straight.
- **EN** No, the doughnut is perfectly straight and goes forever!

### `audiolog_di_tiger_lily_v2`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 反正我会！

### `end3_heroes_first` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I know *I* do!


<!-- ================= 09_bard ================= -->

# 吟游诗人 The Bard

> Heroes 线配角。配音：Anjali Bhimani。

**统计**：台词块 89 · 英文词数 577 · 涉及段落 45 · 官方中译覆盖 66/89

---


### `heroes3_bard_first`

- **EN** Goblins? Here??
  **CN** 哥布林？怎么会在这里？！

### `heroes3_bard_first_end`

- **EN** That's settled. Let's see if we can find one of these crystals.
  **CN** 这下解决了。我们看看能否找到一颗类似那样的水晶。

### `heroes3_bard_second`

- **EN** This used to be a nice place. A girl can hardly get around, now.
  **CN** 这地方以前挺好的。如今姑娘家连路都不好走。

### `heroes3_bard_second_end`

- **EN** Good riddance.
  **CN** 总算清净了。

### `heroes3_bard_crystals_first`

- **EN** At last! Let's see how they respond...
  **CN** 终于找到了！来看看它们会有什么反应……

### `heroes3_bard_crystals_first_mid`

- **EN** It's true, they are filled with life. How can it be?
  **CN** 果然，里面蕴含着生命。这怎么可能？

### `heroes3_bard_crystals_first_end`

- **EN** Now, to find the man who's making this happen.
  **CN** 现在，该去找促成这一切的人了。

### `heroes3_bard_two_goblins`

- **EN** I was hoping to find some *people* hereabouts.
  **CN** 我本指望在附近能碰到些人。

### `heroes3_bard_warrior_first`

- **EN** Hey fellow traveler, mind helping me move forward?
  **CN** 嗨，同行的旅人，介意帮我往前走吗？

### `heroes3_bard_warrior_first_end`

- **EN** A girl likes to be manhandled once in a while.
  **CN** 姑娘偶尔也喜欢被人粗鲁地推来推去。
- **EN** Makes her feel alive.
  **CN** 那才有活着的感觉。
  *导演注释：Referring to just having been pushed by the Warrior; mildly flirty, not serious.*
- **EN** But don't get used to it.
  **CN** 不过可别上瘾哦。

### `heroes3_bard_thief_first`

- **EN** Brave of you to travel all this way, through chaos and calamity.
  **CN** 一路混乱不堪、灾祸不断，你们还能走到这里，真勇敢。
- **EN** And in such a savvy outfit!
  **CN** 而且还穿得这么有品位！
  *导演注释：Relating to her like a big sister, but also establishing she is the older more mature woman in the group now.*
- **EN** It shows you know how to make friends with all manner of folk.
  **CN** 一看就知道，你们懂得怎么和形形色色的人交朋友。

### `heroes3_bard_thief_mid`

- **EN** This man who makes the crystals, what does he look like?
  **CN** 那个制造水晶的男人，长什么样？
- **EN** Wearing typical garb of your people?
  **CN** 穿的是你们那边的典型服饰？
  *导演注释：By "theatrical" she means he is extremely expressive and very hammy.*
- **EN** Strange. That's not the description I've heard.
  **CN** 奇怪。和我听到的描述不一样。

### `heroes3_bard_warrior_thief`

- **EN** Glad to be of service.
  **CN** 能帮上忙就好。

### `heroes3_bard_and_druid_first`

- **EN** And you're the famous Crystalmancer that I've been hearing about!
  **CN** 你就是那位晶术师，久仰大名！
- **EN** Nor does anyone!
  **CN** 别人也不这么叫！
  *导演注释：a little confused*
- **EN** I made it up.
  **CN** 这是我现编的。
  *导演注释：("but of course not!")*

### `heroes3_bard_and_druid_first_end`

- **EN** I don't call myself a 'performer'; it's more serious than that.
  **CN** 我可不自称“艺人”；我做的事要严肃得多。

### `heroes3_bard_and_druid_second`

- **EN** *We* can 'handle' anything!
  **CN** 我们什么都能“对付”！

### `heroes3_bard_and_priestess`

- **EN** I don't mean to be manipulative, not always, not in a *bad* way...
  **CN** 我不是故意操纵人心的，至少不总是这样…… 也不是出于恶意……

### `heroes3_bard_and_priestess_mid`

- **EN** ... but over time, I caught glimpses. This person wants to be reached in this way. That person, that way.
  **CN** ……但久而久之，我也窥见了几分。 这个人希望我用这种方式去接近， 那个人希望我用那种方式。
- **EN** When I get it *just* right ... they're happy for a minute.
  **CN** 只要我拿捏得刚刚好……他们就能开心上那么一小会儿。
  *导演注释：She is continuing a longer story that we didn't hear the beginning of...*
- **EN** Now ... this is just how I live.
  **CN** 如今……这就是我的活法。

### `heroes3_bard_and_priestess_end`

- **EN** Thanks for talking. I feel a little better about it.
  **CN** 谢谢你听我说。我心里好受多了。

### `heroes3_bard_and_wizard`

- **EN** It's amazing the way you just zip around!
  **CN** 你嗖嗖地到处瞬移，我觉得太酷了！
- **EN** You must be incredibly intelligent... I can't even imagine.
  **CN** 你一定聪明得不得了……我都想象不出来。

### `heroes3_bard_and_wizard_2`

- **EN** When you swap with me... it's exhilarating!
  **CN** 你和我交换位置……太刺激了！
- **EN** First I feel tingly inside, then *pop!*, I'm somewhere else! And short of breath.
  **CN** 先是身体里一阵酥麻，然后“啪”的一下， 我就到别处了！还喘不上气。
  *导演注释：Talking about being teleport-swapped with Wizard. She's giving him some positive attention she suspects he doesn't get much of. But she's not holding a negative view of him either. This is just how she knows to be friendly. She is not intending any kind of innuendo here.*

### `heroes3_bard_dragon_maze`

- **EN** Western fire lizard.
  **CN** 西部火蜥蜴。
- **EN** Got to be quite wary with these, so my new friends said.
  **CN** 新朋友们说，对付这些家伙可得小心。

### `heroes3_four_with_button_quad`

- **EN** Prettier than me?
  **CN** 比我还漂亮？
  *导演注释：As he says "godsend", he realizes he's a bit too on the nose.*

### `heroes3_bard_wizard_druid_pondering`

- **EN** So, this Magistrate fellow... if he's so powerful, and now he's far from the reach of your King, ... why is he running from us?
  **CN** 那么，这位执政官……如果他这么厉害， 如今又在你们国王鞭长莫及的地方……那他为什么要躲着我们？
- **EN** We are a formidable group ... but ...
  **CN** 我们这群人固然不好惹……可是……

### `heroes3_six`

- **EN** That's the spirit!
  **CN** 这才对嘛！
  *导演注释：Trying to be encouraging in a tight spot.*

### `heroes3_victory_lap`

- **EN** What dangers might that territory hold?
  **CN** 那片地方会藏着什么危险？
- **EN** I think he's gone.
  **CN** 他好像走了。
- **EN** The very nerve!
  **CN** 真是岂有此理！
  *导演注释：confused, working it out*

### `heroes3_victory_lap_end`

- **EN** There is a great song to be written about whatever comes next.
  **CN** 接下来无论发生什么，都值得写成一首好歌。
  *导演注释：matter-of-factly*

### `queen_og_ask`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 蛮力通常不是我的风格。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不过玩上一阵倒也无妨。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我喜欢这道光的颜色。让人心静。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 过来吧，闪亮的小家伙。看看能拿你做些什么。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这让我想起那座小镇，我第一次去西部时，最先抵达的就是那里。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 它叫什么来着？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 记忆如最细的尘埃颗粒， 在空气中飘散……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 在西部的那三年，真是段快乐时光。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那时我才开始学着做个大人，学着和形形色色的陌生人打交道， 学着靠自己闯出一条路。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那些记忆……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 啊，集市广场！第一年里，我隔几天就要来一趟。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 很多，而且大家都很照顾我。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可惜，你说得对。 搭把手？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 它们是自找的，不是吗？
  *导演注释：After making a goblin disappear into the infinite, essentially disintegrating it. We emphasize *almost* because that word is relevant in her next line.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 等回到家就知道了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 什么？你怎么会觉得自己失礼？
  *导演注释：Sheepish that she is putting a lot of energy into looking and asking, and Bard seems very spacy.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我倒觉得你做成了不少事。咱们配合得也很好。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 看到这里被毁成这样，我心里很不是滋味。总觉得……它象征着什么。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我也说不上来。正因如此，我才不安。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 美好的回忆付之一炬，就像烧掉一卷伪造的旧手稿，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 又或许本是真迹，却被我当成了赝品。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们干得不错，朋友。
  *导演注释：Trying to cheer her up*

### `sp_bard_takes_wand` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Brute force is not normally my way.
- **EN** But we can have fun for a bit.

### `hp_bard_purple` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I enjoy the color of this light. Soothing.

### `hp_bard_purple_crystal` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Come here, shiny one. Let's see what we can do with you.

### `hp_bard_town` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Reminds me of that small town where I first arrived on my visit to the West.
- **EN** What was it called, again?

### `hp_bard_town_mid` （无官方中译）

- **EN** The memories drift like finest granules of dust through the air...

### `hp_bard_town_mid2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Those three years in the West were such a happy time.
- **EN** I was just learning to be an adult, to interact with all these strangers. How to make my way.

### `hp_bard_town_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** The memories...

### `hp_bard_market_square` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Ahh, the market square! I came here every few days in that first year.
- **EN** Many. And they were very helpful.

### `hp_endpoint_weapon` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Unfortunately, you're right. A hand, please?

### `hp_endpoint_weapon_mid1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** They asked for it, didn't they.
  *导演注释：After making a goblin disappear into the infinite, essentially disintegrating it. We emphasize *almost* because that word is relevant in her next line.*

### `hp_endpoint_weapon_mid2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** We'll find out when we get back home.

### `hp_endpoint_weapon_mid3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** What, why would you think you're being rude?
  *导演注释：Sheepish that she is putting a lot of energy into looking and asking, and Bard seems very spacy.*
- **EN** It seems to me you're getting a lot done. We work well together.

### `hp_endpoint_weapon_mid4` （无官方中译）

- **EN** This destruction has put me in a mood. It feels ... symbolic.
- **EN** I don't quite know, and this is what bothers me.

### `hp_endpoint_weapon_mid5` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Happy memories, burned like an old manuscript that was fake;
- **EN** or a manuscript that was real, but I treated it as a fake.

### `hp_endpoint_weapon_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** We did well, friend.
  *导演注释：Trying to cheer her up*

### `audiolog_di_tiger_lily_v2`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 各位不怎么正派的朋友，你们好啊！
  *导演注释：We hear the sound of a gem placement and the endpoint beam powering up*

### `end3_suit` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Hail, not-so-good fellows!
  *导演注释：We hear the sound of a gem placement and the endpoint beam powering up*


<!-- ================= 10_druid ================= -->

# 德鲁伊 The Druid

> Heroes 线配角。配音：Matthew Waterson。

**统计**：台词块 69 · 英文词数 586 · 涉及段落 22 · 官方中译覆盖 52/69

---


### `heroes3_bard_and_druid_first`

- **EN** So, you're the performer I've been hearing about.
  **CN** 原来你就是我听说的那位艺人。
- **EN** We don't call ourselves that.
  **CN** 我们不这么称呼自己。
  *导演注释：proddingly playful, to try to show that Druid doesn't have to be guarded. (not flirty though)*

### `heroes3_bard_and_druid_second`

- **EN** You can handle this creature, yes?
  **CN** 你能对付这怪物吧？

### `heroes3_druid_thief_first`

- **EN** Hail there! You look to be from the West.
  **CN** 你好！你看起来像是西边来的。
- **EN** I'd been preparing to book passage to the West, myself, but my plans were just interrupted.
  **CN** 我本来也正准备订票去西边，可出了点岔子，打乱了计划。
- **EN** No pleasure involved. Just looking for someone.
  **CN** 与游玩无关。只是找个人。

### `heroes3_warrior_and_druid_1`

- **EN** So. It is your charge to apprehend this man?
  **CN** 这么说，你的任务就是逮捕这个人？
- **EN** My charge is similar. Our Circle of Elders is ... quite angry.
  **CN** 我的任务也差不多。我们的长老团……非常愤怒。

### `heroes3_warrior_and_druid_2`

- **EN** You want to take him back West, to answer for his crimes there.
  **CN** 你想把他带回西边，让他在那里为他的罪行受审。
- **EN** But I must take him East, to answer for his crimes against us. This constitutes a problem.
  **CN** 可我得带他去东边，让他为他对我们所犯的罪行付出代价。 这就成了个问题。

### `heroes3_warrior_and_druid_2_end`

- **EN** ... as evidenced by the seeds being smaller than those of a common apple.
  **CN** ……证据就是，它的种子比普通苹果的还要小。
- **EN** If they are not, transmutation of this plant may only be performed in a private setting.
  **CN** 如果不是这样，对这种植物的转化就只能私下进行。
  *导演注释：Has been explaining some complicated Druidic law for 5 minutes.*
- **EN** I have an eye to make some changes, when I ascend to the Circle of Elders.
  **CN** 等我跻身长老团，我打算做些改革。
  *导演注释：He's been listening to Druid talk about this for like 5 minutes.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们就是这样守护这个秘密的。 只要世人以为转化是一种天生的能力——
- **EN** 
  **CN** 以为根本没有什么技法——那就没人会来窃取这门技法的奥秘。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 说成“这招”，未免太轻巧了。像他这样的外人，竟能——
- **EN** 
  **CN** 学会这门方法，实在了不起。它复杂而精妙， 通常需要经年累月的训练。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不过，确实如此。而且他越是公开地这么干， 就越说明我们一直在讲的那套关于转化的说法， 并不是真相。
  *导演注释："normally" because somehow Magistrate learned it very quickly.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 所以那套说辞也许撑不了多久了。 不过我个人倒无所谓；我对谎言本就没什么好感。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 等我跻身长老团，我打算做些改革。

### `heroes3_warrior_and_druid_transmutation` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Thus we protect the secret. If the public thinks transmutation is an inborn ability --
- **EN** if there is no technique -- then none will try to steal the secrets of the technique.
- **EN** 'Trick' is reductive. It's impressive that a stranger such as he
- **EN** could learn the method. It's complex and nuanced, and normally requires years of training.
- **EN** But, yes. And the more he does it, so publicly, the more evidence that the story around transmutation is not what we've been telling.
  *导演注释："normally" because somehow Magistrate learned it very quickly.*
- **EN** So the story may not last. But I don't personally mind that; I've no love for the falsehoods.
- **EN** I have an eye to make some changes, when I ascend to the Circle of Elders.

### `heroes3_wizard_and_druid_1`

- **EN** It isn't magic of the kind you deal in.
  **CN** 这可不是你所精通的那种魔法。
- **EN** Aeons ago, before living beings arose to crawl the canyons and the steppes,
  **CN** 很久很久以前，还没有生灵在峡谷和草原间爬行，
  *导演注释：Perplexed*
- **EN** the world was a simpler place. Rocky and pristine.
  **CN** 那时的世界要简单得多。到处是岩石，原始而纯净。
- **EN** The world slowly, slowly changed, from a pile of rocks, to this teeming green mass we are today.
  **CN** 世界缓慢又一点一滴地改变，从一堆岩石， 变成了如今这个生机盎然的绿色世界。

### `heroes3_wizard_and_druid_2`

- **EN** But all forms contain their seeds within.
  **CN** 但一切形态之中，都蕴含着自身的种子。
- **EN** Our bodies still hold, in every mote of flesh, the full course of our slow transformation from lithic quietude to animal messiness.
  **CN** 从寂静的岩石到躁动的动物，这段漫长的演变， 至今仍留在我们每一寸血肉里。
- **EN** To be transmuted is a sort of ... descent through corporeal memory.
  **CN** 被转化，是一种……沿着肉身记忆下沉的过程。

### `heroes3_druid_and_priestess_1`

- **EN** We have legends of her kind. Never sure what to believe.
  **CN** 我们那里流传着关于她这类人的传奇故事， 只是不知道有几分可信。
- **EN** I'll say only that I am uneasy.
  **CN** 我只能说，心里有些不安。
  *导演注释：With "Never sure..." he's saying "I have never been sure what to believe."*

### `heroes3_druid_and_priestess_2`

- **EN** I don't doubt your word.
  **CN** 我不怀疑你的话。
  *导演注释：Referring to the Priestess, who protects others from attack.*

### `heroes3_four_with_button_quad`

- **EN** If Golden Girl were here, I could freeze that Goblin and we'd be done.
  **CN** 要是金发姑娘在的话，我就能冻住那个哥布林， 事情也就解决了。
- **EN** I'm warming up to her. She's been a consistent ... godsend. Quite pretty as well.
  **CN** 我对她越来越有好感了。她一直是我们的…… 天降紫微星。人也很漂亮。
- **EN** Let's get that button pressed.
  **CN** 去按那个按钮吧。
  *导演注释：teasing*

### `heroes3_bard_wizard_druid_pondering`

- **EN** I don't believe he's fleeing, exactly.
  **CN** 我也不认为他算是在逃跑。
  *导演注释："Don't get me wrong, we're a formidable group..."*
- **EN** He's going *toward* something, rather than away. Earlier, he mentioned a specific destination.
  **CN** 他不是在逃跑，而是在赶往某个地方。 他之前提过自己要去哪儿。
- **EN** A 'special transport', beyond which his son was not to follow.
  **CN** 说是要用“特别的法子”离开，他儿子之后就不能再跟着他了。

### `heroes3_six`

- **EN** Charming venue for a party.
  **CN** 真是个聚会的好地方。

### `heroes3_victory_lap`

- **EN** It is my charge to apprehend that man. I will proceed regardless of what waits beyond.
  **CN** 逮捕那个人是我的职责。 无论前方等待我的是什么，我都会继续前行。
- **EN** He's saying that actually *we're* the monsters?
  **CN** 他是说，其实我们才是怪物？

### `heroes3_victory_lap_end`

- **EN** He hoped his tale of woe would move us. No. He flees, we follow, and apprehend. Simple.
  **CN** 他指望他的悲惨故事能打动我们。 没门。他逃，我们追，然后抓人。就这么简单。
- **EN** Then we're also going somewhere very strange.
  **CN** 那我们也去那个非常奇怪的地方。
- **EN** Best not fail, then.
  **CN** 那最好别失败。

### `queen_og_ask`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 这回我也能搬东西了。看看是什么感觉。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不能。它们的灵仍在，我只是让形体重新与灵相合。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哦？改天讲给我听听……
  *导演注释：Something of a non-sequitur, but she's looking for somewhere more familiar to take the conversation.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这么说，你那位灵体朋友本身是一艘船，而不是被转化成一艘船的？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那一定是一种非常特别的细丝。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 整天让石头这样在水面上跳来跳去……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这是你们族人的日常活动吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我也是。
  *导演注释：With "prowess" she is bragging a bit lightheartedly (but also is telling the truth).*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 跟我学？不行。跟别人学？明面上也不行。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 私底下嘛……那就复杂了。

### `sp_druid_takes_wand` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I can be a mover, for once. Let's see how that feels.

### `hs_sailor_druid_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I can't choose. They retain their spirit, and I realign form to spirit.
- **EN** Oh? Tell me about that some time...
  *导演注释：Something of a non-sequitur, but she's looking for somewhere more familiar to take the conversation.*

### `hs_sailor_druid_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** So your spirit friend, he is a boat, but he wasn't *transmuted* into a boat?
- **EN** Must be a very special kind of filament.

### `hs_sailor_druid_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** All this skipping of stones across the water all day...
- **EN** is this a typical activity of your people?
- **EN** As am I. As am I.
  *导演注释：With "prowess" she is bragging a bit lightheartedly (but also is telling the truth).*

### `hs_sailor_druid_3_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** From me? No. From someone else? Officially, no.
- **EN** Unofficially... it's complicated.

# 龙与幼龙 Dragon & Baby Dragon

> Heroes 线生物角色。

**统计**：台词块 122 · 英文词数 450 · 涉及段落 38 · 官方中译覆盖 61/122

### `promesst1_end`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 跟我来。我们还有很多事要做。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 真是奇妙，你们与我们竟会在这样的空间里彼此交汇。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你们开矿，我们筑巢；有时才发现，矿洞和巢穴原来就是同一处。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们的光与你们的光交汇。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你们的族人以前是知道这些知识的！
  *导演注释：This is a spiritual/religious saying.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我能听到其他同胞的声音，遥远而微弱。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 他们感谢你孵化了我，还帮助我成长。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们会帮你找到更多宝石。别担心。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这些宝石我已经用不着了。想拿多少拿多少。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 想想我不久前还渴求这些凡俗的碎块。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 说说看，你们的祖先究竟从哪里学会制造这些人造光束发射器的？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哼。至少我的族人还讲卫生。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们不会把光束四处乱射。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 很久以前，我们就与你们的族人彻底失去了联系……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 按你们的寿命来算，那已经是好几代人以前的事了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们的族人并不记恨那段往事。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你们还记恨吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我的职责，是去寻找更多你我两族空间交汇的地方， 让这些区域更稳固、更广阔。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 光。你的族人自以为对光和光束投射的方式有所了解。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 也许他们确实略懂一二。但我要为他们带去新的光。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们的计划，是让我作为使者，跟你一起回去。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们的光与你们的光交汇。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可后来，双方达成了一笔交易。耗资不菲。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你不会直接回家，至少现在还不会。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们会送你去下一个该去的地方。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你在这里做得很好。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 再完成最后一项任务，之后你就不再需要我了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我想，我们以后还会再见。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 下次再会。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你可来了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我知道你在外面还想用我的能力。
  *导演注释："Don't you appreciate what I did for you?"*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你可来了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 去拿宝石。全部拿来。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 全部拿来！！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 现在把宝石给我。更深处还有宝石。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 还有好多。无比珍贵。你拿那些。我拿这些。
  *导演注释：Wants to call them "foods" but forces himself to say "gems" to be understood.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 好饿。全部给我。全部。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 快喂我！就几颗就好。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 喂我宝石。宝石带来生命！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 饿。好饿！给食物。给食物。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 喂我！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 再找食物。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 多拿宝石。多拿食物！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 快去拿。拿回来。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 搜索。找到。拿回。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 尝着冰冰的。冰冰的，好吃。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 能量在体内积聚。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 感觉内脏在翻腾……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 冰冷的光越来越强。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 就快了……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 好饿，但我不能吃。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 留九份食物，直到最后。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 所有食物我们都要。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 想拿到它们，你需要食物。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 留九份食物，等事情结束。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 找到新鲜食物了。真是太好了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 但我也得等着。忍住饥饿。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 现在先留九份。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 食物一直在诱惑我。快忍不住了！但要留九份。再等一会儿。

### `skipping_stones_start`

- **EN** Banged my shoulder when you stopped so suddenly.
  **CN** 你突然停下来，害我撞到了肩膀。
  *导演注释：Boat and Sailor are on the same island, next to each other.*
- **EN** It's bruised, stiff, and annoying, like some people we know, but, nothing permanent.
  **CN** 又青又僵，还烦人，像我们认识的某些人。 不过，不会留下什么后遗症。
- **EN** And since my upper body took the impact, my legs feel *great*!
  **CN** 好在撞的是上半身，我的腿可好得很！
  *导演注释："bruised and stiff" are the immediate physical description, "annoying" comes after feeling it out.*
- **EN** Fetch? Shall I also scratch my fleas and bark at the parcelier?
  **CN** 叼回来？要不要我再挠挠身上的跳蚤，冲邮差汪汪两声？

### `ssnew_pre_map`

- **EN** South. Into uncertainty. Got it.
  **CN** 向南，驶入未知。明白了。

### `ssnew_interdiction_first`

- **EN** When we get back to civilization, this will make a great story!
  **CN** 等我们回到文明世界，这事儿准能成个好故事！
- **EN** I've never been interdicted before.
  **CN** 我还从没被阻航过。
- **EN** Well... I did have a moment of panic when you just froze up! That counts for something.
  **CN** 可你突然动不了的时候，我也被吓了一跳啊！ 总不能说我没受一点影响吧。
  *导演注释：An interdiction affects the boat in a severe and almost painful way. But nothing happens to the Sailor, except indirectly as she is riding on the boat.*
- **EN** 'Miracle' is going in my official report.
  **CN** “奇迹”这个词，我要写进正式报告里。

### `ssnew_traps`

- **EN** Traps?
  **CN** 陷阱？
- **EN** So ... someone *else* did it?
  **CN** 所以……是别的什么人干的？
- **EN** Then, we'll have to have a talk with them.
  **CN** 那我们可得和他们好好谈谈。

### `ssnew_what_do_i_do`

- **EN** So, when I get to the South, what do I do?
  **CN** 所以，我到了南边之后该怎么做？
- **EN** Heroic vandalism. My favorite kind.
  **CN** 搞破坏也能当英雄。我最喜欢这种事了。

### `ssnew_no_traps`

- **EN** Borraidus?
  **CN** 博莱达斯？
- **EN** No traps, and no strangers. We're alone.
  **CN** 没有陷阱，也没有外人。就我们两个。
- **EN** But I feel you're still nervous. Is there something you're not telling me?
  **CN** 可我感觉你还是很紧张。有什么事瞒着我？
- **EN** Oh come on. Just give me a hint.
  **CN** 哎呀，好歹给点提示嘛。
- **EN** 'Gold and Conflux'. Dockside palm-readers give more for a copper.
  **CN** “黄金、汇流”？码头上看手相的，收一个铜板都比你说得多。

### `ssnew_interdictions_secret`

- **EN** So, an interdict is just writing?
  **CN** 所以，所谓的阻航阵，说白了就是写几个字？
- **EN** They don't teach us Boat secrets, okay! Sailor school is mostly knots.
  **CN** 谁教过我们船灵的秘密啊！航海师学校净教如何打绳结。
- **EN** So why, no, *how* are we being interdicted *here*? In a place that's just a smudge on the charts?
  **CN** 那为什么……不对，我们怎么会在这种地方被阻航？ 这地方在海图上不过是个模糊的小点！
- **EN** The nearest outpost is a week away.
  **CN** 最近的前哨站也得走上一星期。

### `ssnew_interdictions_secret_2`

- **EN** Let's tally the day so far. First insult: you called me "fleshy one,"
  **CN** 我们来清算一下今天这笔账。第一项罪状：你管我叫“肉体凡躯”，
- **EN** Two, you sent me to fetch. Three, refused to tell me
  **CN** 第二，你叫我去叼东西；第三，你不肯告诉我，
  *导演注释：A conversation to entertain herself while trekking.*
- **EN** what I'm blindly strolling into, that for all I know could kill me;
  **CN** 我正稀里糊涂地往什么地方走，说不定连命都要丢在那里；
- **EN** Four, belittled me for not knowing *secrets* that are, as you know, *kept secret* exactly so Sailors like myself don't know them!
  **CN** 第四，你嘲笑我不知道那些秘密，可你明明知道， 它们之所以保密，就是为了不让像我这样的航海师知道！
- **EN** Four offenses.
  **CN** 四项罪状。
- **EN** You can't tally during the tally!
  **CN** 我在跟你算账，你不能反过来跟我算！

### `ssnew_heading_west`

- **EN** Looks like the islands run West for a while.
  **CN** 看样子这些岛屿往西延伸一段。
- **EN** That's good, right?
  **CN** 这是好事，对吧？
- **EN** Maybe?
  **CN** 也许吧？
- **EN** You're very wise.
  **CN** 您可真有智慧。

### `ssnew_weather`

- **EN** Yes?
  **CN** 怎么了？
- **EN** Noted!
  **CN** 记下了！

### `skipping_stones_gold_room_impending`

- **EN** Calling me "skipper" now? Thank you for the joke that is clearly very funny.
  **CN** 现在肯叫我“船长”了？真是好笑，多谢啊。
- **EN** Oh?
  **CN** 哦？
- **EN** Maybe that's one of your traps...
  **CN** 没准那就是你说的陷阱之一……
- **EN** ... well then ...
  **CN** ……好吧……
- **EN** This is incredible, actually! All we have to do is tell them where it is; The guild can send a team to figure it out.
  **CN** 说真的，这也太惊人了！我们只要告诉他们位置， 公会就能派人来查个明白。
  *导演注释：confused, thinking*
- **EN** They'll pay us a fabulous bonus, and we'll be legends!
  **CN** 他们一定会给我们发一大笔奖金！我们要成传奇人物了！
  *导演注释：Seeing ramifications, perking up*

### `skipping_stones_shrine`

- **EN** I touched the center, and ... lights bloomed all around me. Then, nothing.
  **CN** 我碰了碰正中央，然后……光芒在我四周绽开。 再之后，什么也没发生。
- **EN** Now what?
  **CN** 然后呢？
- **EN** That, I can do.
  **CN** 这个我在行。

### `ssnew_tacking`

- **EN** You know how happy I am to follow your instructions.
  **CN** 你知道我有多乐意听从你的指令。
- **EN** You said go West, so I kept going West... but now, you know, I think we've overshot.
  **CN** 你说往西，我就一直往西走……可现在，我觉得我们走过头了。
  *导演注释：Sarcastically*
- **EN** Tacking, on land? That's ridiculous.
  **CN** 在陆地上还要“航行”？这也太离谱了。
- **EN** *I'll* get there!
  **CN** 到达的是我！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哦？愿闻其详。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 怎么了？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我这辈子见过的最大的一张嘴？你还见过更大的？
  *导演注释：He is impressed and kind of excited by the size of the whirlpool.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 谢了，船灵。我会想办法的。
  *导演注释：If the Boat is stuck here, Borraidus would eventually unbind and go back to the spirit world. That said, there's a chance someone would come by...*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我每次都能！

### `skipping_stones_whirlpool` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Oh, do tell.
- **EN** Yes?
- **EN** Biggest mouth that *I've* ever seen? You've seen bigger?
  *导演注释：He is impressed and kind of excited by the size of the whirlpool.*
- **EN** Thanks, boat. I'll figure something out.
  *导演注释：If the Boat is stuck here, Borraidus would eventually unbind and go back to the spirit world. That said, there's a chance someone would come by...*
- **EN** I *always* do!

### `ssnew_good_place`

- **EN** So now what...
  **CN** 接下来该怎么办……
  *导演注释：As if to say "This is good progress; what can you do next?"*

### `ssnew_good_place_2`

- **EN** Glad *that's* over with.
  **CN** 总算把这段熬过去了。

### `ssnew_built_in_1`

- **EN** I've been thinking.
  **CN** 我一直在琢磨。
- **EN** *I've been thinking*, how does some writing on the ground actually hold *you* locked down, from so far away?
  **CN** 我在想，地上那几个符号，怎么就能隔着那么远让你动不了？
- **EN** My shoulder will attest that the effect was not gentle.
  **CN** 我的肩膀可以作证，那一下可一点都不温柔。
  *导演注释：Re-emphasizing "I've been thinking" as though she's annoyed.*
- **EN** But you're saying some small-ish rocks on a beach did this. How could that possibly work?
  **CN** 可照你的说法，海滩上那几块不大的石头，就能把你困住？这怎么可能？
- **EN** I ... oh?
  **CN** 我……啊？

### `ssnew_built_in_2`

- **EN** It's built into the bones?
  **CN** 刻进龙骨？
- **EN** -- fashioned from the skipping stones, I know --
  **CN** ……它们是用飞石造的，我知道……
- **EN** -- and you turn them to various angles --
  **CN** ……你还会把它们转向不同的角度……
- **EN** I think you're *very* willful!
  **CN** 我看你倒是犟得很！
- **EN** Ahh yeah, with the cables and bushings. I had to clean those, that one time.
  **CN** 啊，对，还有那些缆索和衬套。那回我还得清理它们呢。
- **EN** Self-sabotage.
  **CN** 自废武功。

### `ssnew_built_in_3`

- **EN** So these famous interdictions, that *feel* like super-powerful magic, are just self-sabotage.
  **CN** 所以，那些大名鼎鼎、让人觉得威力无穷的阻航阵，原来不过是自废武功。
- **EN** No enemy would self-sabotage. So actually, this would be useless in a battle?
  **CN** 敌人可不会自废武功。所以真打起仗来，这东西根本没用？
  *导演注释：She's thinking through what she's just learned about this, versus what is the "common knowledge" about it.*
- **EN** But the public perception is that the interdiction is a combat weapon.
  **CN** 可大家都以为，阻航阵是打仗用的武器啊。
- **EN** Wait. Are you allowed to tell *me* this?
  **CN** 等等，这事你能告诉我吗？

### `ssnew_please_disable_interdict`

- **EN** If I'm not fast enough for you, you can find someone else!
  **CN** 嫌我动作慢，你大可以另找别人！
- **EN** 'Quickly'? I don't like the way you said that. What's going on?
  **CN** “越快越好”？你这语气可不太对劲。到底怎么回事？

### `skipping_stones_return_no_impeller`

- **EN** I'm not sure what happened... I don't ... hmm.
  **CN** 我也不知道是怎么回事……我不知道……呃。
- **EN** Why are you saying that, in such a suggestive way?
  **CN** 你干嘛用那种意味深长的口气说这话？
  *导演注释：When talking about changing the past, he is referring to the undo function in the game in a slightly fourth-wall-breaking way.*
- **EN** Such as?
  **CN** 比如呢？

### `skipping_stones_return_no_impeller_repeat`

- **EN** So, now what?
  **CN** 所以，现在该怎么办？
- **EN** Nothing?
  **CN** 没办法？

### `ssnew_interdict_destroyed_treasures_0`

- **EN** Got it!
  **CN** 搞定！

### `ssnew_interdict_destroyed_treasures_n`

- **EN** Got it!
  **CN** 搞定！
- **EN** Can you tell who did this?
  **CN** 能看出是谁干的吗？
- **EN** Right! Freeze up anyone who wants to scoot around and dig.
  **CN** 对啊！把想跑去挖宝的人全都困住。
- **EN** *Can* we scoot around and dig, now?
  **CN** 那我们现在总能去挖宝了吧？

### `skipping_stones_treasures_1`

- **EN** Borraidus, look what I found!
  **CN** 博莱达斯，看我找到了什么！
- **EN** Rolled up parchment. Unrolling, it's completely blank.
  **CN** 一卷羊皮纸。打开一看，完全空白。
- **EN** But here's the thing: it's perfect. There are no crinkles or rips.
  **CN** 可奇就奇在，它完好如新，连一道折痕、一处破口都没有。
- **EN** Grains of sand just slide off. So do droplets of water, and the paper remains dry.
  **CN** 沙粒会直接滑落，水滴也是，纸始终干干的。
- **EN** Nor I. This is an incredible find.
  **CN** 我也是。我们可发现了不得了的东西。
- **EN** Consider this: we Sailors are trained not to put all our eggs and so forth.
  **CN** 你想啊，我们航海师在受训时就学过什么 “别把鸡蛋都放在一个篮子里”之类的。
- **EN** We've found *one* chest. To me, that's a clue that there may be more. Can you see anything?
  **CN** 我们找到了一只宝箱。我觉得这说明很可能还有别的宝箱。你能看出什么吗？
- **EN** Six! If they're all as nice as this one, we'll be made sector supervisors when we get back!
  **CN** 六件宝藏！要是每件宝藏都这么有价值，我们回去一定能当区域主管！
- **EN** I'm always quite shrewd!
  **CN** 我向来机灵！

### `skipping_stones_treasures_2`

- **EN** I've found a second treasure! This looks like a ruler for measuring distance on charts.
  **CN** 找到第二件宝藏了！看着像一把在海图上量距离的尺。
- **EN** The markings are of fine platinum; the units of measurement are smaller than our inches.
  **CN** 尺上的刻度用的是上好的铂金，计量单位比我们的英寸还小。
- **EN** In place of the numbers are strange symbols, one solitary symbol where each number would be.
  **CN** 本该标数字的地方，却刻着奇怪的符号，每处都只有一个符号。
- **EN** I guess these *are* the numbers? But no multiple digits.
  **CN** 我猜，这些就是数字？可每个数怎么都只用一个符号表示？
- **EN** The body of the ruler looks like dark wood. But the strangest thing is ...
  **CN** 尺身看着像深色木头。可最奇怪的是……
- **EN** it's stretchy along its length, as if it were made of rubber.
  **CN** 它能沿长度方向拉伸，像是橡胶做的。
- **EN** But only when I pull it lengthwise. When I try to bend it, it's rigid.
  **CN** 但只有顺着长度拉，它才会伸长。 我想把它掰弯，却怎么也掰不动。
- **EN** When I stretch it, the length markings all become further apart, uniformly.
  **CN** 拉长时，所有长度刻度都会均匀地拉开距离。
- **EN** And all the while, it just looks and feels like natural wood.
  **CN** 而且从头到尾，它看起来、摸起来都像天然木头。
- **EN** Rulers are for measuring. So why would you want the measurement lines to stretch?
  **CN** 尺子是拿来测量的，为什么要让刻度线也跟着拉开？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 又找到一件宝藏，是一袋大头针，也是铂金做的。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 每根针的针帽上都刻着不同的景象，还上了色：
- **EN** 
  **CN** 城市、沙漠、海崖……可能有上百根针，每幅景象都不一样。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 一时认不出来。嗯……奇怪，袋里的针始终朝着同一个方向。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 摸着不像有磁性，可每当两根针靠近，就会自动排齐， 我却感觉不到有任何力在作用。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 把它们转开一点也不费劲，可它们总会自己转回来。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 雕刻没有磨损，颜色没有褪去，边缘也完好无损。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 目前看来，这些宝藏似乎都是绘制海图的工具。

### `skipping_stones_treasures_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I've got another treasure. This is a bag of pins, also platinum.
- **EN** Engraved on the head of each pin, and then painted, are a variety of scenes:
- **EN** cities, deserts, ocean cliffs ... there may be a hundred such pins. Each image is different.
- **EN** Not immediately. Hmm. Strange. The pins stay aligned in the bag.
- **EN** They don't feel magnetic, but any time two of them come close, they just happen to line up neatly. But with no force I can detect.
- **EN** It's effortless to turn them away from each other, but they just prefer to turn back.
- **EN** The engravings are unworn; the colors and edges are perfect.
- **EN** So far, all these treasures seem to be implements for charting.

### `skipping_stones_treasures_4`

- **EN** This one's strange. It's like a portable sundial, with engravings around the circumference and a post in the middle.
  **CN** 这件很奇怪。像个便携日晷，外圈刻着纹路，中间竖着一根柱子。
- **EN** As I turn it, the shadow from the post lands in places I don't expect.
  **CN** 我转动它时，柱子的影子总落在意想不到的位置。
- **EN** It doesn't match the natural shadows from my hands.
  **CN** 和我双手投下的自然影子对不上。
- **EN** I don't understand how this is possible.
  **CN** 我完全不明白这是怎么做到的。
- **EN** I'm just amazed. As far as valuables of unknown origin,
  **CN** 我就是觉得不可思议。像这种来历不明的贵重物品，
- **EN** normally if we find one such in an entire season, we'd be lucky.
  **CN** 照平常，一整个航季能碰上一件就算走运了。
- **EN** Yes, okay, nevertheless, this is still great.
  **CN** 行行行，话虽如此，这还是很了不得。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这件是一瓶墨水，可瓶口没有塞子，就这么敞着。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我打开箱子时，瓶子横躺着，墨水却一滴也没洒出来。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我起初还以为墨水都干了，可一晃瓶子，里面的墨水照样会来回晃荡。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 没错。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 没错。加上这件，我们已经找到五件宝藏了，差不多完成99%啦。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我就这么算！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这一件看着像支钢笔。笔杆的颈部有个转盘， 我一转动它，笔尖……居然会变形！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 细尖……粗尖……短毛刷……吸墨头……一个刻着螺旋凹槽的圆球。
  *导演注释：Pretty weird for the tip of the pen to just morph into different shapes like this.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 抱歉！它拿着手感实在太好了……摆弄起来也是！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你看！这趟绕路值得吧！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 而我……能活下去了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 得了吧！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你觉得回去这一路会风平浪静，还是又会被什么拦住？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那就朝下一场灾祸出发吧。向海进发，嘿哟！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 嘿，先生！
- **EN** 
  **CN** + victory 嘿。宝藏。你说过一共有六件。我们把剩下的也找齐吧。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 好吧。那就等风暴过去，再回来拿剩下的！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 嗯……是！合格的航海师埋保险箱，至少也得埋到三英尺深。 真要认真埋的话，还得再深一倍。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 宝藏，嘿哟！
- **EN** 
  **CN** + victory 宝藏还没找齐。不过剩下的现在应该很容易找了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们赶紧去那些漏掉的岛上，稍微挖一挖就行。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 好吧，等天气放晴，也让我们歇一会儿，再回来拿剩下的宝藏。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可不是嘛！但凡像样点的航海师，都会把这些宝藏埋得深深的。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 向前进发，嘿哟！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 嘿，先生！
- **EN** 
  **CN** + victory 为什么急着走？阳光这么温暖，天空这么平静。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 没拿到最后一件宝藏，真不甘心。不过走吧。 五件宝藏已经不少了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 拿钱咯，嘿哟！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我回来了，而且全拿到了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** + victory 风暴还会来吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我真为我们骄傲！面对灾难，我们只是满足于苟活吗？并没有！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们要的是发财！我看，还能在公会里升上一级。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 明明是我选的你吧？！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 收获，嘿哟！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这边这座岛看起来很有意思，也很可疑。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们……必须……拿到……所有宝藏！
  *导演注释：insistently*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 别说这是贪心。这叫……冒险精神。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这些宝藏代表的，不只是我们将来的财富，也不只是地位。 （想想那得多风光！）
  *导演注释：She's making up the word "adventureness" even as she says it.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 它们还是某个未知族群留下的遗物， 是这个世界一段失落历史的珍贵见证！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你总不希望这些宝物里藏着的珍贵学问就这么没了吧？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我很快回来！你可哪儿都别去。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这边这座岛看着挺有意思。我也说不上来。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我想看看这边过去有什么。要是现在就走，我们就永远不会知道了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 公会肯雇我，靠的正是我这股不分轻重、只管冒险的劲！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 分内的事。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 明白。两处！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 两处阻航阵，不只一处。
  *导演注释：She can't hear Borraidus, so doesn't know what Sailor was responding to.*
  *导演注释：(calling across the water)*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 所以，你平时……我是说，没被……困住的时候，都做些什么？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 目前看来你在陆地上也挺能干的嘛！没想过去当航海师？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们才不会遇袭呢！至少不常有…… 好吧，之前倒是被袭击过那么一回。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哦，对…………还有那次！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 抱歉……我是说，经常上岸办事的那种航海师……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 没别的意思！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 所以你究竟来自哪里？看你的头发，我猜……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 东境？那么远，那你是怎么进公会的？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 所以……你见过死水域吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 听起来很美，像盘踞在远海的那头巨兽，泛着玻璃般光泽的眼睛。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 船只能从阻航阵所在的位置往外走——
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那么……你觉得这些阻航阵是怎么回事？ 怎么偏偏出现在这里，又偏偏是现在？
  *导演注释："Ask her about the interdicts to see if she responds suspiciously in any way".*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 有结论了吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 早被沙子埋住，或者被海水冲走了吧？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 感觉说不通。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 她都管自己的船灵叫维迪！那我以后是不是该叫你博莱？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 还不是多亏了我！
  *导演注释："I've half a runner..." is like "I've half a mind..." but a different idiom. The runners are part of the propulsion system for the boat.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 当然！博莱和我刚刚还在聊那些传闻。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 她原来不是航海师啊，我压根没看出来。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 今天又干得漂亮！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你是说，除了头发？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们出发吧！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那些古怪的红色能量……是什么？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 喂……那边有人吗？能不能帮个忙，打个水漂， 把一块石头送回我这边来？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这点水，在你们看来就很多了？
  *导演注释：Calling out across the water to Sailor. Also confused about how this big ocean could possibly be water, since water only coheres in smaller droplets in their world. But since Warrior just asked this, she accepts the premise and goes one step further.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我是贾奈拉。幸会。
  *导演注释：He doesn't know her name or how to address her, as she's dressed very strangely...*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哦，又来这一套。
  *导演注释：In this conversation they are both calling across the water.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 又来这一套。你好！
  *导演注释：To self.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不是这里。是一个很像这里、却又截然不同的地方。
  *导演注释：Still calling across water.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可以试试几个办法。
  *导演注释：Still calling across water.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这场面可真够有意思。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 是吗？你觉得有意思？
  *导演注释：Calling back across the water.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那你要是让我也能“平静”地回到岸上，我就感激不尽了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哇……你还能这么做？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 佩服。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 谢谢你们回应我的求救！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 谢——谢——啦！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 现在什么情况？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哦，我负责发现新地界，再把它们勘测清楚……
  *导演注释：(calling across the water)*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 好让你们这种“岸上莽夫”去冒险，再赚足名声呗。
  *导演注释：(calling across the water)*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 勘测更合我的性子。不用跟什么大人物打交道。
  *导演注释：Now they are closer together, talking normally. "Sailor" here actually means "Footsailor", a euphemism like "Groundpounder" for those who spend a lot of time off the boat dealing with local conditions.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 而且我不擅长交朋友。这个也算原因吧。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 再说，我从不运贵重物品，别人也没理由来抢我。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 而且严格来说，咱们都是水手。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我知道那是口头上的叫法，只是在我老家不太这么说。
  *导演注释：Realizing just now it was kind of an insult.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 对，东境一带，那里差不多就是公会地图所能绘到的最远处了。
  *导演注释：Breaking the ice on a semi-obvious subject, as her hair is an obvious and unusual white color.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 如果天气作美，要走上六个月，
  *导演注释：She is pretending to be someone from the sticks who is self-conscious about where she's from... and is covering up the self-conscoiusness with a kind of boasting about how far it is.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哪怕坐的是我这艘快船，也得这么久。坐你的船也一样！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我遇到过一个家伙，是从你们大陆来的外交官。 给情人安排个差事并不稀奇； 但要一步步干到勘测员，那可得拿出真本事来。
  *导演注释："Eastern Reaches, wow... that's far!"*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 他费尽心思容忍我， 但等我踏上第一次远航时， 他反倒松了口气。
  *导演注释：Here she is saying "I took advantage of a temporary boyfriend to career climb," but it's part of her cover. This is something that would be typical in the "barbarian" Eastern Reaches but considered deeply uncouth on the Mainland where most of the higher-up Guild people are (Sailor included).*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 是个好人。后来就再没见过他。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 年轻小伙子会互相叫板，跑到那儿去，把这种危险当成儿戏。 通常他们都会回来。
  *导演注释：The characters are calling back and forth to each other from afar. The Eastern Reaches are out near the Deadwaters, which cannot be sailed into.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可正是他们回不来的那些时候，让那地方出名。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我跟他们去过一次，只敢远远看着。 水面像抛光的缟玛瑙一样发亮，放眼望去，湖面死寂，平到天边。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 石头一碰到它就会当场停住，根本打不起水漂。 所有动能，就这么被吞掉了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那很可怕。我再也没回去过。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 维迪，你不能来接我们吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 别说了别说了。行行行，我明白。
  *导演注释：Cut off while saying "They can only go outward from where the interdicts were placed; there's a residue."*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 维迪会等我们。
  *导演注释：She is hearing both Sailor and her own Boat at the same time. Like you're trying to listen to someone on the phone and someone in the same room is talking to you at the same time. The first "Too much" is to her Boat, the second to Sailor.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 在你到这儿之前， 我唯一能做的，就是坐在这儿 琢磨这件事。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 也许只是这项技术开发时期留下的旧测试，后来被人遗忘了？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 也许埋过，最近又露出来。
  *导演注释："piled over" as in covered by sand over time.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 好了，嘘，维迪！嘘——维迪，我正跟人说话呢。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 好啦好啦，好好好，维迪，我会的。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 维迪说我们可以信任你。所以……你有没有听说过一种传闻：
- **EN** 
  **CN** 据说有个势力，有人称它为“远方公会”，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 因为没人知道它真正的名字……一个位于死水彼岸的帝国？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我不只是在勘测。我被派到这里，还另有任务。
  *导演注释：(at being called 'Borrie')*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 寻找远方公会入侵的证据。要是这些阻航阵是偷来的， 那证据或许已经找到了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 啊哈！终于到了！这就是我的船灵，维迪苏德。 嘿，维迪，想我没有？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 维迪！这话听着有点失礼。
  *导演注释：To Jainaila, the Sailor.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 维迪！你说得我都不好意思了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 没事，没事……到海上会合，再来一次航中换船就行。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 谢谢你救了我们。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 报告上就这么写吧。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 事情都弄好了。现在，只要离开这里就好。

  **CN** + fog_clear 'exit 0.2 出口已经标在地图上了。看着有点远。
  *导演注释：to self*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不过我运气不错，这些光束最适合用来迅速跨越空间。
  *导演注释：to self*
- **EN** 
  **CN** + overhead_map_release 2 那应该很容易！

### `skipping_stones_treasures_5` （无官方中译）

- **EN** This one is a bottle of ink, but there's no cap. Just an open-mouthed bottle.
- **EN** When I opened the chest, it was lying on its side, but no ink had spilled out.
- **EN** At first I thought the ink was all dried up, but as I wave the bottle, it sloshes around inside, just as you'd expect.
- **EN** That's right.
- **EN** Indeed. With this, we've got five treasures now, so we're basically 99 percent done.
- **EN** I shall!

### `skipping_stones_treasures_6` （无官方中译）

- **EN** This one looks like a fountain pen. There's a dial around neck of the pen, and as I turn it, the tip ... changes shape!
- **EN** Fine point... thick point... a short-whiskered brush... a blotter... a ball scored with a spiral groove.
  *导演注释：Pretty weird for the tip of the pen to just morph into different shapes like this.*
- **EN** Sorry! It just feels good to hold... And manipulate!
- **EN** You see! It was worth the excursion!

### `skipping_stones_done_treasures_0` （无官方中译）

- **EN** And I ... get to live!
- **EN** Come now!
- **EN** Do you think we'll have smooth sailing all the way back, or shall we be waylaid again?
- **EN** Off into the next calamity, then. Seaward heyo!

### `skipping_stones_return_treasures_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Hey there, Sir!

### `skipping_stones_done_treasures_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Fine. We'll wait out the storm, then come back and get the rest!
- **EN** Hmm, ... yes! No decent sailor would bury a strongbox less than three feet under. Double that, if he's actually serious.
- **EN** Treasure, heyo!

### `skipping_stones_done_treasures_2_to_4` （无官方中译）

- **EN** We'll just quickly hop to any islands I missed, and dig a bit.
- **EN** Okay. Once the weather's clear, -- and we get a bit of a rest! -- we'll come back for the other treasures.
- **EN** So right! Any decent Sailor would bury these *nicely* deep.
- **EN** Onward heyo!

### `skipping_stones_return_treasures_5` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Hey there, Sir!

### `skipping_stones_done_treasures_5` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I'm annoyed we didn't get that final treasure, but, let's go. Five treasures is a lot.
- **EN** Remuneration heyo!

### `skipping_stones_return_treasures_all` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I'm back, and I have it all!

### `skipping_stones_done_treasures_all` （无官方中译）

- **EN** + victory Storm's still coming?
- **EN** I'm proud of us! Faced with disaster, did we settle for mere survival? No!
- **EN** We settled for *rich*! And a step up within the Guild, I'd predict.
- **EN** But *I* chose *you*!?!
- **EN** Bounty heyo!

### `skipping_stones_island_west_yes_treasures` （无官方中译）

- **EN** This island over here looks interesting. And suspicious.
- **EN** We. need. all. the TREASURES!
  *导演注释：insistently*
- **EN** Don't think of it as greed. Think of it as ... adventureness.
- **EN** These treasures represent not just our future wealth. Not just our future *status*. (Think of the status!)
  *导演注释：She's making up the word "adventureness" even as she says it.*
- **EN** But also, they're remnants of an unknown people, a precious material record of some lost part of the world!
- **EN** You don't want the precious knowledge contained within these treasures to be lost, do you?
- **EN** I'll be back soon! Don't *go* anywhere.

### `skipping_stones_island_west_no_treasures` （无官方中译）

### `pals_where_from_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** So where *do* you come from? I'm guessing by your hair --
- **EN** Yes, the Eastern Reaches, about as far as the Guild has ever mapped.
