- **EN** Six months' journey if the weather be kind,
  *导演注释：She is pretending to be someone from the sticks who is self-conscious about where she's from... and is covering up the self-conscoiusness with a kind of boasting about how far it is.*
- **EN** even in a fast boat like mine. Or yours!

### `pals_where_from_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Eastern Reaches. How'd you get into the Guild, then?
- **EN** Met a fellow. Diplomat from your Mainland. It is not uncommon for a paramour to be given a job; working up to Surveyor took demonstration of real skills.
  *导演注释："Eastern Reaches, wow... that's far!"*
- **EN** He did all he could to put up with me, but he was relieved when I set out on my first voyage.
  *导演注释：Here she is saying "I took advantage of a temporary boyfriend to career climb," but it's part of her cover. This is something that would be typical in the "barbarian" Eastern Reaches but considered deeply uncouth on the Mainland where most of the higher-up Guild people are (Sailor included).*

### `pals_deadwaters` （无官方中译）

- **EN** So... have you seen the Deadwaters?
- **EN** Sounds beautiful. Like the glassy eye of a beast of the Distant Sea.
- **EN** Young guys would go out there on dares, make games of the danger. Usually they all came back.
  *导演注释：The characters are calling back and forth to each other from afar. The Eastern Reaches are out near the Deadwaters, which cannot be sailed into.*
- **EN** But it is the times they do not that make the reputation.
- **EN** I went along once, watched from afar. The water shines like polished onyx, dead flat for as far as you can see.
- **EN** It stops stones dead in their tracks. They cannot skip. All that energy, just eaten.
- **EN** It is frightening. I have never gone back.

<!-- ================= 15_boat_borraidus ================= -->

# 船灵 Borraidus（船名 Vedessud）

> "会自己操帆的船"。证据：演员表 "Jamieson Price — Borraidus, the spirit of Sailor’s ship"；sailor 台词 "Here is my boat, Vedessud"；pals_borrie "Ought I call you Borrie from here out?"。配音：Jamieson Price。

**统计**：台词块 325 · 英文词数 2161 · 涉及段落 70 · 官方中译覆盖 214/325

---


### `skipping_stones_start`

- **EN** Are you all right, fleshy one?
  **CN** 你还好吗，肉体凡躯？
- **EN** Fortunate, as I cannot move at all. We'll need you to fetch that map over there.
  **CN** 算你走运，我可是一动也动不了。得靠你去把那边那张地图叼回来。
  *导演注释：(beat)*
- **EN** Do it how you do it, just get that map!
  **CN** 你爱怎么弄怎么弄，把地图给我拿来就行！
  *导演注释："parcelier" is the equivalent of a mailman, in a situation where there aren't many paper-mail letters, but mostly what's sent is packages.*

### `skipping_stones_start_repeat`

- **EN** This is a complex chain of islands; your first step is getting that map!
  **CN** 这片岛链错综复杂，第一步就是拿到那张地图！

### `ssnew_pre_map`

- **EN** The problem is coming from a spot south of here.
  **CN** 问题出在南边的某个地方。
- **EN** On the map, you'll see the one area I could scry through the fog. Go there.
  **CN** 地图上会标出我透过迷雾唯一能窥测到的那片区域。去那里。
  *导演注释：From here, Sailor is further away; Boat and Sailor are communicating telepathically. But we probably act it as though they are just speaking normally next to each other in the same room, and may add some post-effects on Boat.*

### `ssnew_interdiction_first`

- **EN** *You* weren't!
  **CN** 被阻航的又不是你！
- **EN** Miracle we weren't dashed to pieces.
  **CN** 我们居然没被撞得粉碎，真是奇迹。

### `ssnew_traps`

- **EN** I've been thinking... Look out for traps.
  **CN** 我一直在想……当心有陷阱。
- **EN** Triplines, or spikejaws just beneath the sand? It's just ... very suspicious.
  **CN** 绊线，还是沙底下藏着的钉牙夹？ 这实在是……太可疑了。
- **EN** If the Guild had placed an interdict on these islands, we'd have been told.
  **CN** 要是公会在这几座岛上下的阻航阵，按理说会提前知会我们一声。
- **EN** Someone unauthorized.
  **CN** 某个未经许可的人。
  *导演注释：considering*

### `ssnew_what_do_i_do`

- **EN** You'll see a bunch of writing on the ground. Carefully-arranged symbols. You just need to mess them up.
  **CN** 你会看到地上写着一大片东西，都是精心排列的符号。 把它们弄乱就行。
- **EN** I can't quite scry *how* the interdict is written, but if it's symbols laid in the sand,
  **CN** 我无法窥测这道阻航阵具体是怎么写的， 但要是摆在沙地上的符号的话，
- **EN** a good scuff will do. You just need to get there.
  **CN** 上去使劲踩几脚就行。你只要能到那儿就好。

### `ssnew_no_traps`

- **EN** Here, shipmate.
  **CN** 我在呢，船友。
- **EN** Wellll... There are... features... of these islands...
  **CN** 呃……这些岛上……确实……有说法……
  *导演注释：From their telepathic link, she can feel his emotional state.*
- **EN** I'll brief you when the time comes. For now let's stay focused.
  **CN** 到时候再告诉你。现在先专心眼前的事。
- **EN** I'll give you two: Gold, and Conflux.
  **CN** 那就给你两个词：黄金、汇流。

### `ssnew_interdictions_secret`

- **EN** I can't believe you don't know!
  **CN** 你居然连这都不知道！
  *导演注释：Thinking about it*
- **EN** Then learn: an interdict is essentially just a sign, written on a wall or on the ground.
  **CN** 那你听好了：阻航阵其实不过是个写在墙上或地上的符号。
- **EN** If you can copy the writing, you can lay interdicts of your own...
  **CN** 只要你能把那些符号照抄下来，自己也能布阻航阵……
- **EN** so the Guild hides the writing. Locked in dark vaults, opened only on true emergency:
  **CN** 所以公会把阵图藏了起来，锁在不见天日的地窖里， 只有真正遇上紧急情况才会打开：
- **EN** theft of a critical Boat, or an insurrection needs quelling. You know ... business.
  **CN** 比如重要的船灵被盗，或是需要镇压叛乱。你懂的……正经事。
- **EN** That is the question I'd like you to go kick.
  **CN** 这就要劳你亲自去把答案挖出来了。

### `ssnew_interdictions_secret_2`

- **EN** Well, I dislike your counting. Five offenses!
  **CN** 我不喜欢你这么数落我。第五项罪状！
  *导演注释：In summary.*
- **EN** I'm a bonded carrier of the Guild. I can always tally.
  **CN** 我可是公会特许的运载船灵，随时都能清算。

### `ssnew_heading_west`

- **EN** Does it get you closer to where you want to go?
  **CN** 能让你离想去的地方更近吗？
- **EN** Then, maybe.
  **CN** 那就是也许吧。
- **EN** I'm pretty old. From a distance they look the same.
  **CN** 我只是活得久。隔远了看，跟智慧差不多。
  *导演注释：Sarcastically.*

### `ssnew_weather`

- **EN** Just an early warning...
  **CN** 先提醒你一声……
- **EN** I'm feeling a change in the winds, far to the south.
  **CN** 我感觉遥远的南方，风势正在变化。
- **EN** They're getting faster and rougher. Nothing I'd worry about yet,
  **CN** 风越来越急，越来越乱。暂时我们还不用担心，
- **EN** but we might face trouble later on.
  **CN** 但之后可能会碰上麻烦。
- **EN** Noted that you noted.
  **CN** 你记下了，我也记下了。

### `skipping_stones_gold_room_impending`

- **EN** Hello, skipper?
  **CN** 喂，船长？
- **EN** You're coming up on one of those things I haven't yet told you about.
  **CN** 你马上要碰到我还没告诉你的东西了。
- **EN** There's a strange structure embedded in the next island over. A big golden platform.
  **CN** 前面那座岛上嵌着一座奇怪的建筑，一个巨大的金色平台。
- **EN** It appears bright on my scry, beacon-like. Someone wants us to go there.
  **CN** 我窥测到它亮得像一座信标。有人想引我们过去。
- **EN** I wish you knew what a scry looked like *internally*, in the mind, so I could describe an important thing here:
  **CN** 真希望你也知道，窥测时脑海里会呈现怎样的景象， 这样我就能向你描述这里发生的一个重要现象：
- **EN** There are tendrils of energy that should connect to somewhere here. But they don't. They go off... as if toward another world.
  **CN** 这里本该有一些能量的触须，连接到这儿的某个地方。 可它们没有。它们延伸开去……仿佛伸向另一个世界。
- **EN** I've never seen its like before.
  **CN** 我还从没见过这样的东西。
- **EN** Maybe so. We'll have to get out of here first.
  **CN** 也许吧。我们得先离开这里再说。
- **EN** Just be sure to visit that ... shrine? ... when you get there.
  **CN** 到了那里，记得去那个……神龛？……看一眼。

### `skipping_stones_shrine`

- **EN** I felt it from here. A great pulse went out, toward some other place.
  **CN** 我在这里也感觉到了。一阵强烈的脉冲向外涌动，奔向某个地方。
- **EN** A message, perhaps. A short one. What it said? No idea.
  **CN** 也许是一条讯息，很短。至于说了什么？毫无头绪。
- **EN** I think you just keep going.
  **CN** 我想，接着往前走就是了。

### `ssnew_tacking`

- **EN** Don't worry; we're tacking!
  **CN** 别担心，我们在逆风航行！
- **EN** Tacking through Life!
  **CN** 我说的是人生航程！
- **EN** The Winds of Fate are attempting to blow us away from our goal.
  **CN** 命运之风正想把我们吹离目标。
- **EN** But, weaving to and fro, we'll get there.
  **CN** 但我们只要左穿右绕，总能到达。
  *导演注释：Todo: Rewrite: Winds of Adversity*
- **EN** I'll be there too, "in spirit"!
  **CN** 我也会以“灵魂”的形式陪你到达！
  *导演注释：The Boat is just sitting back stranded at the start; Sailor is doing all the work.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我又来烦你了。这次有两件事。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 第一，那边的绿岛就是你的目标。
- **EN** 
  **CN** + fog_clear 'whirlpool 0.1 那个圆圈就是阻航阵，就在那里！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那些符号画在石头上，石头就摆在草丛里。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 走到那儿，随便踢开一块石头。理论上很简单。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可是……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 河道不经过那座岛旁边。你得继续逆风航行，从另一侧绕过去。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这就说到“第二”件事了。再看看地图……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你会发现，这条河会径直流进你这辈子见过的 最大一张“嘴”里——是一个巨型漩涡。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我见多识广。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你要是被吞进去，我就再也回不了家了。请考虑一下我的处境。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你经常能想出来。

### `skipping_stones_whirlpool` （无官方中译）

- **EN** It's time for me to bother you again, about *two* things this time.
- **EN** First: that green island there is your target.
- **EN** + fog_clear 'whirlpool 0.1 That circle is the interdict, right there!
- **EN** Symbols drawn on rocks, placed in the grass.
- **EN** Get there, kick a rock aside. Simple in theory.
- **EN** Buuuuut....
- **EN** The river doesn't skirt that isle; you'll have to keep tacking and come from the other side.
- **EN** Which brings us to our *second* point. If you check your map...
- **EN** you'll find this river feeds directly into the biggest mouth you've ever seen. Gigantic whirlpool!
- **EN** I get around.
- **EN** If you are swallowed, there will be no way home for me. Think of me, please.
- **EN** You often do.

### `ssnew_good_place`

- **EN** Okay, okay.
  **CN** 行吧。

### `ssnew_good_place_2`

- **EN** Don't I know it!
  **CN** 可不是嘛！
  *导演注释：Just getting off the dangerous river*

### `ssnew_built_in_1`

- **EN** So *that's* the grinding sound I hear.
  **CN** 原来我听见的嘎吱声，是你在动脑筋啊。
- **EN** You're right. It doesn’t work that way. The boat stops itself.
  **CN** 你说得对。确实不是那么回事。是船自己停下来的。
- **EN** The ship, that I'm currently using as my body, refuses to obey me. It's built into the bones.
  **CN** 我眼下当身体使的这艘船，它不肯听我的。这是刻进龙骨里的。
  *导演注释：Starts to answer, realizes she doesn't know what to answer.*

### `ssnew_built_in_2`

- **EN** As you know, the way my runners pull us forward at such tremendous speeds --
  **CN** 你也知道，我的推进器能以惊人的速度拉着我们前进，是因为……
- **EN** -- carved with exquisite precision! --
  **CN** ……而且雕琢得极其精密！……
- **EN** Yes, but I don’t move the runners directly. My force of will, such as it is, is insufficient for that --
  **CN** 对，但我并不直接操控推进器。我的意志力还没强到那个地步……
- **EN** Compared to yourself, a thimble per a dram.
  **CN** 跟你比，我可就是小巫见大巫了。
- **EN** My will acts on control struts made of a special *different* stone
  **CN** 我的意志作用在控制框架上，那是用另一种特殊石头做的，
  *导演注释："a thimble per a dram" is a kind of common saying or comparison.*
- **EN** that is quite sensitive and pliant...
  **CN** 那种石头非常敏感，也很柔顺……
- **EN** Those struts have embedded scriers that constantly watch for interdiction marks;
  **CN** 这些框架里嵌着窥测器，会时刻监测是否有阻航标记；
  *导演注释："that one time" vs "that other time", as referenced later.*
- **EN** One glimpse and the control struts lock rigid.
  **CN** 只要监测到标记，控制框架就会立刻锁死。
  *导演注释：Todo: Rewrite: "have embedded scriers" is awkward.*
- **EN** Yes.
  **CN** 对。

### `ssnew_built_in_3`

- **EN** Indeed.
  **CN** 的确。
- **EN** If we die here, then it didn't matter, eh. As for the public, it's a boring rumor.
  **CN** 万一我们死在这儿，说了也无妨。至于外头的人嘛，这不过是条无聊的传言。
  *导演注释：Her eyes narrow as she considers whether this is an intentional falsehood spread by the Guild.*
- **EN** False tales of interdictions stopping an invasion fleet cold in the water -- now those are exciting!
  **CN** 那些关于阻航阵让入侵舰队在水面上戛然而止的虚假传说——那才叫精彩呢！

### `ssnew_please_disable_interdict`

- **EN** Finally!
  **CN** 总算到了！
- **EN** Have to set sail with the crew you have, as they once said.
  **CN** 有什么船员，就得带着什么船员出海。老话是这么说的。
- **EN** Now do the deed and let's get going... quickly.
  **CN** 现在快动手，我们好赶紧离开……越快越好。
- **EN** Storm incoming. It feels big.
  **CN** 风暴要来了。感觉来头不小。

### `skipping_stones_return_no_impeller`

- **EN** So, let me chart this out. You went right by the island with the interdict,
  **CN** 好，让我理一理。你刚才明明经过了那座布有阻航阵的岛，
- **EN** but you didn't disarm it? What kind of a joke is this?
  **CN** 却没把阵解除？你在开什么玩笑？
- **EN** Now that you've displaced all these stones and lilies,
  **CN** 你已经把这些石头和莲叶挪得七零八落，
- **EN** I don't think it's possible for you to get back there again. If only there were some way to change the past.
  **CN** 我看你是回不去那儿了。要是能改变过去就好了。
- **EN** Dear, I'm from the spirit world. I know some things.
  **CN** 亲爱的，我可是从灵界来的。有些事我略知一二。
- **EN** We'll see.
  **CN** 到时候你就知道了。

### `skipping_stones_return_no_impeller_repeat`

- **EN** Now what? Nothing.
  **CN** 还能怎么办？没办法。
- **EN** Nothing.
  **CN** 没办法。

### `skipping_stones_approaching_impeller`

- **EN** There it is just north of you. A miniature paradise, but tarry not.
  **CN** 它就在你正北边。是一处很小的乐园，但别久留。
- **EN** Besmirch those symbols, break the interdiction!
  **CN** 把那些符号弄花，破掉阻航阵！
  *导演注释：Rewrite: The island does not actually look that cool now.*

### `ssnew_interdict_destroyed_treasures_0`

- **EN** Get back before the storm wipes us out!
  **CN** 趁风暴还没把我们掀了，快回来！

### `ssnew_interdict_destroyed_treasures_n`

- **EN** Get back before the storm wipes us out!
  **CN** 趁风暴还没把我们掀了，快回来！
- **EN** I see nothing helpful. Maybe this is just about protecting treasure.
  **CN** 没看到有用的线索。也许他们只是单纯在保护什么宝藏。
- **EN** No. There's a residual effect. I can now sail away from these isles,
  **CN** 不，阻航阵的影响仍然存在。我现在能驶离这些岛屿，
- **EN** but were I to go further in, I'd get stuck again. We'd be hurricane food.
  **CN** 但要是再往里走，又会被困住，到时我们都得成飓风的口粮。

### `skipping_stones_treasures_1`

- **EN** A buried treasure! How did I not -- Ahh. Yes, it was a special insulating chest that occults from scry.
  **CN** 埋着的宝藏！我怎么没……啊，原来如此。 这是一只能屏蔽窥测的隔离宝箱。
- **EN** Heard of those before, never seen one. What's inside?
  **CN** 以前听说过，但从没见过。里面有什么？
- **EN** I've never heard of such a thing, outside fantasy stories.
  **CN** 这种东西，我只在奇幻故事里听说过。
- **EN** This disaster we're in has yielded a boon. Just like your tales of old.
  **CN** 身陷灾难，反倒得了份厚礼。倒像你那些古老传说里的情节。
  *导演注释："incredible find" is more about thinking, and understated wonderment, than outright emotional expression.*
- **EN** Now that I have this example to work from, ... yes. All occulted, so I can't see where,
  **CN** 现在有这个样本作参照，……就可以了。 它们全都屏蔽了窥测，所以我看不到位置，
- **EN** but when the boxes hide the space inside, it makes little bumps in the aether, which I can feel.
  **CN** 但箱子把内部空间藏起来时，会让以太微微隆起，我能感觉到。
- **EN** There are six treasures in total and now you have one.
  **CN** 一共六件宝藏，现在你已经拿到一件了。
- **EN** Maybe so. To get them all, you'd have to be quite shrewd.
  **CN** 也许吧。不过想全拿到手，你可得机灵点。
- **EN** Well. As you say.
  **CN** 好吧。你说是就是吧。

### `skipping_stones_treasures_2`

- **EN** Wood that stretches? For a boat, the stuff of nightmares.
  **CN** 会伸缩的木头？对一艘船来说，简直是噩梦。
- **EN** Let's not get too distracted. If we don't get out of here,
  **CN** 先别只顾着研究尺子。要是我们出不去，
- **EN** these treasures may be our only companions for quite a long time. I doubt they're entertaining to converse with.
  **CN** 我们就只得在很长一段时间里和这些宝物做伴儿了。 跟它们聊天，估计没什么意思。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 有你认识的地方吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 幸好你想到要找其他箱子。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 应该是同一套工具里的东西。也许再找到几件，就能看明白了。

### `skipping_stones_treasures_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Any places you recognize?
- **EN** I'm glad you thought to look for other chests.
- **EN** Pieces of the same set. Maybe we'll find more, to help this make sense.

### `skipping_stones_treasures_4`

- **EN** Guild engineers will be keen to pick apart that mystery, I expect.
  **CN** 我猜，公会的工程师一定很想拆开看看，弄清其中的奥秘。

  **CN** 前提是风暴没把宝藏喂给大海。

- **EN**

  **CN** 我们先离开这儿。 一路上你可以尽情欣赏你的新宝藏， 顺便庆幸我们还活着。

  **CN** 活着，嘿哟！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 很好。我们赶紧离开这里。
- **EN**

- **EN** 
  **CN** 就算立刻出发，也不一定能逃过它的怒火。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 前提是风暴没把宝藏喂给大海。
  *导演注释："And we get a bit of a rest" is*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你找到的那几件，都是从沙子里露出一点头的，对吧？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 有可能是一茬茬风暴 在把它们慢慢翻出来。这一场 兴许就能彻底翻出来。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不过眼下这都不重要。 等我们脱了身、稳稳航行时， 你再好好宝贝你的宝藏。

# 商人 The Trader（及克隆体）

> trader_clone_1/2 证实商人存在克隆体；trader_alt 为变体录音。

**统计**：台词块 329 · 英文词数 1584 · 涉及段落 59 · 官方中译覆盖 165/329

---


### `skipping_stones_treasures_4`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 就是那儿。第一面镜子。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 在儿时的我眼里，这些岛可大得多。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这次没有仪式，没有监考官。只有我和风。当然，还有这些镜子。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 成也好，败也罢，都不会有人知道。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 忙着呢。
  *导演注释："keeping busy" is a traditional greeting.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我一直在想，什么时候会有人来见我。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这算是我人生里一处没收拾干净的角落。我总忍不住想， “要是当初……”你懂吧？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 嗯，是这样。 我很快就要死了。 而我想弄明白。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 是一位神谕者告诉我的。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 很久以前，她预言我会成为最成功的商人。后来果然应验了。
  *导演注释：He is not so much questioning the belief as attempting to draw out more of an explanation.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 她说得很含糊。
  *导演注释：He's trying to figure out why this means the guy has come to the Mirror Isles. Maybe she said he would die in the Mirror Isles?*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 她的原话是：“今年之内，你将离开这个世界。 你须将诸事安排妥当。”
- **EN** 
  **CN** 什么？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那还能是什么意思？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这正是她们的本事。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 什么？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 哦。你知道我是谁？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那……恭喜你吧。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 然后呢？
  *导演注释：muttering to self*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 真希望当年我能坚持到底，完成最初那场试炼，成为一名镜匠。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我知道很难，但我们必须继续前进。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 倒是个主意。
  *导演注释：(the idea occurs to him), muttering*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 得集中精神。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 得集中精神。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们能挺过去的。
  *导演注释：muttering*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我站在这里，完整如一。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 身体又变沉了，还有点冷。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 可心底有一丝轻盈在颤动。一片澄明。
  *导演注释：to self*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 但你不会先跟我透个底……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 完全不是。是有人……说服了我。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我被一个陌生人说服了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 如今我平时不喝酒，可自己命不久矣的消息压得我喘不过气。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我每天都会经过一家酒馆，那天便想进去解解愁，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 尽管我已经许多年没踏进酒馆的大门。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我在那里遇见了一个肤色白皙的陌生人。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 他的言谈举止有些古怪，仿佛已走过许多地方，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 身上还留着各地的一点习惯。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 他穿着打扮像个平民，但身上每件衣服都挺括得出奇。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 陌生人问起我的经历。我一反常态，竟一股脑全说了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我的航运帝国，我是慢慢把它做大的， 起初只是一条不起眼的航线，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 而这条航线，是靠我那个简陋的 集市小摊赚来的利润才建起来的，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 而那个小摊，又是靠我年轻时 打零工、什么都干，攒下的 一枚枚硬币才开起来的，

  **CN** 我年轻时的生活，就是在遭到镜匠们拒绝后留下的废墟上， 一点点拼凑起来的……
- **EN**

- **EN** 
  **CN** 如今我这个大限将至的人，说出这些心底话后，竟觉得如释重负。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那个陌生人说，他拥有一份千锤百炼、远胜常人的判断力。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 他说，凭他敏锐的判断力， 他能清楚地看出 我的精神受了伤；
- **EN** 
  **CN** 说我有一部分已经碎裂，我的碎片就躺在镜之群岛的沙地上，闪着微光，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 还说除非我回去把它取回，否则我永远无法完整。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那个陌生人说，即便此事极难完成，而且耗资不菲，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我仍该回到镜之群岛，疗愈我的灵魂。
  *导演注释：TRANSLATOR note: The phrase "very difficult to eventuate and took lavish expenditure" recurs several times through the game, and should be translated the same way each time, so that the player can match them up. That said, this one is subjunctive whereas the others aren't; I have opted here to say "took" since "even if it took lavish expenditure" mostly works in English for not-totally-formal speech; "were to take" or "would take" are the alternatives and I suspect some other languages have to go more in that direction.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那是我若要作为一个完整而自重的人 离开这世间的必经之事。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我告诉他这想法毫无希望。 我在这儿有太多事要打理， 得确保在我走之前一切都安排妥当。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 他说我和那些生意伙伴的事看起来已经安排妥当了； 反正他们往后有的是时间慢慢处理。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 他说，真正该抓紧时间修补灵魂的是我，我剩下的时间已经不多了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 酒下肚，血液都暖了。 一个计划在我脑中成形。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我告诉他，这会相当麻烦，还得花上一大笔钱，但……并非办不到。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 晨曦的第一缕光透过窗户飘了进来；
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我多喝了两杯。谢过他后，我便告辞了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那一夜，我辗转反侧，满脑子都是那些镜子，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 还有我那碎裂的玻璃， 几十年的成功里，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我试着把它重新拼好、粘好、贴上， 却从未真正让它完整。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我知道，那个陌生人说得对。 有一块碎片不见了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 以一名并无公务在身之人的身份， 预订前往镜之群岛的船票， 自然是闻所未闻的。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 但我自己有一支船队。 调一艘商船改道，再容易不过了， 反正它的那笔买卖， 我也未必能活着看到了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我在脑中规划了一条迂回的航线， 好绕道前往群岛，避开耳目。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 等太阳升到正午， 我已经把计划付诸行动了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你这话什么意思？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 受邀？谁邀请的？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 为什么是我？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那么，我受邀……去哪里？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你好！能把那道光遮住吗？太晃人了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 就是那根橙色的……管子。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那要不你就……帮我一把？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 忙着呢？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你知道我们这是在哪儿吗？
  *导演注释：Doesn't understand "keeping busy".*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 虽说少见，但我好像碰上了一个无解的难题。我卡住了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 看起来挺有意思！
  *导演注释：Calling across the water. Sailor is riding on a big Lily pad that is looping around in a river, and cannot go anywhere. Trader is standing on firm ground.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我已经习惯了水面更……平静。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 是这些镜子能。
  *导演注释：Now Sailor is near Trader, so they are no longer calling a long way.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们为什么在这里？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我要去哪儿？我还以为 自己已经走得够远了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 能成为这个例外，我很荣幸。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我连他们的面都没见过，居然就要在他们学院里晋升了？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这似乎不太合规矩。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我怎么就成镜匠学徒了？我只会把镜子推来推去，至于怎么造，我可是一窍不通。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我会吗？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 忙着呢？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 如今时局诡异。 我们一直保持低调。
  *导演注释：(beat) He is not supposed to be here, but came because he is a "strong personality".*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 嗯。我来是想说明，我们对你的 到来并无不悦。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你可以按自己的方式进行试炼。 我会在这里帮你。
  *导演注释：(waiting for Trader to respond; Trader has nothing to say.)*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那么，你为什么来？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我确实懂。不过，你在未经许可的情况下来到这儿，这很不寻常。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你一定是费了不少周折。 到底有什么急事？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 当然，这是个敏感话题，不过…… 你说你快死了？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 那你信？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我自己跟神谕者 没打过直接交道， 但她们颇受敬重。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 她给过你什么细节吗？ 你会怎么死，又或者什么时候死？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我听说线索往往就藏在措辞里。她当时原话怎么说？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你确定她说的就是“死”？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你确定“离开这个世界”就是死的意思？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 如果她的意思就是你会“死”，她大可以直接那么说。 神谕者说话精准，又爱卖关子。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这正是她们的本事。
  *导演注释：thinking about it/realizing*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 嗯。我们终于到这儿了。 是不是挺尴尬？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你当年就是在这里慌了神，逃出了试炼。
  *导演注释：Knows what he's talking about, but doesn't want to admit he knows what he's talking about.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 其实当初我跟你是同一届。咱们都差不多。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我是紧随你之后 下一个接受试炼的人。
  *导演注释："same is same" here is not only saying "the same cohort", but "we two are much the same as each other".*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 当你失败、被他们带走的时候， 我从你脸上的痛苦扭曲里， 读出了些东西。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这让我隐约知道会遇到什么危险，也提前做好了心理准备。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 所以，是你助我成功的。
  *导演注释：The "specific danger" is some kind of psychological entity that attacks you when you use the mirrors. It's about assaulting the ego and trying to break you down. Alt here has seen that thing, and knows how vicious it is, but was more ready for it than Trader was.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 接下来这一段，我就不打扰你了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这项挑战有一部分需要两人配合完成。 我来承担这个角色。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 想都别想。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 所以，你听说自己快死了…… 而在一辈子那些没来得及了结的 心愿当中，
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你偏偏决定回到这儿来？ 都过去这么多年了？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你一直在想这些镜子， 对不对？
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我有个好消息。你不会死！至少没那么快。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 有一桩交易谈成了。交易双方是首席镜匠们和……另一人。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 有一面特殊的镜子，四周环绕着一圈多色之光……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 有一面特殊的镜子，四周环绕着一圈多色之光……
- **EN** 
  **CN** 首席镜匠们说，只有你能使用它，因为受邀者只有你。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这不是我能知道的事。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你是被对方选中的。再多的事，我便无权知晓。
  *导演注释：after a pause*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这就是好消息！你受邀……离开这个世界。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 首席镜匠们想让你过一下这组试炼。他们说，到了你要去的地方，这些思路会派上用场。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 试试吧。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 我们有很多组试炼，不只有世人皆知的第一组。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 初学者通常在十六岁左右接受这组试炼，以证明自己有资格晋升为镜匠学徒。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 没有破例。你已经晋升为镜匠学徒了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你见过我呀！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 凭本事晋升。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 你迟早会学会的。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这一切早已被预见。反正他们是这么跟我说的。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这事还不到该发生的时候。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 别得意忘形。就这样。
  *导演注释：muttering to self*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不不不，我们选对了。如今的成就即为明证。 那一天，恐惧即是智慧。
  *导演注释：muttering to self*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不不不。我们必须马上回去，处理本周商船进港的事。
  *导演注释：muttering to self*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 平时我们也可以这样合作。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不知道我能不能做到。
  *导演注释：muttering*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 不知道我还想不想挺过去。
  *导演注释：muttering*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 麻烦，真麻烦。妻子不待见我，孩子也大到不用操心了。
  *导演注释：muttering to self*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 再说这些镜子还冷冰冰的。
  *导演注释：muttering to self*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 干脆走了算了，去哪儿都行……四处漂泊……落个自在。
  *导演注释：muttering to self*

### `mirror_new_1_start` （无官方中译）

- **EN** There it is. The first mirror.

### `mirror_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** These isles seemed much bigger through the eyes of a boy.

### `mirror_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** No ceremony this time. No proctor. Just me and the wind. And the mirrors, of course.

### `mirror_4` （无官方中译）

- **EN** If I succeed, if I fail, none shall know.

### `mirror_watcher_first` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Keeping busy.
  *导演注释："keeping busy" is a traditional greeting.*
- **EN** I'd been wondering when I would be met.
- **EN** Keeping busy?
- **EN** These are strange times. We've kept quiet.
  *导演注释：(beat) He is not supposed to be here, but came because he is a "strong personality".*
- **EN** Well. I'm here to state that we are unoffended by your presence.
- **EN** You may run the trial as you see fit. I'm here to help.
  *导演注释：(waiting for Trader to respond; Trader has nothing to say.)*

### `mirror_oracle_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** This is an unswept corner of my life. I always wondered 'what if', you know?
- **EN** Well. You see. I'm going to die soon. And I want to know.
- **EN** So, why did you come?
- **EN** I *do* know. But, it's very unusual that you're here without sanction.
- **EN** You must have gone to some lengths. What's the urgency?

### `mirror_oracle_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I was told so by an Oracle.
- **EN** Years back, she predicted I'd become the most successful Tradesman. And it has come to pass.
  *导演注释：He is not so much questioning the belief as attempting to draw out more of an explanation.*
- **EN** Of course, it's a touchy subject, but... you say you're going to die?
- **EN** And you believe it?
- **EN** I've no direct experience with the Oracles myself, but they are well-regarded.

### `mirror_oracle_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** She was very vague.
  *导演注释：He's trying to figure out why this means the guy has come to the Mirror Isles. Maybe she said he would die in the Mirror Isles?*
- **EN** The words were: "You will depart this world before the year is out. You must order your affairs."
- **EN** Did she give you any details? How you will die, or when?
- **EN** I'm told there are clues in the words. What did she say, exactly?

### `mirror_oracle_4` （无官方中译）

- **EN** What?
- **EN** What else could she possibly have meant?
- **EN** Are you sure she meant 'die'?
- **EN** Are you sure "depart this world" meant die?

### `mirror_oracle_4b` （无官方中译）

- **EN** That's their whole thing.
- **EN** If she'd meant you would "die", she could have just said that. Oracles are precise, and tricky.
- **EN** That's their whole thing.
  *导演注释：thinking about it/realizing*

### `mirror_pre_clones` （无官方中译）

- **EN** What's that?
- **EN** Oh. You know who I am?
- **EN** Congratulations, I suppose.
- **EN** Well. We're finally here. Is it awkward?
- **EN** This is where you panicked and fled the trial.
  *导演注释：Knows what he's talking about, but doesn't want to admit he knows what he's talking about.*
- **EN** I was in your cohort, back then. Same is same.
- **EN** I was the next to run the trial, immediately after you.
  *导演注释："same is same" here is not only saying "the same cohort", but "we two are much the same as each other".*
- **EN** When you'd failed, as they were leading you away, I read the contortions on your face, and they told me something.
- **EN** They gave me a hint at the specific danger I would soon face. I steeled myself against that thing.
- **EN** So, you helped me succeed.
  *导演注释：The "specific danger" is some kind of psychological entity that attacks you when you use the mirrors. It's about assaulting the ego and trying to break you down. Alt here has seen that thing, and knows how vicious it is, but was more ready for it than Trader was.*

### `clones_early` （无官方中译）

- **EN** And?
  *导演注释：muttering to self*
- **EN** This isn't yet meant to happen.
- **EN** Don't develop too big a head. That's all.
  *导演注释：muttering to self*

### `clones_first_confusion` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Wish I'd held fast, completed the *original* trial. Become a Mirrorwright.
- **EN** No no no. We chose the right way. Our success attests to it. On that day, Fear was Wisdom.
  *导演注释：muttering to self*

### `clones_triple_confusion` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I know it's hard, but we must press forward.
- **EN** No no no. We must return at once to manage intake of the week's merchantman.
  *导演注释：muttering to self*
- **EN** Hassle, hassle. Wife doesn't care for me. Kid is sufficiently raised.
  *导演注释：muttering to self*
- **EN** And these mirrors are cold.
  *导演注释：muttering to self*
- **EN** Just leave to nowhere special -- become itinerant -- a life of freedom.
  *导演注释：muttering to self*

### `clones_working` （无官方中译）

- **EN** It's a thought.
  *导演注释：(the idea occurs to him), muttering*
- **EN** We could work together like this, normally.

### `clones_working_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** We need to focus.

### `clones_merge` （无官方中译）

- **EN** We need to focus.
- **EN** We will get through.
  *导演注释：muttering*
- **EN** Not sure I can do this.
  *导演注释：muttering*
- **EN** Not sure I want to get through.
  *导演注释：muttering*

### `mirror_after_clones_first` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I stand, whole.

### `mirror_icy_start` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I feel heavy again. A bit cold.
- **EN** But there's a fluttering of lightness inside. Clarity.
  *导演注释：to self*

### `mirror_pals` （无官方中译）

- **EN** But you won't clue me in as to --
- **EN** A portion of the challenge requires work in tandem. I will perform this role.
- **EN** Wouldn't think of it.

### `mirror_stranger_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Not at all. I was ... convinced.
- **EN** So, you heard you were going to die... and of all the unfinished business a man might wish to settle,
- **EN** you decided to come back here? After all these years?
- **EN** Been thinking about these mirrors a lot, have you?

### `mirror_stranger_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I was convinced, by a stranger.
- **EN** These days I don't normally drink, but the news that I would soon die weighed on me.
- **EN** I sought levity in a tavern I'd passed daily,
- **EN** but whose door I hadn't darkened in many years.

### `mirror_stranger_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** There I met a stranger, fair of complexion.
- **EN** His mannerisms were odd, as though he'd set foot on many shores,
- **EN** and come away carrying a bit of each.
- **EN** His manner of dress presented him as a commoner, but everything he wore was oddly crisp.

### `mirror_stranger_4` （无官方中译）

- **EN** The stranger asked my story. Atypically, it all came out.
- **EN** My shipping empire, which I'd grown slowly, starting from a single modest route,
- **EN** which I'd established by investing the profit from my humble market stall,
- **EN** which I started with the pennies I'd hoarded across my youth of doing odd jobs, anything at all,
- **EN** that young life I'd cobbled from the wreckage of rejection by the Mirrorwrights...
- **EN** a boy who had failed.
- **EN** Now a man nearing the end of his allotment, I felt relief as the confessions welled out.

### `mirror_stranger_6` （无官方中译）

- **EN** The stranger said that he possessed judgement refined at a level far exceeding the typical man.
- **EN** He said that this refined judgement permitted him to plainly see that I was wounded in my spirit;
- **EN** That some broken shard of me lay glinting in the sand of the Mirror Isles,
- **EN** and that I would never be whole til I went back to reclaim it.

### `mirror_stranger_double_mirrors` （无官方中译）

- **EN** The stranger said that even if it were very difficult to eventuate and took lavish expenditure,
- **EN** I should still return to the Mirror Isles and heal my soul.
  *导演注释：TRANSLATOR note: The phrase "very difficult to eventuate and took lavish expenditure" recurs several times through the game, and should be translated the same way each time, so that the player can match them up. That said, this one is subjunctive whereas the others aren't; I have opted here to say "took" since "even if it took lavish expenditure" mostly works in English for not-totally-formal speech; "were to take" or "would take" are the alternatives and I suspect some other languages have to go more in that direction.*
- **EN** That it was necessary if I were to depart this world a full and self-respecting man.
- **EN** I told him the idea was hopeless. I had too much to manage here, to ensure all would be set right before I'm gone.
- **EN** He said that things seemed right enough with my associates already, and anyway, they all had plenty more time to work with.
- **EN** He said that I was the one with few days remaining to repair my soul.
- **EN** The libations warmed my blood. A plan formed in my mind.
- **EN** I told him it would be fairly inconvenient, and a significant outlay, but ... doable.
- **EN** The early rays of dawn drifted through the window;
- **EN** I'd had two drinks too many. I thanked him and took leave.

### `mirror_stranger_before_hm_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I tossed and turned that night, thinking of the mirrors
- **EN** and my own shattered glass that, through decades of success,
- **EN** I'd tried to reassemble, glue, tape over, but had never truly made whole.
- **EN** I knew the stranger was right. A shard was missing.

### `mirror_stranger_before_hm_3` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Booking passage to the Mirror Isles, as a man with no official business, is, of course, unheard of.
- **EN** But I have a fleet of my own. Easy enough to divert a merchantman, whose trades I may not live to see, anyway.
- **EN** I charted in my mind the circuitous route we'd take to the Isles to avoid attention.
- **EN** By the time the sun had peaked, I'd set the plan in motion.

### `mirror_deal_1` （无官方中译）

- **EN** What are you saying?
- **EN** I've good news! You're not going to die. At least, not anytime *too* soon.

### `mirror_deal_2` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Invited? By whom?
- **EN** Why me?
- **EN** There's a special mirror set within a perimeter of polychromic light ...
- **EN** the Chiefs say only you can use it, as only you have been invited.
- **EN** That is not given me to know.
- **EN** You were chosen by the counterparty. More than that is not given me to know.
  *导演注释：after a pause*

### `mirror_deal_2_end` （无官方中译）

- **EN** So, invited ... to where?
- **EN** That's the *good* news! You've been invited to ... depart this world.

### `worlds_collide_MP_start` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Hello! Can you cover that light? It's distracting.
- **EN** That orange ... tube.
- **EN** Well, maybe you can just ... help me out, then.

### `worlds_collide_HM_start` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Keeping busy?
- **EN** Have you any idea where we are?
  *导演注释：Doesn't understand "keeping busy".*
- **EN** It's unusual, but I seem to have an unsolvable problem. I'm stuck!

### `worlds_collide_MS_start` （无官方中译）

- **EN** It looks enjoyable!
  *导演注释：Calling across the water. Sailor is riding on a big Lily pad that is looping around in a river, and cannot go anywhere. Trader is standing on firm ground.*
- **EN** I'm used to water being more ... placid.

### `worlds_collide_MS_first_teleport` （无官方中译）

- **EN** These mirrors can do that.
  *导演注释：Now Sailor is near Trader, so they are no longer calling a long way.*

### `mm_why` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Why are we here?
- **EN** Where I'm going? I've gone pretty far by now, I thought.
- **EN** The Chiefs want you to see this sequence. They said you'll need these ideas where you're going.
- **EN** Try it.

### `mm_course` （无官方中译）

- **EN** I'm honored to be the exception.
- **EN** We have many sequences, not just that first trial which is known to the public.

# 宝石匠人 Caver（?）

**统计**：台词块 190 · 英文词数 785 · 涉及段落 66 · 官方中译覆盖 96/190

---

# Jackays

> 女王的同伴（intro_queen_callout_alt："Jackays! Conroe! Are you here?"）。仅出现于开发用段落 audiolog_di_tiger_lily_v2，文本为空——疑似未实装或仅音效。

**统计**：台词块 18 · 英文词数 55 · 涉及段落 2 · 官方中译覆盖 9/18

---


### `audiolog_di_tiger_lily_v2`

- **EN** 
  **CN** 女王陛下！情况紧急！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 拿下那个叛徒！
  *导演注释：we hear an arrow streaking in, and a pop as the Queen disappears, and the arrow thuds into the tree right behind where she was kneeling*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 什么……
  *导演注释：turning to see the source of the shot; shouting to three men nearby*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 这是什么邪术……陛下！ 女王陛下，您在哪儿？难道他们……
  *导演注释：(faintly echoing; she is no longer here)*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 躲到后面去！
  *导演注释：she reappears, somewhat disorienting, no trace of being distraught as she was just a moment ago.*
- **EN** 
  **CN** 还不到十秒！您刚才……突然就不见了！
- **EN** 
  **CN** 能为您而战，是我的荣幸，陛下。跟紧我，没时间了。
- **EN** 
  **CN** 近卫军，集结！我们被包围了，全力迎敌！
- **EN** 
  **CN** ……谁……？
  *导演注释：(loudly, brashly, from nowhere)*

### `end3_suit` （无官方中译）

- **EN** My Queen! Urgently!
- **EN** Seize that traitor!
  *导演注释：we hear an arrow streaking in, and a pop as the Queen disappears, and the arrow thuds into the tree right behind where she was kneeling*
- **EN** Wha...
  *导演注释：turning to see the source of the shot; shouting to three men nearby*
- **EN** What dark enchantment... Queen, my Queen, where are you? Have they...
  *导演注释：(faintly echoing; she is no longer here)*
- **EN** Get behind!
  *导演注释：she reappears, somewhat disorienting, no trace of being distraught as she was just a moment ago.*
- **EN** Not 10 seconds! You were ... just gone!
- **EN** It is an honor to fight for you, my Queen; stick close, we've no more time.
- **EN** Inner Guard, rally! Hold nothing back, as we are surrounded!
- **EN** ...Who...?
  *导演注释：(loudly, brashly, from nowhere)*

# 彩蛋与演员表（jon / misc）

### `paradise_lost_invocation_credits` （无官方中译）

- **EN** Instruct me, for Thou know'st; Thou from the first Wast present, and with mighty wings outspread
- **EN** Dove-like satst brooding on the vast Abyss And mad'st it pregnant:
  *导演注释：TRANSLATOR NOTE: This is mostly the same text as in paradise_lost_invocation_2, but with a small amount added at the end. The translation should be the same.*
- **EN** What in me is dark Illumin, what is low raise and support;
- **EN** That to the highth of this great Argument I may assert Eternal Providence, And justifie the wayes of God to men.
- **EN** Unto ages of ages. Thy will be done. Amen.

His tone here is not intellectual, it's more about empathy and connection and figuring out how to reply to someone who told you their mom just died. "Rich children" is a mild joke that shows both that he was actively listening to her, as well as showing that the ice has been broken enough to*

  **CN** : audiolog_sky_play_of_form_1b
  *导演注释：The meaning of this phrase doesn't quite fully translate (this is telepathic communication in a way, as they speak different languages). He doesn't know what an alligator is, so he envisions some other moderately reptilian creature, but there is no rhyme, so there seems to be no point to the utterance. He's confused. The first 'what' is like he's trying to ask a concrete question, like "What do you mean," but he just cuts himself off and is completely confused, so the second "what" is just bafflement.*
